# Outfitter → Ka0s Outfitter Reborn: design (P2)

Status: P2 output of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-03-ka0s-conversion.md`. This document designs and does not build. P3 (scaffold) and P4 (port) execute it.

The document synthesizes six independently written design slices: skeleton, data, engine, triggers, ui and tests. Where they disagreed, one answer was picked and used everywhere. Each such pick is listed in §0.3 with its reason, and the text below uses only the winning name.

**Citation keys**

| Key | Source |
|---|---|
| **PLAN** | `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-03-ka0s-conversion.md` (the Decisions table) |
| **INV §n** | `specs/2026-11-03-outfitter-feature-inventory.md` |
| **BP §n** | `specs/2026-11-03-dpsrace-blueprint.md` |
| **CHK** / **H*n*** | `specs/2026-11-03-standard-checklist.md` and its hard calls H1–H30 |
| **Q*n*** | BP §21 open questions |
| `<section>-§n` | a section of the Ka0s WoW Addon Standard (`WowAddonStandards/standards/standards/`) |
| `File.lua:n` | the current Outfitter source at base `7ea3dc3` |
| DR `path` | DpsRace at `fc5160b` |
| LK `path` | LibKa0s at `v1.85.0` (`bea4ac8`, the tag's latest re-cut, re-synced in 19e2496; C4 vendored the first cut, `fd57b83`, and the C7 fix the `e134d5b` re-cut); test kit at rev 50 |

---

## 0. Decisions and rulings

### 0.1 Owner rulings (binding)

| # | Ruling | Source | Where it lands |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | The Title is **Ka0s Outfitter Reborn**. The folder, the TOC basename, `addonName` and the AceAddon name are **`OutfitterReborn`**. The git checkout directory stays `Outfitter`, and CurseForge project 1698220 is kept. | PLAN Identity (2026-11-03 ruling) | §1, §1.6 |
| R2 | Version stays **1.1.0** despite the break, with a versioning-git deviation row re-checked at the next MAJOR. | PLAN Version (H3) | §10 row D1 |
| R3 | **Native triggers (H1).** Every preset becomes a native trigger descriptor (events, condition fn, typed params). The Custom script editor is dropped, and there is no `loadstring` anywhere. | PLAN Scripts | §6 |
| R4 | A fresh AceDB store with no migration from `gOutfitter_*`. | PLAN Saved data | §3 |
| R5 | The window stays docked to the character sheet and LibKa0s-skinned, and the slot checkboxes are kept. | PLAN Main window | §7 |
| R6 | `Deprecated.lua` and the retired features (INV §18) are dropped. | PLAN Deprecated.lua | §1.3, §6.8 |
| R7 | We are free to rewrite all the code, but the functionality must survive. | PLAN Code shape | the whole document |
| R8 | Credits to John Stephen (mundocani) and the later maintainers are kept. The MIT LICENSE keeps the original copyright. | PLAN Credits | §1.7, §4.2, §9 `test_credits` |
| R9 | H2, H4–H30 and Q1–Q5 take the research recommendation or default. | PLAN Other hard calls | §0.2 |
| R10 | Every authored Lua file stays well under 1000 lines (the hard cap is 1500). No function has CCN > 15. luacheck reports 0, and the headless suite is green. | task constraint | §1, §9, §13 |

### 0.2 Hard calls and open questions as adopted

| Call | Adopted value (short) | Deviation? |
|---|---|---|
| H1 | Native triggers, no `loadstring`, no Custom (R3) | no |
| H2 | Title `Ka0s Outfitter Reborn`, folder `OutfitterReborn` (PascalCase of the Title minus `Ka0s `, so layout-§2 is satisfied) | no |
| H3 | `_G.OutfitterReborn.API.v1`, with no `Outfitter_*`/`Outfitter:` shim. INV §10.3's shim rule is superseded. | no |
| H4 | Docked window, host-matched strata/level, not toplevel, in `UISpecialFrames` (see §0.3 C13) | yes: D2 standalone-windows |
| H5 | Chat tag `[OR]`. `/of` is primary and `/outfitter` is the alias. The verb set is in §4.3. `/of auto on\|off` replaces the old `enable/disable`. | no |
| H6 | Static LDB launcher. No quick-switch minimap menu, and the DBIcon fork is dropped. | no |
| H7 | Reset all = the active profile only. `db.char` (outfits) survives. Delete-all is a separate confirmed act. | no |
| H8 | Six locales re-keyed with early-return gates. The font fallback must be checked in the client. | conditional D7 (O-item, §10) |
| H9 | Zones match on uiMapID/instance mapID. Subzones match on `GetMinimapZoneText()` under a deviation. | yes: D3 |
| H10 | `UnitFactionGroup` **first** return, with a comment. The standard defect goes upstream in P9. | no |
| H11 | 14 CLICK bindings on permanent hidden buttons, Show window first. Names are re-derived for `OutfitterReborn` (§0.3 C4). | no |
| H12 | Per-outfit editing lives in the window and its dialogs, so options-ui-§14 is N/A. | no |
| H13 | Typed trigger params written through the Schema seam with an instance id (§3.5). LibKa0s-Schema v3 supports this (`Set(path, value, instanceId)`, host `resolveRoot(parts, instanceId)`: LK `docs/api/Schema/version-3-docs.md:127,179`). | no |
| H14 | One AceEvent/AceTimer target per bound outfit, plus one Catalog-backed pseudo-event table | no |
| H15 | Delete the MC2*, LibBabble*, LibTipHooker, LibDropdown, UTF8, LibButtonFacade and DBIcon fork. Item stats move to `C_Item.GetItemStats`, tooltip lines to `TooltipDataProcessor`, and links to LibKa0s-Item. **Optimize survives** (both the simple and genetic paths). | no |
| H16 | Pawn and WeightsWatcher are kept as optional stat sources (OptionalDeps, `IsAddOnLoaded`, an `ADDON_LOADED` re-check, built-in fallback) | no |
| H17 | Test mode fills the **bar** with placeholder outfits and a "Test mode" strip. The window shows real data. | no |
| H18 | No simulator. Recorded in ARCHITECTURE and scope.md. | no (MAY) |
| H19 | Every file ≤ 700 planned lines, so the 1000–1500 band is empty. The four sanctioned CCN shapes. Census: "Nothing is over the cap today". | no |
| H20 | Drop `X-ReloadTag`, `AddonCompartmentFunc`, `RequiredDeps` and `SavedVariablesPerCharacter` | no |
| H21 | Options are not split into LoadOnDemand. The design has **3** subcategories (General, Outfit Bar, Profiles), below options-ui-§4's ≥5 trigger. The recommended comment stays in `settings/OptionsSetup.lua`. This is compliance, so it gets no register row (documentation-§3 :337). | no |
| H22 | README screenshots wait for the first client capture | yes: D6 |
| H23 | Icons are declared by name (§1.4) | no |
| H24 | The first LibKa0s vendoring is written up as a re-vendor bundle `docs/revendor/2026-11-03-v1.85.0/` | no |
| H25 | `SecureCmdOptionParse` routing wrapper (§4.3.1) | no |
| H26 | One Lifecycle latch, owned driver frames, latch-gated hook bodies, and an update counter reset on Suspend/Resume (§5.12) | no |
| H27 | `.gitattributes` and renormalization is the first commit (§11 C1) | no |
| H28 | `CHANGES.txt` deleted, `UsersManual.md` → `docs/users-manual.md`, `media/` lower-case | no |
| H29 | Roster row in WowAddonStandards on the same-named branch (P9) | no |
| H30 | `AceDB:New("OutfitterRebornDB", defaults, true)`. Outfits live in `char`. | no |
| Q1 | No `AddonCompartmentFunc` | — |
| Q2 | Superseded by H4 plus the combat-hide rule. The window is in `UISpecialFrames` (§0.3 C13). | in D2 |
| Q3 | Outfits are `char`-scoped | — |
| Q4/Q5 | No sim, so there is no launcher sim row | — |

### 0.3 Cross-slice contradictions resolved

| # | Contradiction | Slices | Resolution | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | SV names: `OutfitterRebornDB`/`OutfitterRebornPerfDB` versus `OutfitterDB`/`OutfitterPerfDB` | skeleton, data vs tests, H30 text | **`OutfitterRebornDB`, `OutfitterRebornPerfDB`** | toc-file-§2 and savedvariables-§1 say `<Addon>DB`, and DR builds the name as `addonName.."DB"` (`core/Database.lua:272`). Keeping `OutfitterDB` would need a deviation row. Open issue O1 asks the owner to confirm. |
| C2 | Bus wire prefix: `Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_` versus `Ka0s_Outfitter_` | skeleton, triggers vs engine, tests | **`Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_<Pascal>`**, regex `^Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_%u%a+$` | It derives from the AceAddon name |
| C3 | Test global `_G.OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST` versus `_G.OUTFITTER_TEST` | skeleton vs tests | **`_G.OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST`** | Same DR pattern (`DPSRACE_TEST`) |
| C4 | Binding names `OutfitterReborn*`/`BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTERREBORN` versus `OutfitterToggleWindow`/`BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTER` | skeleton, data, ui vs tests, H11 text | **`OutfitterReborn*`** | Avoids colliding with a legacy `Outfitter` install that is still present |
| C5 | Logo file names `outfitterreborn.logo.*` versus `outfitter.logo.*` | skeleton vs data, tests | **`media/logos/outfitterreborn.logo{,.128}.tga`** | layout-§4: `<addon>` is the folder name, lower-cased |
| C6 | Settings pages: 5 pages (General, Automation, Outfit Bar, Display, Profiles) versus 3 pages with tabs | skeleton, tests vs data | **3 pages**: General (tabs Master controls · Behavior · Tooltips · Skin · Keybindings), Outfit Bar, Profiles | The data slice designed every row. With fewer than 5 pages, H21 needs no real deviation. |
| C7 | Schema paths `equip.sounds`, `automation.enabled`, `bar.show/scale/alpha/locked`, `tooltips.*` | engine, triggers, ui | **data-slice paths**: `equip.muteSounds`, `auto.enabled`, `bar.shown`, Master `scale`/`alpha`/`locked`, `bar.showBackground`, `tooltip.*`. The UI's new `quickSlots.enabled` is kept. | One owner for the schema |
| C8 | Outfit messages: one `OUTFITS_CHANGED` versus `OUTFIT_ADDED/DELETED/EDITED/RENAMED`; `OUTFIT_UNWORN` versus `OUTFIT_REMOVED` | data vs engine, ui | **`OUTFIT_ADDED/DELETED/EDITED/RENAMED`, `OUTFIT_WORN`, `OUTFIT_REMOVED`** | Receivers need the kind of change. `NS.Outfits` remains the only sender of the four outfit-record messages. |
| C9 | The `char` store shape (`nextID` vs `nextId`, `stack{entries,layers}` vs an entry array, `emPreserved` vs `preservedEM`, `bindings` map vs `bindingIndex` on the record, `shapeshiftIndex` persisted) | data, engine, triggers | `nextID`; `stack` = an array of `{id\|temp, layer}` (engine, which fixes B6 by construction); `emPreserved`; **`char.bindings[n] = id`** (unique by construction); `shapeshiftIndex` is **runtime** | §3.2 |
| C10 | The per-outfit trigger record (`trigger={id,params,state}` vs `trigger={id,disabled,combatDisabled}` + `triggerParams` + `triggerState`) | data vs triggers | **The triggers shape.** Params are kept per trigger id, so switching triggers and back keeps the values, and runtime state sits apart so the dialog can't wipe it. | §3.3 |
| C11 | Instance schema paths `outfits.<id>.trigger.<key>` versus `outfit.*`/`trigger.<TID>.<key>` with `instanceId` | ui, tests, H13 text vs triggers | **`outfit.<field>` and `trigger.<TID>.<key>`, with `instanceId = outfitId`**, resolved by the host `resolveRoot` | This is the shape LibKa0s-Schema v3 supports. The data slice's claim that "v3 resolves only global/profile" is wrong (LK Schema v3 doc :127). |
| C12 | Outfit-store writers: everything through `NS.Outfits` versus trigger params through Schema | data vs triggers, ui | Instance rows write through `NS.SetByPath(path, v, id)`, which forwards to the separate `NS.OutfitSchema` instance (§3.5). Its `announce` calls `NS.Outfits.Changed(id, "trigger")`, which sends `OUTFIT_EDITED`, and sends no `SETTINGS_CHANGED`. Every other record write goes through the `NS.Outfits` API. | One message sender, and one typed write seam for params |
| C13 | Escape: Q2 default (CharacterFrame stack only) versus `UISpecialFrames` | tests vs ui | **`UISpecialFrames`** | The window is hidden at `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`, so `CloseSpecialWindows` can never hit a protected Hide (§7.2). standalone-windows :8 is a MUST. |
| C14 | Engine module names: `Equip_Plan/Equip_Run/Stack/Inventory_Link…` versus `Equip/Equip_Changes/Equip_Moves/OutfitStack…` | skeleton, tests vs engine | **The engine slice's file set** (§5), with NS tables `NS.Stack`, `NS.Equip`, `NS.Inventory`, `NS.Outfits`, `NS.EM`, `NS.PlayerState`, `NS.ItemInfo`, `NS.Changes`, `NS.Moves`, `NS.Bank`, `NS.Stats`, `NS.Optimize`, `NS.Companion` | The most detailed slice |
| C15 | Trigger module names: `Triggers_State/Class/Activity/World` versus `Triggers_General/Trade/Pvp/Class/Quest` versus `Triggers_Params/Families/Conditions/Effects/Binder` + `States*` | skeleton, tests vs triggers | **The triggers slice's set**, with `Triggers_Conditions` split by category (`Triggers_CondGeneral`, `_CondTrade`, `_CondQuest`) so P4 can parallelize | §6.1 |
| C16 | Bracket API: `NS.Equip.Run(fn, immediate)`, `SetUpdateDelay`, `RequestUpdate` versus `Batch`/`BatchNow`/`Request`/`DelayUntil` | triggers vs engine | **`NS.Equip.Batch(fn, ...)`, `BatchNow`, `Request()`, `DelayUntil(t)`**, plus a thin `SetUpdateDelay(start, delay)` that keeps the old `start + delay − 1.5` rule (`Outfitter.lua:7536-7542`) | §5.10 |
| C17 | `Stack.Wear(id, layer, byTrigger)` versus `St.Wear(id, opts)`; `NS.Engine.WearOutfit(outfit, …)` | triggers, ui vs engine | **`NS.Stack.Wear(id, opts)`** with `opts = { layer, byTrigger, now, hw, clearDisabled }`. `NS.Engine` does not exist. | |
| C18 | Combat flags: `NS.State.inCombat`/`maybeInCombat` versus `NS.PlayerState` | triggers vs engine | **`NS.PlayerState.InCombat()/MaybeInCombat()/SetMaybeInCombat()/SetInterruptCasting()`**. `NS.State` holds session UI flags only. | One owner for the combat events |
| C19 | Trigger ids: legacy ids kept versus new SCREAMING_SNAKE ids | tests vs triggers | **New ids**, with a parity table from every legacy id (§6.8). Seeds use `DRUID_CASTER`…, `SPEC_n`. | A fresh store holds no legacy ids |
| C20 | Param types: `boolean/number/string/stringlist/zonelist` versus `bool/number/string/spelllist/itemlist/maplist/subzonelist/unitlist/pet` | ui, data vs triggers | **The triggers set**. The UI field registry covers all nine. | Matching on IDs (localization-§4) |
| C21 | Locale layout: one enUS + PostLoad, three enUS topic files, or per-trigger-family files | data vs skeleton vs tests | **`enUS.lua`, `enUS_Triggers.lua`, `enUS_Window.lua`** plus six overlays. No PostLoad. Parallel P4 tasks hand back their new keys, and the orchestrator appends them at the wave merge (the same way as `SUITES` flips). | Keeps every file under 500 lines with no shared-file edits in a wave |
| C22 | Slash split `Slash.lua` + `Slash_Outfits.lua` versus `Slash.lua` + `modules/Commands.lua` | skeleton vs data | **`settings/Slash.lua` + `modules/Commands.lua`** | Handlers are engine-adjacent, and the dispatcher stays in settings |
| C23 | Zones: five tables + `defaults/BuiltinEvents.lua` + `modules/Zones.lua` versus one source table | skeleton vs triggers | **One table** `defaults/Zones.lua` with derivations in `modules/States_Zone.lua`. `BuiltinEvents.lua` is not created. | Makes the lockstep structural |
| C24 | Legacy characterization: pins in the legacy harness (C2) versus fixtures captured from a worktree | skeleton vs tests | **Captured fixtures** `tests/fixtures/legacy/*.lua` by `tools/legacy_capture.lua`, run in P3 C2 against a worktree of `7ea3dc3`. `test_refactor_pins` is reserved for the P5 CCN splits. | The new suites read the fixtures, with no ordering coupling to the deletion |
| C25 | Mocks: `mock_inventory.lua` versus `mock_gear.lua` | skeleton vs tests | **`mock_gear.lua`** + `mock_world.lua` | |
| C26 | Textures: keep `outfitter-button.blp`/`checkbox-unknown.blp` versus the logo plus catalog `help` | skeleton vs ui | **Both deleted.** The sheet button uses `C.LOGO_128`. The tri-state overlay uses `NS.Icon("help")`. `media/textures/` is not created. | One less restyle question |
| C27 | Trigger mark icon `script` versus `ph:lightning`; icon list | ui vs skeleton | **`ph:lightning`**, plus the UI's `ellipses` and `help` (checked present: LK `LibKa0s/media/icons/{ellipses,help}.tga`, `phosphor/lightning.tga`) | §1.4 |
| C28 | Test-mode ids `sample:*` versus `preview:<n>` | engine vs ui | **`preview:<n>`**. `NS.Outfits.Get` answers nil for any id not matching `^o%d+$`. | |
| C29 | Current-outfit label: `St.CurrentInfo` / `CurrentLabel` / `OutfitTooltip.CurrentLine` | engine, data, ui | `NS.Stack.CurrentInfo() → name, id` (`""` for empty, `L["Custom"]` for a temp top). `NS.Stack.CurrentLabel()` maps `""` to `L["None"]`. The launcher host line is `L["Current outfit: %s"]:format(CurrentLabel())`. | |
| C30 | Default trigger icons as table `NS.DefaultTriggerIcons` versus a descriptor field | ui vs skeleton | **The descriptor `icon` field** (ported from `OutfitterBar.lua:9-53`, `Solo` → `INSTANCE_DIFFICULTY`) | One table fewer to keep in step |
| C31 | `didEquip`/`didUnequip`: persisted versus runtime | triggers vs data, engine | **Runtime** in `NS.Stack` (`rt[id]`). Open issue O9 covers smart unequip after `/reload`. | INV §2.5 marks them runtime |
| C32 | Seeding: `LoadPass` at first PEW versus `Seed.FirstRun` 1 s after OnEnable | data vs engine | `NS.Outfits.LoadPass()` runs from a 1 s AceTimer after the **first** `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`, and from `DeleteAll`. Seeding is inside it, guarded by `char.seeded`. | Needs gear, class and spec (INV §6.1) |
| C33 | Settings opener `NS.OpenSettings(page)` versus `NS.OpenOptionsPanel()` | ui vs data | **`NS.OpenOptionsPanel(pageId?)`** | DR name |
| C34 | Popup keys `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_DELETE_ALL` versus `OUTFITTERREBORN_DELETE_ALL`; `OUTFITTER_SERVER_FULL` | ui vs data, engine | `OUTFITTERREBORN_DELETE_ALL`, `OUTFITTERREBORN_RESET_ALL`, `OUTFITTERREBORN_EM_FULL`, `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_DELETE`, `…_CONFIRM_REBUILD`, `…_CONFIRM_SET_CURRENT` | |
| C35 | `/of show\|hide\|toggle` versus `/of show` | ui vs data | **`show`** opens and **`hide`** closes the window (standalone-windows :52). The window-`toggle` verb is declined, because `toggle` is the legacy outfit verb that macros use; the reason is a comment at `NS.COMMANDS` and a line in `docs/slash-dispatch.md`. The key binding and the sheet button toggle. | A SHOULD declined needs its reason stated (STANDARDS severity) |
| C36 | Legacy-coexistence guard: warn only, or warn and stand automation down | data vs skeleton | Warn once at `PLAYER_LOGIN`; **no hold** (owner ruling O2, 2026-11-03). | The owner accepts that both engines may change gear until the old addon is disabled. |
| C37 | The `Equip` SFX row default `equip.sounds = false` versus `equip.muteSounds = true` | engine vs data | `equip.muteSounds = true` (the same behaviour, positive phrasing) | |
| C38 | The engine's module-local `NS.Items` versus LibKa0s-Item seam naming | engine vs skeleton | Data file `defaults/Items.lua` publishes **`NS.ItemData`**. The LibKa0s-Item lease is `NS.Item` (`core/ItemSetup.lua`). | Avoids a near-name clash |
| C39 | Compat size: one `core/Compat.lua` (250) versus ~45 engine accessors | skeleton vs engine | **One file, `core/Compat.lua` (~680)**: secret detectors, version variants, unit/CVar/addon reads, and the item, container, EM, pet, bank, tooltip, title and macro-icon accessors. P3 C6 writes the first part and P4.2 adds the accessors. | compat.md :11: a split file MAY be opened only when `cat core/Compat*.lua \| wc -l` would pass 1000, and a split by size alone is a violation. 680 is under that line, and an underscore name (`Compat_Items`) is outside the family pattern (compat.md :7). |
| C40 | Window hidden for the whole of combat (UI) versus "stays visible" (today) | ui | Adopted (UI rule 1.2), and flagged as behaviour change O4 | Secure rows make in-combat Show/Hide/SetPoint blocked actions |

## 1. Target tree and TOC

### 1.1 File tree

Line budgets are planned ceilings. No authored file is planned above 700 lines. Files with `*` are created by P3 and the rest by P4. Unless marked, every file opens with `local addonName, NS = ...` and a `-- path — purpose` line 2 (BP §4).

```
Outfitter/                              git checkout dir; the shipped folder is OutfitterReborn
  OutfitterReborn.toc            ~200 *  metadata + load order with LOAD-BEARING notes (toc-file-§1/§5)
  Bindings.xml                    ~35 *  14 CLICK bindings, Show window first (launcher-§6, H11); not in the TOC
  CLAUDE.md                       ~45 *  Ka0s stub + provenance + icons lines (documentation-§2, H23/H24)
  DEPENDENCIES.md                 ~90 *  runtime/dev toolchain + junction note (documentation-§7, BP §17.2)
  README.md                            *  minimal US-English fix in P3; Ka0s restyle in P10; credits kept
  LICENSE                          22 *  MIT: "Copyright (c) 2018 John Stephen" + "Copyright (c) 2026 Ka0s"
  .gitattributes                       *  DpsRace body verbatim (H27)
  .gitignore                           *  /Outfitter/, /OutfitterReborn/, /.idea/, *.zip, .playwright-mcp/
  .luacheckrc                    ~220 *  §1.5
  .pkgmeta                        ~25 *  §1.6
  core/
    Namespace.lua                  25 *  NS.PREFIX "|cff00ffff[OR]|r", NS.name, NS.BRAND "Ka0s Outfitter Reborn",
                                         NS.SLASH "/of", NS.SLASH_LONG "/outfitter", NS.FALLBACK_VERSION "1.1.0" (BP §6.1)
    MediaSetup.lua                 25 *  LibKa0s-Media lease: NS.Icon, NS.MediaFont, RegisterLSM (BP §6.2)
    Constants.lua                 110 *  FONT_MONO, LOGO/LOGO_128 (derived), LAYERS (§7.1), OUTFIT_MESSAGE_COLOR (INV §14) (BP §6.3)
    State.lua                      15 *  NS.State = { debug, testMode, legacyLoaded } (session only)
    EnvSetup.lua                   25 *  NS.Version, NS.Meta (BP §6.5)
    CoreSetup.lua                 130 *  NS.Util.print, NS.SafeRegisterEvent/UnitEvent, NS.rejectedEvents, NS.SafeToString,
                                         ApplySkin, MakeCloseButton, RaiseStaticPopups, LIBKA0S_MISSING (BP §6.6)
    SkinSetup.lua                 700 *  Skin seam, RegisterOwner at file scope, of.* tokens (§7.1) (BP §6.7; DR is 694)
    Compat.lua                    680 *  NS.Compat, the whole compat family (compat.md :7, :11): secret detectors, version
                                         variants, unit/CVar/addon/spec reads (C6); item/container/EM/pet/bank/tooltip/
                                         title/macro-icon accessors (P4.2, §5.13). Reaches NS.Secrets at CALL time only.
    Secrets.lua                   170 *  NS.Secrets (§2.2) + the NS.IsSecret/Unsecret/SafeNumber aliases (BP §6.9)
    Bus.lua                       160 *  NS.MSG catalog (§2.3), NS.NewBusTarget, NS.SendBus, BusStandDown/Up (BP §6.11)
    PoolSetup.lua                  30 *  NS.Pool (BP §6.12)
    ItemSetup.lua                  45 *  NS.Item: LibKa0s-Item v3 lease (ItemIDFromLink, BonusIDsFromLink) (H15)
    WidgetsSetup.lua              150 *  MakeDropdown, OpenMenu, CloseMenu, MakeTestStrip, TEST_STRIP_HEIGHT, ShowCopy (BP §6.13)
    SecureQueue.lua                70 *  NS.SecureQueue.Run(key, fn) / Flush() (events-frames-taint-§2)
    LifecycleSetup.lua            160 *  latch, holds, MODULES (§5.12) (BP §6.16, H26)
    PerfSetup.lua                  55 *  NS.Perf over OutfitterRebornPerfDB (BP §6.17)
    DebugLogSetup.lua             110 *  console + diagnostics; tags Equip, Stack, Trigger, Zone, Dock, Bar, EM, Opt (BP §6.18)
    LauncherSetup.lua             220 *  Launcher + 14 binding buttons + BINDING_* + NS.WearBoundOutfit (§4.4, §4.5)
    OutfitterReborn.lua           260 *  NewAddon(NS, addonName, …), NS.Print reclaim, host handlers, legacy guard (BP §6.21)
    Database.lua                  330 *  AceDB, profile callbacks, migration runner (profile + char walks), resolveRoot (§3)
                                         (DR's is 296 and does less)
  defaults/
    Profile.lua                   140 *  NS.defaults (§3.1)
    Items.lua                     220    NS.ItemData: slots, invtypes, alternates, aliases, ignored, phantoms, corrections (§5.1)
    Zones.lua                     160    NS.Zones.rows + BRAWL_MAP_IDS: the single zone source (§6.5)
    TriggerData.lua               170    aura/form/NPC/difficulty/map/skillLine/pet ID tables (§6.2)
    Triggers.lua                  500    NS.TriggerCatalog: one data row per non-zone trigger + CATEGORY_ORDER (§6.3)
    PreviewOutfits.lua             40    test-mode placeholder rows (§8)
  modules/                              (TOC order = this order)
    PlayerState.lua               180    combat/dead/cast/spec flags (§5.2)
    ItemInfo.lua                  420    link parse, identity, comparison (§5.3)
    Inventory.lua                 460    scan, cache, bank-open semantics (§5.4)
    Inventory_Lists.lua           320    missing/unused/BoE/warbound/using/cooldown/sections (§5.5)
    Outfits.lua                   500    outfit store and the sole writer of db.char.outfits/order/bindings (§5.6)
    Outfits_Seed.lua              170    LoadPass seeding + repair (§5.7)
```

**Not created:**
- `core/ToastSetup.lua`: hotkey messages stay on `UIErrorsFrame` (INV §5).
- `core/ChartsSetup.lua`, `core/SimSetup.lua` and `modules/Sim.lua`: H18.
- `defaults/BuiltinEvents.lua`: C23.
- `media/textures/`: C26.
- Any `.xml` other than `Bindings.xml`: every frame is built in Lua.

Glow, Toast, Charts and Sim are therefore unleased (library-stack-§3).

**Totals (planned):** 89 authored addon Lua files with a budget in this tree plus the 6 locale overlays (95 in all). The listed budgets sum to about 24 000 lines, and the overlays add at most 2 700 more, so the planned ceiling is about 24 000–26 700 addon lines. These are ceilings, not estimates. The largest are `core/SkinSetup.lua` (700), `core/Compat.lua` (680), `Window_Rows.lua` (520) and `Bar.lua` (520). That leaves headroom on every file below the 1000 notice line.

### 1.4 Icons declaration (H23)

These two lines go in CLAUDE.md, directly under the provenance line:

```
Bundles [LibKa0s](https://github.com/tusharsaxena/LibKa0s) v1.85.0 (MIT).
LibKa0s icons: add, cancel, chevron-down, chevron-right, chevron-up, clear, close, confirm, copy, ellipses, help, info, move, ph:lightning, reset, settings, volume-high.
```

| Icon | Use |
|---|---|
| chevron-down, chevron-up, close, confirm, info, move, reset, volume-high | **Mandatory**: the kit's `VendorSync.LIBRARY_MARKS` (LK `testkit/vendor_sync.lua:358`), drawn by the library for its host. `chevron-up`/`chevron-down` are every `S.ScrollBar`'s step arrows (the window list §7.1, the icon chooser §7.8, the list editors §7.6); `volume-high` is the sound picker's play button (LK `docs/releasing.md:361-366`). `test_vendor_sync` is red without any of them. |
| copy, clear | DebugLog console and copy window |
| cancel | Dialogs |
| add | New-outfit button |
| chevron-right / chevron-down | Category expand |
| settings | Window title strip |
| reset | Bar reset |
| ellipses | Row menu button |
| help | Slot-checkbox unknown overlay; drag-handle help |
| move | Outfit bar drag handle (replaces TopDragHandle/LeftDragHandle) |
| ph:lightning | "Has a trigger" mark (replaces `Gear.blp`) |

Every name was checked present: `Media.lua:92-140`, `media/icons/{ellipses,help}.tga` and `media/icons/phosphor/lightning.tga`.

Shipping a `ph:` icon also ships `phosphor/LICENSE-phosphor.txt`, and the README credits Phosphor and Open Iconic.

The ui task closes P4 by grepping `NS.Icon(` against this list (P4.31).

**P4.31 revision.** The grep found three names nothing draws: `edit` (the Rename item is a plain menu line, §7.5) and `lock`/`unlock` (the bar's Master Lock is a setting and a slash verb, and the bar draws no lock mark, §7.7). They left the declaration and the payload (`tools/prune-icons.lua` at v1.85.0). `test_packaging` now holds the line to exactly what is drawn: every literal `NS.Icon(` name in the shipped files, the kit's `LIBRARY_MARKS`, and the debug console's two title-bar buttons.

### 1.5 `.luacheckrc`

The base is DpsRace's skeleton (BP §12):
- `std = "lua51"`, `max_line_length = false`, `codes = true`
- No top-level `ignore`
- `exclude_files = { "libs/", "tests/_kit/", "docs/audits/", "docs/reviews/", "docs/revendor/", "_dev/" }`
- `read_globals` = DpsRace's base list
- `globals = { "OutfitterRebornDB", "OutfitterRebornPerfDB", "StaticPopupDialogs", "BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTERREBORN", "OutfitterReborn" }`. The `BINDING_NAME_*` globals are written via `rawset`.
- `files["tests/"].globals = { "_G.OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST", "_G.OutfitterRebornDB", "_G.OutfitterRebornPerfDB" }`
- `212/self` stanzas: `core/Database.lua`, `core/OutfitterReborn.lua`, `settings/Slash.lua`
- `tests/wow_mock.lua`, `tests/mock_gear.lua` and `tests/mock_world.lua` also take `432/self`

**Per-file client-API stanzas.** Each stanza names only what that file may touch, and each carries a reason comment. This makes "equipment calls live only in Compat" a lint fact. Engine modules name **no** client global; they read everything through `NS.Compat` (§5).

| File | Client APIs |
|---|---|
| `core/Compat.lua` | `issecretvalue, canaccessvalue, issecrettable, canaccesstable, C_AddOns, GetSpecialization, GetSpecializationInfo, GetNumSpecializations, UnitClass, UnitFactionGroup, UnitLevel, UnitIsDeadOrGhost, InCombatLockdown, GetCVar, SetCVar, GetTime, C_PlayerInfo`, and (from P4.2) `C_Container, C_Item, C_ItemUpgrade, C_EquipmentSet, C_PetJournal, C_TooltipInfo, C_AzeriteEmpoweredItem, GetInventoryItemLink, GetInventoryItemID, GetInventorySlotInfo, GetInventoryItemCooldown, PickupInventoryItem, EquipCursorItem, ClearCursor, CursorHasItem, GetCursorInfo, SetCurrentTitle, GetMacroIcons, GetMacroItemIcons, EquipmentManager_GetLocationData, PawnGetItemData, GetNumTitles, GetTitleName, IsTitleKnown, Enum` |
| `modules/States.lua` | `C_UnitAuras, C_RestrictedActions, GetShapeshiftFormInfo, GetNumShapeshiftForms, IsMounted, IsSwimming, UnitOnTaxi, UnitPower, UnitPowerMax, UnitHealth, UnitHealthMax, GameTooltip` |
| `modules/States_Zone.lua` | `C_Map, GetInstanceInfo, IsInInstance, GetZoneText, GetMinimapZoneText, GetRealZoneText` (H9) |
| `modules/Triggers_*.lua` | Each file lists the reads its conditions make (`IsFalling, IsResting, UnitIsPVP, UnitGUID, UnitIsPlayer, UnitName, UnitInVehicle, C_QuestLog, C_TradeSkillUI, C_Minimap, SpellIsTargeting, HasFullControl, DoEmote, ChangeActionBarPage`) |
| `modules/Tooltips*.lua`, `modules/OutfitTooltip.lua` | `TooltipDataProcessor, TooltipUtil, GameTooltip, ShoppingTooltip1, ShoppingTooltip2, ConvertFormatStringToSearchPattern, EQUIPMENT_SETS, hooksecurefunc` |
| `modules/Dock.lua`, `Window*.lua`, `SlotEnables.lua` | `CharacterFrame, PaperDollFrame, CharacterFrameCloseButton, ToggleCharacter, UISpecialFrames, GameTooltip, SpellCanTargetItem, SpellCanTargetItemID, MerchantFrame, CursorHasItem` |
| `modules/QuickSlots*.lua` | `PaperDollItemsFrame, EquipmentFlyout_Hide, EquipmentFlyoutPopoutButton_OnClick, hooksecurefunc` |
| `modules/Bar*.lua`, `modules/IconChooser.lua` | `GetCursorInfo, C_Spell, C_Item, GetMacroInfo, IsAltKeyDown` |
| `settings/Slash.lua` | `SecureCmdOptionParse` |

The gate is `~/.claude/dev-copilot/bin/ka0s-bounded luacheck .` at 0 warnings / 0 errors.

`.luacheckrc` is a **spine file** (§12). P4.0 writes every per-file stanza in this table up front, including those for files that are still stubs, so no parallel task edits it. A task that finds it needs another global hands the addition back with its `SUITES` flip.

### 1.6 `.pkgmeta`

```yaml
package-as: OutfitterReborn

# Libraries are vendored in libs/ and committed to git -- NOT fetched as externals.

ignore:
  - .luacheckrc
  - .pkgmeta
  - .gitignore

  - docs
  - tests
  - tools
  - CLAUDE.md
  - DEPENDENCIES.md

  - media/screenshots
  - _dev
  - "*.bak"
```

`Bindings.xml`, `README.md` and `LICENSE` ship (layout-§1).

**Every place the name appears:**

| # | Site | Value | Literal or derived |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | TOC | `git mv Outfitter.toc OutfitterReborn.toc` | file |
| 2 | `.pkgmeta` | `package-as: OutfitterReborn` | literal |
| 3 | TOC `IconTexture` | `Interface\AddOns\OutfitterReborn\media\logos\outfitterreborn.logo.128.tga` | the only hand-typed AddOns path |
| 4 | `addonName` / AceAddon | `OutfitterReborn`; `GetAddon("OutfitterReborn")` in `test_skeleton` | client vararg |
| 5 | SVs | `OutfitterRebornDB` (`addonName.."DB"`), `OutfitterRebornPerfDB` | derived + TOC/lint literals |
| 6 | LDB + LibDBIcon key | `OutfitterReborn` (`Launcher:New{ name = addonName }`). This supersedes INV §1's "keep `Outfitter` forever": LDB `NewDataObject` returns nil on a duplicate, so a legacy install would steal the launcher. Version History and FAQ say so. | derived |
| 7 | Bindings | `BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTERREBORN`; buttons `OutfitterRebornToggleWindow`, `OutfitterRebornWearOutfit1..10`, `OutfitterRebornAutoOn`, `OutfitterRebornAutoOff`, `OutfitterRebornUpdateOutfit` | permanent literals (launcher-§6) |
| 8 | Media | `C.LOGO_128 = ("Interface\\AddOns\\%s\\media\\logos\\%s.logo.128.tga"):format(addonName, addonName:lower())`; the same for `C.LOGO` | derived |
| 9 | LibKa0s owner keys | `Media.Icon(addonName, …)`, Skin owner, DebugLog `addonName`, Lifecycle `name`, Bus `name` | derived |
| 10 | Public API | `_G.OutfitterReborn.API.v1`; no shim (H3) | derived |
| 11 | Bus wire names | `Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_<Pascal>` | literals in `core/Bus.lua` only |

| 17 | Unchanged | Title/brand; `/of`, `/outfitter`; GitHub `tusharsaxena/Outfitter`; CurseForge 1698220; the checkout dir; the prune path `../Outfitter`; smoke ids `SM-OF-*`; skin tokens `of.*` | — |

The owner's new Ka0s logo already landed in `11ccec5`: sources renamed, both TGAs regenerated from the 2000² PNG with the layout-§4 recipe (`.128.tga` 128², `.tga` 300², both type 2 / 32 bpp). The move is a rename only; regenerate rather than hand-edit if the art changes again. On this case-insensitive checkout `Media`→`media` goes via a temporary name.

## 2. Seams

### 2.1 Core seams (BP §6, each with a degrade stub)

| Seam file | Publishes | LibKa0s major | Stub when the library is missing |
|---|---|---|---|
| `core/MediaSetup.lua` | `NS.Icon(name)`, `NS.MediaFont`, `RegisterLSM` | Media 12 | `NS.Icon` → nil (callers hide the texture) |
| `core/CoreSetup.lua` | `NS.Util.print`, `NS.SafeRegisterEvent(target, ev, fn, rejected)`, `NS.SafeRegisterUnitEvent(target, ev, unit, fn, rejected)`, `NS.rejectedEvents`, `NS.SafeToString`, `NS.ApplySkin(frame)`, `NS.MakeCloseButton(f, onClick)`, `NS.RaiseStaticPopups()` | Core 15 | DR `CoreSetup.lua` shape |
| `core/SkinSetup.lua` | `NS.Skin` (`Skin:For(addonName)`), `NS.SKIN_TOKENS` (`of.*`, §7.1), `NS.SkinEscape(token)`, `NS.QualityColor(q)` (heirloom 7 drawn as 6), `NS.SkinChoice`/`WriteSkinChoice`/`ApplySkinChoice`/`HideSkinPicker`/`MaybeShowSkinPicker`/`RunSkinVerb` | Skin 15 | DR shape |
| `core/Compat.lua` | `NS.Compat.*`. Every member answers plain-or-nil (§5.13 table) | Compat 1 | The library's `Compat` provides the detectors; the version-variant ladders are host code |
| `core/Secrets.lua` | §2.2 | — (host) | — |
| `core/Bus.lua` | `NS.MSG`, `NS.NewBusTarget()`, `NS.SendBus(key, ...)`, `NS.BusStandDown()`/`BusStandUp()` | Bus 2 (`Catalog`, `NewTarget`) | Degraded arm: AceEvent messages with per-handler `xpcall` |
| `core/PoolSetup.lua` | `NS.Pool.New/Acquire/ReleaseAll/NewKeyed/AcquireKeyed` | Pool 3 | Plain-table pool |
| `core/ItemSetup.lua` | `NS.Item.ItemIDFromLink`, `NS.Item.BonusIDsFromLink` | Item 3 (LK `docs/api/Item/version-3-docs.md:28`) | Pattern fallback |
| `core/WidgetsSetup.lua` | `NS.MakeDropdown`, `NS.OpenMenu(anchor, fill)`, `NS.CloseMenu`, `NS.MakeTestStrip(parent, label, opts)`, `NS.TEST_STRIP_HEIGHT`, `NS.ShowCopy` | Widgets 21 (`Widgets.lua:746,757,788,836,1003`; `WidgetsDragHandle.lua:979`) | `MenuUtil.CreateContextMenu` |
| `core/SecureQueue.lua` | `NS.SecureQueue.Run(key, fn)`: runs now out of lockdown, otherwise stores per key (latest wins). `Flush()` on `PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED`. | — (host) | — |
| `core/LifecycleSetup.lua` | §5.12 | Lifecycle 3 | DR shape |
| `core/PerfSetup.lua` | `NS.Perf` with the fixed bucket list below (performance-§3) | Perf 15 | no-op |
| `core/DebugLogSetup.lua` | `NS.Debug(tag, fmt, ...)`, `NS.DebugAtEnable`, `NS.DebugLog:ConsoleCheckbox()`. The descriptor's host `initSummary` (debug-logging-§5, DR `core/DebugLogSetup.lua:73-81`) returns `("%s v%s, schema v%s, profile '%s', %d outfits, %d with triggers"):format(NS.BRAND, NS.version, schema, profile, n, m)`, each value through `NS.SafeToString` and the counts walked over `db.char.outfits` | DebugLog 22 | print-gated stub |
| `core/LauncherSetup.lua` | `NS.Launcher`, binding buttons, `NS.BINDINGS`, `NS.WearBoundOutfit(n)` | Launcher 7 | DR `LauncherSetup.lua:58-80` |
| `settings/OptionsSetup.lua` | `NS.Helpers`, `NS.RegisterTabbedPage`, `NS.OpenOptionsPanel(pageId?)`, `NS.ShowResetAll` | Options 37 | DR shape |
| `settings/Schema.lua` | `NS.Schema` rows, `NS.RegisterSchemaRows`, `NS.SetByPath(path, v, instanceId)`, `NS.GetSetting(path, instanceId)`, `NS.FindSchemaRow`, `NS.ApplyDefault`, `NS.ValidateSchema` | Schema 3 | DR shape |
| `settings/Slash.lua` | `NS.COMMANDS`, `NS.RunVerb(verb, rest)` | Slash 21 | DR `Slash.lua:100-172` |

**Perf buckets** (performance-§3; lowerCamelCase per the naming cheatsheet). Declared in this order in the `core/PerfSetup.lua` descriptor, each bracketed by exactly one call site:

| Key | `within` | Bracketed at |
|---|---|---|
| `auraScan` | — | `States` player `UNIT_AURA` scan (§6.10), the hottest path |
| `zoneUpdate` | — | `States_Zone` debounced zone producer (§6.5) |
| `bagSync` | — | `Inv.Sync` (§5.4) |
| `equipUpdate` | — | `E.Update` (§5.10) |
| `equipExecute` | `equipUpdate` | `E.Execute`, when called from `E.Update` (Bank's direct call is not bracketed) |
| `triggerDispatch` | — | `Binder.Dispatch` (§6.9). One static key; the trigger id goes to `NS.Debug("Trigger", …)` only. |
| `optimizeStep` | — | one coroutine resume of `GA` (§5.14) |
| `dockFollow` | — | one throttled follow tick (§7.2) |
| `barRender` | — | `Bar.Render` (§7.7) |
| `tooltipUsedBy` | — | `Tooltips.OnItem` past its gates (§7.10) |

`test_perf_harness` drives each path once and asserts every declared bucket has a non-zero count, and that `equipExecute` only ever runs inside `equipUpdate`.

### 2.2 Secrets (`core/Secrets.lua`)

The names are unified across the slices:

| Function | Returns |
|---|---|
| `NS.Secrets.IsSecret(v)` | bool. Alias `NS.IsSecret`. |
| `NS.Secrets.Unsecret(v)` | `v` if plain, else nil. Alias `NS.Unsecret`. |
| `NS.Secrets.PlainString(v)` | a plain string, or nil (nil, secret or non-string) |
| `NS.Secrets.PlainNumber(v)` | a plain number, or nil |
| `NS.Secrets.UnsecretNumber(v, fallback)` | a plain number, or `fallback`. Alias `NS.SafeNumber(v, fallback)`. |
| `NS.Secrets.PlainTruth(v)` | `true`/`false`/nil (also `1` → true) |
| `NS.Secrets.PlainEquals(a, b)` | bool; false if either side is secret |
| `NS.Secrets.SafeIterate(t, max)` | an iterator bounded at `max` (default 512) that never uses `#` |

Rule (INV §12, BP §6.9): no module receives a raw value that might be secret. Risky reads happen in Compat **before** any engine state moves, and a raise inside an equipment Batch cannot strand the counter anyway (§5.10).

### 2.3 Bus catalog (`core/Bus.lua`, `NS.MSG`)

The wire name is `Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_<Pascal of the key>`. Every message has exactly one sending module (architecture-§4 :113). With 25 messages, `docs/message-bus.md` is required.

| Key | Sender | Payload | Main receivers |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SETTINGS_CHANGED` | `settings/Schema.lua` announce | `(path, value)`; profile and global paths only (instance rows announce through `NS.OutfitSchema`, §3.5) | Binder (`auto.enabled`), Bar (`bar.*`), Window |
| `PROFILE_CHANGED` | `core/Database.lua` | `(key)` | everything that re-applies |
| `OUTFIT_ADDED` | Outfits | `(id)` | Window, Bar, Binder (`Bind(id)`), API v1 |
| `OUTFIT_DELETED` | Outfits | `(id, name)` | Window, Bar, Binder (unbind), API v1 |
| `OUTFIT_EDITED` | Outfits (also for instance-row writes via the `NS.OutfitSchema` announce, C12) | `(id, what)` | Window, Bar, Tooltips, Binder (coalesced rebind on `what == "trigger"`), API v1 |
| `OUTFIT_RENAMED` | Outfits | `(id, oldName, newName)` | Window, Bar, API v1 |
| `OUTFIT_WORN` | OutfitStack | `(id, layer, byTrigger)` | Binder (`OUTFIT_WORN` pseudo), Window, Bar, API v1 |
| `OUTFIT_REMOVED` | OutfitStack | `(id, byTrigger)` | Binder, Window, Bar, API v1 |
| `SELECTION_CHANGED` | OutfitStack | `(id\|nil)` | Window, SlotEnables |
| `STACK_CHANGED` | Equip (on bracket close) | `()` | Window, Bar, LDB tooltip line |
| `INVENTORY_CHANGED` | Inventory | `(equippedChanged, bagsChanged)` | OutfitStack_Watch, Window, Tooltips cache |
| `SWAP_COMPLETE` | OutfitStack_Watch | `()` | EM (pending save) |
| `EM_SYNCED` | EquipmentManager | `()` | Window (`MarkDirty`) |
| `STATS_CHANGED` | ItemStats | `()` | Rebuild dialog stat menus |
| `OPTIMIZE_STARTED` | Optimize | `(job)` | Progress dialog |
| `OPTIMIZE_PROGRESS` | Optimize | `(job, noChangeCount, maxNoChange, bestText)` | Progress dialog |
| `OPTIMIZE_DONE` | Optimize | `(job, ok, reason)` | Progress dialog |
| `PLAYER_STATE_CHANGED` | PlayerState | `(key, value)`, key ∈ `combat, dead, casting, spec` | Bar (combat alpha), Preview. Spec triggers use the `ACTIVE_PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED` game event, not this message (§6.8). |
| `STATE_CHANGED` | States (`NS.States.Set`, shared by `States.lua` and `States_Zone.lua`) | `(stateId, isActive)` | Binder |
| `AURAS_SCANNED` | States | `()` | Binder (HAS_BUFF) |
| `TOOLTIP_SHOWN` | States | `()` | Binder |
| `TOOLTIP_HIDDEN` | States | `()` | Binder |
| `TRIGGERS_REBOUND` | Binder | `(count)` | Window (row badges), Diagnostics |
| `PREVIEW_CHANGED` | Preview | `(active)` | Bar |
| `LEGACY_DETECTED` | `core/OutfitterReborn.lua` | `()` | Diagnostics |

**Isolation.** Each handler is isolated by LibKa0s-Bus, or by the degraded arm. A handler's return value is never treated as an error (fixes INV §6.4, §19 `DispatchOutfitEvent`). Nothing is dispatched before `OnEnable` and the LoadPass (INV §6.4); that includes `LEGACY_DETECTED`, which is held until the first LoadPass finishes (§4.7).

---

## 3. Saved data and schema

### 3.1 AceDB store (`core/Database.lua`, `defaults/Profile.lua`)

`NS.db = LibStub("AceDB-3.0"):New(addonName .. "DB", NS.defaults, true)` creates the shared "Default" profile (H30).

`OnProfileChanged`, `OnProfileCopied` and `OnProfileReset` all go to `NS.OnProfileChanged(event, db, key)` (BP §6.22), which runs:
1. `NS.HideSkinPicker()`, then `NS.ApplySkinChoice()`.
2. `NS.RunMigrations()`, on `OnProfileReset` only.
3. `NS.SyncEnabledHold()`.
4. `NS.ApplyProfile()`: Bar re-anchor, scale and alpha; `NS.TriggerBinder.SetActive(NS.GetSetting("auto.enabled"))`. It rebuilds from current state.
5. `NS.MaybeShowSkinPicker()`.
6. `NS.SendBus(NS.MSG.PROFILE_CHANGED, key)`.

**`db.char` is never touched by a profile callback** (H7, Q3). There is no reader of `gOutfitter_*` anywhere (R4). `test_database` pins that.

**Migration runner** (BP §6.22, extended):
- `NS.SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`, `STEPS = { { to = 1, run = function() end } }`.
- A step may declare `scope = "profile"` (walks `sv.profiles`) or `scope = "char"` (walks `sv.char`). The char walk carries a comment that the standard names only profiles.
- The stamp `db.global.schemaVersion` advances only after a step returns.

```lua
NS.defaults = {
  profile = {
    -- Master controls (options-ui-§15): they govern the outfit bar; the docked window has no geometry.
    enabled = true, visibility = "always", scale = 1.0, alpha = 1.0, locked = false,
    auto       = { enabled = true },                                -- was Options.DisableAutoSwitch (inverted)
    equip      = { muteSounds = true, showErrors = true, announceKeys = true },
    tooltip    = { usedBy = true, compare = true },
    quickSlots = { enabled = true },                                -- new row (today it cannot be turned off)
    bar        = { shown = false, vertical = false, showBackground = true, combatAlpha = 1.0 },
    windows    = { bar = { point = "TOPLEFT", x = 200, y = -200 }, dialogs = {} },   -- named non-setting state
    skin       = { source = "ka0s", skin = "ka0s", ignorePanel = true, ignoreFonts = false,
                   font = "", texture = "", outline = "hud", pickerSeen = false },
  },
  char = {
    seeded      = false,                        -- LoadPass has created the built-in outfits
    nextID      = 1,                            -- outfit id counter, never reused; ids are "o<N>"
    outfits     = {},                           -- [id] = OutfitRecord (§3.3)
    order       = { Complete = {}, Accessory = {} },   -- display order, arrays of ids
    bindings    = {},                           -- [1..10] = outfit id; unique by construction
    stack       = {},                           -- array of StackEntry, bottom → top (§3.4)
    recent      = {},                           -- Complete-outfit ids, most recent LAST
    emPreserved = nil,                          -- [lowerName] = OutfitRecord while the EM reports zero sets (Q8)
  },
  global = { schemaVersion = 0, minimap = { hide = false }, skinNotice = {} },
}
```

- `global.schemaVersion = 0` is never the current version, because AceDB strips a value equal to its default.
- Session-only state stays out: `NS.State.debug`, `NS.State.testMode`, `NS.State.legacyLoaded`.
- Defaults where a falsy value is a real choice are read with `== nil` (savedvariables-§5). The positive phrasing removes every inverted `Disable*` flag.

### 3.2 Old key → new home (INV §2.1–§2.4)

| Old key | New home | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Version` (22) | dropped | `global.schemaVersion` + the runner |
| `Options.DisableAutoSwitch` | `profile.auto.enabled` | inverted (INV §7.4 Fix) |
| `Options.MinimapButton` | `global.minimap` (LibDBIcon's table) | only `hide` has a default. The `{x,y}` free-drag form and `showInCompartment` go with the fork (H6, H20) |
| `Options.HideMinimapButton` | dropped | INV §18 |
| `Options.DisableHotkeyMessages` | `profile.equip.announceKeys` | |
| `Options.DisableToolTipInfo` | `profile.tooltip.usedBy` | |
| `Options.DisableItemComparisons` | `profile.tooltip.compare` | |
| `Options.ShowStackContents` | dropped | debug tag `Stack` |
| `Outfits[categoryID]` | `char.outfits` + `char.order[category]` | §3.3 |
| `LastOutfitStack` + `LayerIndex` | `char.stack` (layer tag on each entry) | one array, one writer: fixes B6 (C9) |
| `RecentCompleteOutfits` (names) | `char.recent` (ids) | renames are safe |
| `OutfitBar.ShowOutfitBar` | `profile.bar.shown` (`false`) | one default kills the fresh-vs-repair split (INV §0, §19) |
| `OutfitBar.Position` | `profile.windows.bar` | exposed as row `bar.position` |
| `OutfitBar.Scale` / `.Alpha` / `.LockPosition` | Master `scale` / `alpha` / `locked` | |
| `OutfitBar.CombatAlpha` | `profile.bar.combatAlpha` | effective = `alpha × combatAlpha` |
| `OutfitBar.Vertical` | `profile.bar.vertical` | |
| `OutfitBar.HideBackground` | `profile.bar.showBackground` | positive phrasing |
| `ShapeshiftIndexInfo` | runtime cache in `NS.States` | rebuilt from `GetShapeshiftFormInfo` (C9) |
| `PreservedEMOutfits` | `char.emPreserved` | owner: EM |
| `EnableEquipSounds` (nil = muted) | `profile.equip.muteSounds = true` | keeps quirk 13 (INV §16) |
| `DisableEquipErrors` | `profile.equip.showErrors` | |
| `LBFSettings` | dropped | no Masque |
| `GlobalSettings.SavedScripts` | dropped | INV §18, H1 |
| `GlobalSettings.Banned` | dropped | INV §17 |
| `gOutfitter_OutfitToDelete` | dropped | popup `data` (INV §19) |

### 3.3 Outfit record and item record

**Ids** (C9):
- `id = "o" .. char.nextID`, after which `nextID` is incremented. Ids are never reused.
- `NS.Outfits.Get` answers nil for any id not matching `^o%d+$`, so `preview:*` placeholders can never reach the Stack or Equip (C28).
- Readers treat a missing target as "none" (stale ids in `stack`, `recent` and `bindings`).
- LoadPass re-asserts `nextID = max + 1`.

```lua
-- db.char.outfits[id]
{ id = "o7", name = "Normal",
  kind = "native" | "em",
  category = "Complete" | "Accessory",          -- computed by NS.Outfits.Categorize
  items = { [slotName] = ItemRecord },           -- absent = slot unused; { id = 0 } = must be empty
  setID = 12,                                    -- kind "em" only; items is then the last resolved snapshot
  icon = fileID | nil,                           -- nil = Outfitter chooses; wildcard 134400 saves nil (INV §7.6)
  titleID = n | nil, unequipOthers = nil|true, ignoreComparisons = nil|true,
  bar = { hide = nil|true },
  trigger = nil | { id = "IN_DUNGEON",
                    disabled = nil|true,         -- THE one Disabled flag (INV §2.5): trigger inactive, outfit hidden from the bar
                                                 -- and dimmed in the list; cleared on manual wear (checkbox, left click, binding)
                    combatDisabled = nil|true },   -- H1
  triggerParams = { [TRIGGER_ID] = { [key] = value } },   -- typed per descriptor (H13); per trigger, survives switching
  triggerState  = { [TRIGGER_ID] = { … } },               -- what a trigger itself persists (Fishing/Stealth CVar restores, wasPvP)
  stat = nil | { { stat = "STA", min = n|nil, max = n|nil }, complex = bool },   -- StatID/StatConfig unified
  builtin = nil | "NORMAL" | "NAKED" | "CLASS_<KEY>" | "SPEC_<n>",              -- seed key, makes LoadPass idempotent
}
```

**Dropped from the record:**
- `Script`, `SavedScript`, `ScriptID` and `ScriptSettings` (H1)
- a record-level `disabled`. Today's single `Disabled` flag (INV §2.5) is `trigger.disabled`, and every reader and writer uses it: the menu's "Turn trigger off", `ctx:Disable`, the manual-wear clear (§5.8), `O.Status` (`"disabled"`), the row dimming (§7.3), `Bar.LiveSource` (§7.7), `Binder.Gate`/`Bind` (§6.9). An outfit with no trigger cannot be disabled, as today (the menu row is disabled without one).
- `BindingIndex`, which moves to `char.bindings`
- `didEquip`/`didUnequip`, now runtime in `NS.Stack` (C31)
- `Unused`, `ItemLocations` and `TemporaryItems`, which are EM runtime
- `wasPvP`, which moves to `triggerState.PVP_FLAGGED`

**ItemRecord** has one canonical spelling per field, which fixes the four `BonusIDs` spellings (INV §2.5) and B12:

```lua
{ id = 12345, suffix = 0, enchant = 0, gems = "0:0:0:0", uid = 0,
  upType = 0, diff = 0,              -- link fields 11, 12 (UpgradeTypeID, InstanceDifficultyID)
  bonus = "6652:1472",               -- ALL bonus ids, link order, ":"-joined; "" when none
  mods  = "9=70:28=2462",            -- modifier pairs after the bonus list, "type=value"; "" when none
  azerite = "",                      -- sorted power ids ":"-joined
  link = "|cnIQ4:|Hitem:…|h[…]|h|r", name = "…", invType = "INVTYPE_HEAD", quality = 4, level = 639 }
```

- `NS.ItemInfo.EMPTY = { id = 0 }` is the single empty marker.
- `NS.ItemInfo.Snapshot(info)` copies only these keys. Live cache objects (`loc`, claims, score caches) are never saved (B15).
- A link is stored only after `Secrets.PlainString` succeeds.

### 3.4 Named non-setting state (architecture-§5 :155-161)

| Key | Sole writer | When |
|---|---|---|
| `profile.windows.bar` | `NS.Bar.SavePosition` | drag stop; the `bar.position` row default/reset **copies** the default (fixes `OutfitterBar.lua:107-115` aliasing) |
| `profile.windows.dialogs[key]` | `NS.Dialogs.SavePosition(key, point, x, y)` | dialog drag stop |
| `global.minimap.minimapPos` | LibDBIcon | minimap drag |
| `char.outfits`, `char.order`, `char.nextID`, `char.bindings`, `char.seeded` | `NS.Outfits` | the §5.6 writer API, plus the instance-row writes through Schema `resolveRoot` (C12) |
| `char.stack`, `char.recent` | `NS.Stack` | Wear, Remove, temp collapse, LoadPass repair |
| `char.emPreserved` | `NS.EM` | zero-sets protection (INV §3.5) |
| `outfits[id].triggerState[TID]` | `NS.TriggerBinder` via `ctx.persist` | trigger side effects (INV §9.9) |

**`StackEntry`** is `{ id = "o7", layer = "zone"|"shapeshift"|nil }` or `{ temp = { [slot] = ItemRecord }, layer = nil }`.

**Persisting the stack.** The stack and the recents are persisted because restoring the layering after `/reload` is current behaviour (INV §2.1). Under architecture-§5 (:155-161) they are named non-setting state (recorded data): this table names each key, its one owner and every writer, and ARCHITECTURE `## Settings Schema` (or `schema.md` once it spills) carries the same sentence. That sentence is the compliance, so no register row is owed (documentation-§3 :166). The checklist's "the outfit stack is runtime state" (CHK :204) is the checklist's note, not a rule of the standard.

### 3.5 Schema rows and instance rows

There are **two Schema instances** (LK Schema v3 `lib:New` is "one runtime for one host", and a host may hold more than one):
- `NS.Schema` (`settings/Schema.lua`): every settings row. It is the one Options, Slash and Reset read, so none of them ever sees an outfit row.
- `NS.OutfitSchema` (`modules/Outfits_Schema.lua`, ~140, owned by P4.6): the instance rows only. It is built at `OnInitialize` with the same degrade stub as `NS.Schema` (BP §9.1).

**`resolveRoot(parts, instanceId)`** lives in `core/Database.lua`, one function per instance (LK Schema v3 :127):

| Instance | `parts[1]` | Root | `first` | When `instanceId` is nil |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NS.Schema` | `"global"` | `db.global` | 2 | — |
| `NS.Schema` | anything else | `db.profile` | 1 | — |
| `NS.OutfitSchema` | `"outfit"` | `db.char.outfits[instanceId]` | 2 | `nil, "no outfit"` |
| `NS.OutfitSchema` | `"trigger"` | `db.char.outfits[instanceId].triggerParams` (created on demand) | 2 | `nil, "no outfit"` |

Because the settings instance has no outfit arm and the outfit instance refuses a nil id, a profile reset can never reach outfits (H7).

**One write seam.** `NS.SetByPath(path, v, instanceId)`, `NS.GetSetting(path, instanceId)` and `NS.Schema.SetMany(entries, opts)` stay the public names. When `instanceId` (or `opts.instanceId`) is non-nil they forward at call time to `NS.OutfitSchema.Set/Get/SetMany`; with `NS.OutfitSchema` absent they answer `false, "no outfit schema"`. P3 C12 writes the forward and `test_schema` pins both arms.

**Instance rows** (H13):
- The five `outfit.*` rows below are in `Outfits_Schema.lua`, registered at `:New` (P4.6).
- `NS.Triggers.Finalize()` (P4.15) appends the generated `trigger.<TID>.<key>` rows with `NS.OutfitSchema.AddRows(T.SchemaRows())`.
- `NS.OutfitSchema.Validate{ pages = { outfit = true }, types = … }` runs right after, in `OnInitialize`.
- Every row carries `page = "outfit"` and a `group` (anti-patterns #69): `group = "outfit"` for the `outfit.*` rows and `group = <TID>` for each trigger's params.
- Because they are not on `NS.Schema`, Options `rowsForPage`, Slash `allRows` (`/of list|get|set`, slash-commands-§3 "settings only, not outfits") and Reset all never reach them, with no filter to forget.

| Path | Type | Validate |
|---|---|---|
| `outfit.trigger.id` | string | `NS.Triggers.Get(v) ~= nil or v == ""` |
| `outfit.trigger.disabled` | bool | |
| `outfit.trigger.combatDisabled` | bool | |
| `outfit.icon` | number or nil | positive fileID |
| `outfit.bar.hide` | bool | |
| `trigger.<TID>.<key>` | per descriptor param | `NS.TriggerParams.TYPES[type].validate/normalize`; default from the descriptor |

That is about 250 rows, 132 of them zone spec toggles.

- The outfit instance's `Validate` `types` adds `spelllist, itemlist, maplist, subzonelist, unitlist, pet`.
- The settings announce sends `SETTINGS_CHANGED (path, value)`.
- The outfit instance's announce `(path, value, instanceId)` sends **no** `SETTINGS_CHANGED`. It calls only `NS.Outfits.Changed(instanceId, parts[1] == "trigger" and "trigger" or parts[2])`, so `OUTFIT_EDITED` is the one per-outfit message (C12) and the binder has one rebind route (§6.9).
- The dialog's OK writes `NS.Schema.SetMany(entries, { instanceId = id })`, which forwards and is all-or-nothing.

**Profile rows** are in §4.2.

- `writeThrough = { "enabled", "locked", "auto.enabled" }` (options-ui-§1 route (a)).
- `NS.MINIMAP_PATH = "global.minimap.shown"`, with `RESET_EXEMPT = { [NS.MINIMAP_PATH] = true }` (BP §9.1).
- `VALID_PAGES = { general = true, bar = true, profiles = true }` (settings instance only; the outfit instance validates against `{ outfit = true }`).

### 3.6 Reset semantics (H7, options-ui-§12)

**Reset all settings** is one act with three entry points: the General Defaults button, `/of resetall` and Profiles "Reset Profile".
- Popup `OUTFITTERREBORN_RESET_ALL`, defined in `settings/OptionsSetup.lua`. The text is options-ui-§12 verbatim through `L`; Yes/No, `timeout = 0`, `whileDead`, `hideOnEscape`.
- Accept runs `NS.Helpers.RestoreAllDefaults()`, then prints `L["All settings reset to defaults."]`.
- It resets the **active profile only**:
  - `skipRestoreAll` vetoes the Profiles rows and `global.minimap.shown`.
  - The session sweep ends test mode and turns the debug console off.
  - `NS.Dialogs.CloseAll("reset")` runs.
  - `OnProfileReset` then re-applies and publishes `PROFILE_CHANGED`.
- All of `db.char` and `db.global` survive.

**Delete all outfits…** is reached from `/of deleteall` and the General › Behavior act.
- Popup `OUTFITTERREBORN_DELETE_ALL`, defined in `modules/Popups.lua`:
  - Text: `L["Delete every outfit on this character and re-create the built-in ones? Blizzard equipment sets come back as outfits. Your settings are not affected."]`
  - Buttons: `L["Delete all outfits"]` / `L["Cancel"]`
- Refusals:
  - In combat: `L["Outfits can't be deleted in combat."]`
  - While stood down: `NS.StoodDownRefusal()`
- Accept runs `NS.Outfits.DeleteAll()`:
  1. `St.Select(nil)`
  2. `NS.TriggerBinder.UnbindAll()`
  3. `St.Clear()`
  4. wipe `outfits/order/bindings/stack/recent/emPreserved`, set `seeded = false`
  5. `LoadPass()`, which re-seeds and runs `EM.Sync`
  6. one `OUTFIT_DELETED` per id, then print `L["All outfits deleted. The built-in outfits were re-created."]`

  This replaces the old `reset` and fixes `AskReset`'s `strlower(nil)` (INV §4, §19).
- Blizzard equipment sets and the profile are untouched.

**Per-page and per-row resets:**
- Page Defaults restores that page's rows only (options-ui-§11).
- `/of reset <path>` resets one row.
- `/of reset bar.position` replaces the old `reset bar` (H5).

---

## 4. Settings pages, slash, bindings, launcher, locales

### 4.1 Files

| File | Budget | Derived from |
|---|---|---|
| `settings/Schema.lua` | ~220 | DR `settings/Schema.lua` (208), BP §9.1, plus instance-row registration |
| `settings/OptionsSetup.lua` | ~230 | DR (224), BP §9.3. Carries the H21 comment: "Options stay in-addon: three subcategories, below options-ui-§4's five-subpanel threshold (DpsRace precedent)." |
| `settings/General.lua` | ~260 | DR `settings/General.lua` (207), BP §9.4 |
| `settings/OutfitBar.lua` | ~100 | BP §9.5; pattern from DR `settings/Race.lua:269-275` |
| `settings/Profiles.lua` | ~50 | DR, verbatim shape (BP §9.6) |

**Combat** (options-ui-§2): the open gate is the library's. Bar buttons are insecure and minimap show/hide is insecure, so no rows need a combat gate.

### 4.2 Pages

**Landing page** (parent canvas, `OutfitterRebornMainPanel`). `NS.LANDING` holds:
- logo `NS.Constants.LOGO` and notes `NS.Meta("Notes")`
- a "Slash Commands" section from `NS.Slash:LandingRows()`
- a host credits line: `L["Originally created by John Stephen (mundocani). Maintained by aDd1kTeD2Ka0s, with thanks to Nulian and every earlier maintainer, tester and translator."]`

This replaces the About panel and its credits rain (INV §7.5: "the animation is optional"). The full credits table moves to the README (`test_credits`).

**Page `general`** (first subcategory, `OutfitterRebornGeneralPanel`). Tabs, in order: **Master controls | Behavior | Tooltips | Skin | Keybindings** (options-ui-§13, Keybindings last per §20).

*Master controls* is composed by `H.MasterControls` (options-ui-§15):

| Row | Path | Reaction |
|---|---|---|
| Enable Ka0s Outfitter Reborn | `enabled` | `NS.ApplyEnabled` |
| General visibility | `visibility` (`always\|inCombat\|outOfCombat\|never`) | `NS.OnVisibilityChanged()`; governs the bar, after `bar.shown` |
| Master scale | `scale` | `NS.Bar.ApplyScale()`; bar only (O14) |
| Master opacity | `alpha` | `NS.Bar.ApplyAlpha()` |
| Lock frame | `locked` | `NS.Bar.SetLocked(v)` |
| Debug console | `state.debugConsole` | `NS.DebugLog:ConsoleCheckbox()` (session) |
| Minimap button | `global.minimap.shown` | `NS.Launcher:IsShown()`/`SetShown()` |
| Test mode | `state.testMode` | `NS.Preview.Start/Stop`; host stamps `default = false` |
| Reset position | button | `NS.ApplyDefault(NS.FindSchemaRow("bar.position"))` |
| Reset all settings | button | `NS.ShowResetAll` |

The composer is called with `addonName = NS.BRAND` and nothing omitted, because the bar is a real movable frame. Its arguments:
- `notes.scale = L["Applies to the outfit bar. The outfit window follows the character sheet."]`
- `notes.locked = L["Locks the outfit bar."]`
- `resetPositionTooltip = L["Moves the outfit bar back to its starting place."]`

There are no `extra` rows.

*Behavior* (group `L["Behavior"]`):

| Subgroup | Path | Default | Label | Tooltip / onChange |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Outfit changes | `auto.enabled` | `true` | "Switch outfits automatically" | "Lets outfits with a trigger equip and unequip themselves. Turning this off stops every trigger; outfits you wear by hand are unaffected." No `onChange`: the binder's `SETTINGS_CHANGED` handler for `auto.enabled` is the one route, so the panel, `/of auto`, the bindings and a profile change all reach `SetActive` the same way (§6.9) |
| Outfit changes | `equip.muteSounds` | `true` | "Mute sound effects while changing outfits" | "Silences the equip sounds during a swap and restores them afterwards." |
| Outfit changes | `equip.showErrors` | `true` | "Show equip errors" | |
| Outfit changes | `equip.announceKeys` | `true` | "Announce outfit changes from key bindings" | "Shows 'Outfit equipped' on screen when a key binding wears or removes an outfit." |
| Character sheet | `quickSlots.enabled` | `true` | "Open the item flyout when you click an empty slot or drop an item on it" | → `NS.QuickSlots.SetEnabled(v)` (hook bodies read the flag) |

The old one-row "Automation" subsection is merged into "Outfit changes" (options-ui :176). "Character sheet" stays a one-row subsection on purpose: its heading names the surface the row acts on, which the row's label (an action) does not say. A one-line comment at the row records that reason.
| *(none, last)* | act | button | "Delete all outfits…" | §3.6; appended through `afterGroup[L["Behavior"]]`, with no heading (options-ui-§7) |

*Tooltips* (group `L["Tooltips"]`):
- `tooltip.usedBy` (`true`), "Show which outfits use an item" → `NS.Tooltips.Refresh()` (§7.10)
- `tooltip.compare` (`true`), "Compare items with your outfits" → `NS.Compare.Refresh()` (§7.10)

*Skin*: `H.AppearanceTab({ owner = addonName, get = NS.SkinChoice, set = NS.WriteSkinChoice })` plus eight `skin.*` `skipRender` rows, copied from DR General.lua.

*Keybindings*: `H.KeybindingsTab({ bindings = NS.BINDINGS })`. The General Defaults tooltip ends with `H.KEYBINDS_UNCHANGED` (options-ui-§20).

**Page `bar`** (`OutfitterRebornBarPanel`, title "Outfit Bar"). It has one tab, **"Display"** (options-ui-§13; one-row subsections merged per §7). It is not "Size and position" (options-ui §16): the bar has no size row (scale is a Master control), and its one anchor row, `bar.position`, is a `skipRender` row that the panel never draws. It is not "Layout" either, which §16 keeps for a tab arranging many cells:

| Path | Type / default | Label | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bar.shown` | bool / `false` | "Show outfit bar" | leads the block (options-ui-§16) → `NS.Bar.Refresh` |
| `bar.vertical` | bool / `false` | "Vertical layout" | "One column per category." |
| `bar.showBackground` | bool / `true` | "Show background" | O13 |
| `bar.combatAlpha` | number / `1.0`, 0..1, step 0.05 | "Opacity in combat (%)" | "Multiplied by Master opacity on General > Master controls." |
| `bar.position` | string / `"TOPLEFT 200 -200"` | "Outfit bar position" | `skipRender`; its own get/set encode `profile.windows.bar` |

`defaultsTooltip = L["Restore every Outfit Bar setting to its default. The bar's position is reset too."]`.

**Page `profiles`** (last). AceDBOptions through AceConfigDialog in a Fill `SimpleGroup` (DR), with one line above it: `L["Profiles hold settings only. Outfits belong to each character and are not changed by switching, copying or resetting a profile."]` (H7, H30, Q3).

### 4.3 Slash surface (`settings/Slash.lua` ~340, `modules/Commands.lua` ~300)

#### 4.3.1 Registration and macro conditionals (H25)

```lua
local function route(msg)                       -- host routing, not a second dispatcher (H25)
  msg = msg or ""
  if not msg:find("|h", 1, true) and (msg:sub(1, 1) == "[" or msg:find(";", 1, true)) then
    local parsed = SecureCmdOptionParse(msg)
    if parsed == nil then return end            -- no clause matched: silent no-op (INV §4)
    msg = parsed
  end
  return Sl:OnSlash(msg)
end
function Sl:Register()
  NS.addon:RegisterChatCommand(NS.SLASH:sub(2), route)        -- /of
  NS.addon:RegisterChatCommand(NS.SLASH_LONG:sub(2), route)   -- /outfitter
end
```

- An outfit verb whose argument starts with `[` passes that argument alone through `SecureCmdOptionParse`, so `/of wear [spec:1] Tank; DPS` works.
- Both paths are documented in `docs/slash-dispatch.md`.
- Tested cases: the `|h` bypass, the nil result, the top-level parse and the argument parse.

#### 4.3.2 `NS.COMMANDS`

Positional triples (slash-commands-§3). Reserved verbs come first, in DR order (DR `settings/Slash.lua:30-49`).

| Verb | Description key | Handler | Live while disabled? |
|---|---|---|---|
| `help` | "Show this help" | `cli:PrintHelp()` | live |
| `config` | "Open the settings panel" | `NS.OpenOptionsPanel()` | live |
| `skin` | "Choose a skin: open the skin picker" | `feature(NS.RunSkinVerb)` | refused |
| `enable` / `disable` | "Turn the addon on" / "Turn the addon off without unloading it" | `cli:CliSet("enabled true\|false")` | live |
| `list` / `get` / `set` / `reset` | library | `cli:Cli*` | live |
| `resetall` | "Reset every setting (asks first)" | `NS.ShowResetAll()` | live |
| `debug` / `diagnostics` / `perf` / `version` | library | as in DR | live |
| `show` | "Show the outfit window on the character sheet" | `feature(NS.Dock.Open)` | refused |
| `hide` | "Hide the outfit window" | `feature(NS.Dock.Close)` | refused |
| `wear [outfit]` | "Wear an outfit (default: the current Complete outfit)" | `feature(Commands.Wear)` | refused |
| `unwear [outfit]` | "Take an outfit off" | `feature(Commands.Unwear)` | refused |
| `toggle [outfit]` | "Wear an outfit, or take it off if it is worn" | `feature(Commands.Toggle)` | refused |
| `update [outfit]` | "Update an outfit to the items you have equipped" | `feature(Commands.Update)` → `NS.Outfits.SetToCurrent` | refused |
| `deposit [outfit]` | "Put an outfit's items in the bank (bank must be open)" | `feature(Commands.Bank, "Deposit")` | refused |
| `depositunique [outfit]` | "Put in the bank only the items no other outfit uses" | `"DepositUnique"` | refused |
| `depositothers [outfit]` | "Put every other outfit's items in the bank" | `"DepositOthers"` | refused |
| `withdraw [outfit]` | "Take an outfit's items out of the bank" | `"Withdraw"` | refused |
| `withdrawothers [outfit]` | "Take every other outfit's items out of the bank" | `"WithdrawOthers"` (implemented, INV §3.7 Fix) | refused |
| `missing` | "List the outfit items you no longer have (bank must be open)" | `feature(Commands.Missing)` → `NS.Bank.MissingReport()` | refused |
| `unequip <item or slot number>` | "Move one equipped item to your bags" | `feature(NS.Moves.UnequipByName)` | refused |
| `updatetitle` | "Re-apply the title of the outfit you are wearing" | `feature(function() NS.Stack.UpdateTitle(true) end)` | refused |
| `auto [on\|off]` | "Automatic outfit changes: /of auto on, off, or no word to see the state" | `cli:CliSet("auto.enabled …")`; bare → `CliGet`; `enable`/`disable` accepted as synonyms | live (`HOST_LIVE_VERBS`) |
| `zone` | "Show what Outfitter knows about where you are" | `Commands.Zone` → `NS.States.ZoneInfo()` .. `NS.Equip.Diagnostic()` | live (read-only) |
| `iteminfo <link>` | "Print an item link's fields" | `NS.ItemInfo.LinkInfoLines` (secret-guarded) | live (read-only) |
| `test` | "Sample outfits on the outfit bar: /of test" | `feature(NS.Preview.Toggle)` | refused |
| `lock` / `unlock` | "Lock the outfit bar in place" / "Unlock the outfit bar to move it" | `cli:CliSet("locked …")` (slash-commands-§8) | refused |
| `deleteall` | "Delete every outfit on this character (asks first)" | `feature(NS.Popups.ShowDeleteAll)` | refused |

- `HOST_LIVE_VERBS = { "auto", "zone", "iteminfo" }`, appended to `SlashLib.LIVE_VERBS` and mirrored in the stub's gate.
- A comment above the table records the declined SHOULD (standalone-windows :52): `show`/`hide` exist, but the window-`toggle` verb does not, because `toggle` has been the outfit verb in players' macros since before this fork (C35). `docs/slash-dispatch.md` says the same.
- `NS.RunVerb(verb, rest)` follows DR `Slash.lua:82-87`.

**Old verbs:**

| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `sound on\|off` | `/of set equip.muteSounds false\|true` (fixes the inverted messages, INV §19) |
| `errors on\|off`, `daxdax` | `/of set equip.showErrors …` |
| `reset bar` | `/of reset bar.position` |
| old `reset` | `deleteall` |
| old `enable`/`disable` (auto-switch) | `auto on\|off` |
| `summary`, `rating`, `itemstats` | dropped (dead, INV §18) |
| bare `/unequip` | dropped (H5) |

**Outfit-argument rules** (`Commands.ResolveOutfit(rest)`, INV §4):
- Case and spacing are kept, and `NS.Outfits.FindByName` matches case-insensitively.
- An empty argument means `NS.Stack.CurrentComplete()`, or prints `L["No Complete outfit is equipped."]`.
- A missing name prints `L["Couldn't find an outfit named %s."]`.
- Every wear, unwear, toggle and update passes `hw = true` (INV §16 quirk 22).
- No chat line ends in `:` (slash-commands-§4). The `missing` intro is `L["Missing items (an item used by several outfits is listed once per outfit)."]`.

**Output conventions:**
- Help header: `[OR] v1.1.0 — slash commands (/outfitter is an alias for /of)`.
- The disabled refusal uses the library `DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT`: `[OR] Ka0s Outfitter Reborn is disabled — enable it with /of enable`.

### 4.4 Key bindings (H11; `core/LauncherSetup.lua`, `Bindings.xml`)

There are 14 named, hidden, **non-secure** buttons, built in a loop over one spec table. Each uses `RegisterForClicks("AnyDown")`. Every button's `OnClick` first asks `NS.StoodDownRefusal()` and returns if it refuses (launcher :146), then calls the handler its verb uses. That covers AutoOn/AutoOff even though `auto` is a live slash verb: a press while disabled never writes `auto.enabled` (slash-commands-§7).

| # | Button (permanent) | Handler | `BINDING_NAME_` label | Old binding |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `OutfitterRebornToggleWindow` | `NS.Dock.Toggle()` | "Toggle outfit window" | `OUTFITTER_OUTFIT` |
| 2–11 | `OutfitterRebornWearOutfit1` … `10` | `NS.WearBoundOutfit(n)` | "Wear outfit 1" … "Wear outfit 10" | `OUTFITTER_OUTFIT1..10` |
| 12 | `OutfitterRebornAutoOn` | `NS.RunVerb("auto", "on")` | "Turn on automatic outfit changes" | `OUTFITTER_ENABLEAUTOMATION` |
| 13 | `OutfitterRebornAutoOff` | `NS.RunVerb("auto", "off")` | "Turn off automatic outfit changes" | `OUTFITTER_DISABLEAUTOMATION` |
| 14 | `OutfitterRebornUpdateOutfit` | `NS.RunVerb("update", "")` | "Update outfit to equipped items" | `OUTFITTER_UPDATEOUTFIT` (INV §5 Fix: with no Complete outfit it prints the line) |

- `NS.BINDINGS` is one table in `Bindings.xml` order: `{ command = "CLICK <btn>:LeftButton", tooltip = L[…] }`.
- `rawset(_G, "BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTERREBORN", NS.BRAND)` runs at file scope. The brand never goes through `L` (launcher-§1, anti-patterns #84), so no overlay can change it. Labels are verb phrases with no brand.
- `Bindings.xml` has 14 `<Binding name="CLICK …:LeftButton" category="BINDING_HEADER_OUTFITTERREBORN"/>` lines, with no `header`, no `default` and no Lua body.

**`NS.WearBoundOutfit(n)`:**
- Refuses via `NS.StoodDownRefusal()` (the button already asked; the handler asks again for direct callers).
- Calls `worn, rec = NS.Stack.WearBound(n)`, which owns the anti-panic rule: a repeat of the same `n` within `0.75` s is ignored, and the timer refreshes on every press (INV §5, §16 #17). It also resolves `char.bindings[n]`, and wears a Complete outfit with `{ now = true, hw = true, clearDisabled = true }` or toggles anything else with `{ now = true, hw = true, clearDisabled = true }` (INV §5: the binding clears `Disabled` and sets `HasHWEvent`; the toggle is `ToggleOutfitNow`, Outfitter.lua:3751, group F review).
- If `n` is unbound, prints `L["No outfit is bound to key %d."]`.
- If `equip.announceKeys` is on, adds `L["%s equipped"]` or `L["%s unequipped"]` to `UIErrorsFrame` in `C.OUTFIT_MESSAGE_COLOR` (0.2, 0.75, 0.3).

Players lose their old key assignments, and the release notes say so (H11). `ToggleCharacter` from an insecure CLICK binding in combat must be checked in the client (O8).

### 4.5 Launcher (H6, H20, Q1)

```lua
NS.Launcher = Launcher:New({
  name = addonName, icon = NS.Constants.LOGO_128, label = NS.BRAND,             -- the plain brand, never L (launcher-§1)
  minimap = function() return NS.db and NS.db.global and NS.db.global.minimap or nil end,
  openSettings   = function() NS.OpenOptionsPanel() end,                 -- left-click, always (launcher-§2)
  openSkinPicker = function() if NS.RunSkinVerb then NS.RunSkinVerb() end end,
  isEnabled = function() return not NS.IsDisabled() end, setEnabled = function(on) NS.RunVerb(on and "enable" or "disable") end,
  isLocked = function() return NS.GetSetting("locked") == true end, toggleLock = function() NS.RunVerb(NS.GetSetting("locked") and "unlock" or "lock") end,
  isTestMode = function() return NS.Preview ~= nil and NS.Preview.IsActive() end, toggleTestMode = function() NS.Preview.Toggle() end,
  isWindowShown = function() return NS.Dock ~= nil and NS.Dock.IsShown() end, toggleWindow = function() NS.Dock.Toggle() end,
  onTooltipShow = function(tt) tt:AddLine(L["Current outfit: %s"]:format(NS.Stack.CurrentLabel())) end,
  version = function() return NS.Version() end, print = function(l) NS.Print(l) end,
  debug = function(t, m) NS.Debug(t, "%s", m) end, debugAtEnable = function(t, m) NS.DebugAtEnable(t, "%s", m) end })
```

- **Static icon and label** (launcher-§1). There is no host `OnClick`, no host menu and no LDB `OnTooltipShow` (anti-patterns #81, #89).
- **Library right-click menu:** Enabled, Locked, Test mode, Show window, Open settings, Open skin selector. There is no Sim row (H18, Q4, Q5).
- **Retired:**
  - `OutfitterMinimapButton.lua`, its quick-switch outfit menu, and `LibWithFreeDragDBIcon`
  - `Outfitter_SetShowMinimapButton`, `cMinimapButtonTitle`/`Description`, and the TOC `AddonCompartmentFunc`

  Quick switching now lives on the outfit bar, the window, `/of wear` and the wear bindings. The Version History says so.
- **Degrade stub:** as DR `LauncherSetup.lua:58-80`.

### 4.6 Locales (H8, C21)

**Load order:** `libs → locales → core`.

| File | Contents |
|---|---|
| `enUS.lua` (~480) | the key-returning metatable and `NS.L`. The key is the English string. Core, settings, slash, engine messages. |
| `enUS_Triggers.lua` (~420) | trigger names, descriptions, param labels, categories, zone names |
| `enUS_Window.lua` (~380) | dock, list, menu, dialogs, bar, tooltips, test mode |
| `deDE frFR zhCN zhTW koKR ruRU` (≤ 450 each) | open with `if GetLocale() ~= "xxXX" then return end` |

- No esES file exists today, so none ships. The esES translators stay credited (INV §15).

**Re-key** (`tools/rekey-locales.lua`, run in P4.28):
1. Read `OutfitterStrings.lua` with `git show 7ea3dc3:<file>` into a `cFoo → English` map (344 assignments).
2. Apply **Drop**, **Re-home** and **Rewrite**.
3. Emit `L["<English>"] = "<translation>"` for each surviving key per overlay. Translations of rewritten English are discarded and fall back to English.
4. Unfold table keys: `cScriptCategoryName.PVP` becomes `L["PvP"]`.
5. Write the kept, dropped and orphaned report into the commit/PR text, not into a file.

**Dropped:**
- Retired features (Argent Dawn, City, Low Health, Has Debuff, CHAMP*, Void Storage, TankPoints, aspects, stances, FuBar)
- The scripting surface (H1: editor title, `Typing…`, `Error at line %d`, `cExtractErrorFormat`, `Custom…`, `Source`, `Events:`, `Script:`, `cInsertFormat`)
- The About/Options tab strings, `cShowMinimapButton`, `cAutoSwitch`, `cCantReloadUI`, `cCantSetIcon`, `cMinimapButtonTitle`, `cOpenOutfitter`, every `BINDING_NAME_OUTFITTER_*`
- The MC2 `{{, %s}}` list formats, replaced by `L["%s and %s"]` plus a host joiner
- `cVersion`/`cTitleVersion`

**Re-homed (localization-§4):**

| Old key | Replacement |
|---|---|
| `cRequiresPrefix` | `ITEM_MIN_LEVEL`/`ITEM_REQ_SKILL` via `ConvertFormatStringToSearchPattern`, then the red-line fallback (B14) |
| `cUniqueEquippedSearchPattern` | `ITEM_LIMIT_CATEGORY_MULTIPLE`/`ITEM_UNIQUE_EQUIPPABLE` |
| `cUseTooltipLineFormat` | `ITEM_SPELL_TRIGGER_ONUSE`; `HasUseEffect` reads `C_Item.GetItemSpell` |
| `cUseDurationTooltipLineFormat*` | stay in `L`, under deviation row **D8** (written in C16; O15) |
| `cMountSpeedFormat`/`cFlyingMountSpeedFormat` | dropped: `IsMounted()` |
| class names | `LOCALIZED_CLASS_NAMES_MALE/_FEMALE` (the `UnitSex == 3` rule, INV §7.7) |
| slot names | `HEADSLOT`… GlobalStrings. "First finger", "Second finger", "First trinket" and "Second trinket" stay in `L`. |
| 31 per-map BG/arena names + descriptions | `L["Battleground: %s"]`, `L["Arena: %s"]` and `L["Equips the outfit when you're in %s"]` over `GetRealZoneText(mapID)` (62 keys gone) |
| `cSilverwingHold`/`cWarsongLumberMill` | dropped: zone tables are ID-only |
| LibBabble `LBI.Herbalism/Mining/Cooking` | `C_TradeSkillUI.GetTradeSkillDisplayName(skillLine)` |

**Rewritten English (translations lost):**
- `cAutoSwitch` → "Switch outfits automatically"
- `cEquipOutfitMessageFormat` → "%s equipped" (UIErrorsFrame bare form)
- `cConfirmResetMsg` → the Delete-all text
- `cTooltipInfo` → "Show which outfits use an item"
- `cChangeIcon` → "Change icon…"
- Title Case → sentence case (options-ui-§21)

There are about 160 new keys.

**Gates (`test_locale`):**
- the enUS file set is **read from the TOC** (every `locales\enUS*.lua` line), never a hand list (localization-§6)
- the union is loaded raw into one namespace, and a key defined in more than one enUS file fails (localization-§6, anti-patterns #94)
- each enUS topic line carries its `LOAD-BEARING` comment
- every `L["…"]` read in code exists in the enUS union
- no dead enUS keys
- each overlay guards its own locale and holds only enUS keys
- no retired strings
- re-keyed coverage per locale ≥ `fixtures/legacy/locale_keys.lua` minus retired keys
- `NS.L` is never passed as a descriptor `L` (BP §4)
- kit `test_prose` over tracked prose, translations exempt (localization-§5)

**Font fallback:** whether LibKa0s-Skin falls back to the client's locale font for zhCN, zhTW, koKR and ruRU must be checked in the client. If it does not, conditional row D7 applies.

### 4.7 Legacy coexistence (C36; owner ruling 2026-11-03: warn only)

At `PLAYER_LOGIN`, if `C_AddOns.IsAddOnLoaded("Outfitter")`:
- Print once: `L["The old Outfitter addon is also loaded. Disable it: both change your gear and both own /outfitter."]`
- Set `NS.State.legacyLoaded = true` (read by `/of diagnostics` only).
- Send `LEGACY_DETECTED` from `NS.OnLoadPassDone()` in `core/OutfitterReborn.lua`, which the first LoadPass calls when it finishes. The message therefore keeps §2.3's isolation rule (nothing dispatched before the LoadPass) and its one sender.
- **No hold.** The owner ruled warn-only: triggers bind and run normally, so both engines may change gear until the player disables the old addon. The FAQ and Version History say so.
- `/of diagnostics` reports that the legacy addon is loaded.
