- Last-registration-wins on `/outfitter` is documented in the FAQ and `known-limitations.md`.

---

## 5. Engine modules (INV §3.1–§3.7, §3.10)

**Conventions:**
- `NS.<Module> = M`.
- Every module that owns events, timers or pending work exposes `M.Suspend()`/`M.Resume()`, listed in Lifecycle `MODULES` (§5.12). The AceAddon object itself owns nothing but the host handlers; `standDown` step 1 covers it.
- Game events use a module-local `events = LibStub("AceEvent-3.0"):Embed({})`, registered through `NS.SafeRegisterEvent(events, ev, fn, NS.rejectedEvents)` (DR `modules/Hud.lua:721-724`). `Suspend` calls `events:UnregisterAllEvents()`.
- Bus listeners use `NS.NewBusTarget()`.
- Timers are AceTimer handles held in the module and cancelled in `Suspend`.
- **No engine module names a client global.** Every read goes through `NS.Compat` (plain-or-nil, §5.13).
- Every event handler is a dispatch table `ON[event] = fn` (CCN).

**Dropped outright** (INV §18, H15):
- `cArgentDawnTrinkets`/`cStatIDItems` and the "Fishing"/ArgentDawn StatID paths
- `cCombatEquipmentSlots`/`HasCombatEquipmentSlots`
- the `_InventoryCache:Swap*` no-ops and the "expected" inventory cache
- `GetBagSlotItemName`, `FindItemInfoBy*`, `IsFullyEquipped`, `LocationsEqual`, `ConstrainScore`, `ItemContainsStats`, `SortItemsByStat`, `CalcMinNumSlotsByStat`, `_MultiStat:GetFilterStats/CompareOutfits`
- `cUniqueGemItemIDs`
- the CT_Mod `CT_oldPickupContainerItem` path (INV §17)
- the TankPoints category, `GetMountIDByName`

### 5.1 `defaults/Items.lua` (220): `NS.ItemData`

Pure data with one builder (INV §14):

| Member | Content | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `SLOT_NAMES` | the 18 slots in durability-first order: Head, Shoulder, Chest, Wrist, Hands, Waist, Legs, Feet, MainHand, SecondaryHand, Back, Neck, Shirt, Tabard, Finger0/1, Trinket0/1 | `Outfitter.lua:850-878`, Q4 |
| `SLOT_ORDER[name]` | index into `SLOT_NAMES` | |
| `SLOT_LABEL_KEYS[name]` | GlobalStrings keys, read at call time | |
| `INVTYPE_TO_SLOT[invType]` | `{ slot, meta }` | :908-935 |
| `HALF_ALT_SLOT`, `FULL_ALT_SLOT` | alternate slots | :937-952 |
| `SLOT_SWAP` | `{ Finger0Slot = "Finger1Slot", Trinket0Slot = "Trinket1Slot", MainHandSlot = "SecondaryHandSlot" }` | Equipment:701 |
| `ALIASES` | item aliases | :960-970 |
| `IGNORED_UNUSED` | items never listed as unused | :972-986 |
| `PHANTOM_IDS` | 18 ids | :625-646 |
| `CORRECTIONS` | item class overrides | :3903-3908 |
| `UNEQUIP_EXEMPT` | `{ ShirtSlot = true, TabardSlot = true }` | OutfitterOutfits.lua:198-210 |
| `ApplyFactionAliases(faction)` | the Horde lance swap `ALIASES[46106] = 46070` (INV §16 #23). `faction` is `UnitFactionGroup`'s **first** return (H10), via `NS.Compat.PlayerFaction()`. Called by `Outfits.LoadPass`. | |

### 5.2 `modules/PlayerState.lua` (180): `NS.PlayerState` (PS)

PS is the single owner of the player flags. The triggers, Bar and Preview read it and never re-register its events.

| Function | Returns / does |
|---|---|
| `PS.InCombat()`, `PS.MaybeInCombat()`, `PS.SetMaybeInCombat(bool)` | bool |
| `PS.IsDead()`, `PS.IsCasting()`, `PS.IsChanneling()` | bool |
| `PS.SetInterruptCasting(bool)` / `PS.InterruptCasting()` | the triggers' "may interrupt a cast" flag (Equipment:868) |
| `PS.CanDualWield2H()` | `class == "WARRIOR" and spec == 2` (Outfitter.lua:2249) |
| `PS.CanDualWield()` | `Compat.CanDualWield()` (B15) |
| `PS.Spec()` | the current spec index |
| `PS.Refresh()` | re-reads `InCombatLockdown()` (clearing `inCombat` and `MaybeInCombat` when out of it, the old `RegenEnabled`), death, casting and spec from current state, and sends `PLAYER_STATE_CHANGED` for each key that moved |
| `PS.Suspend()` / `PS.Resume()` | Resume calls `PS.Refresh()` |

**Events:**
- `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED/ENABLED`, `PLAYER_DEAD`, `PLAYER_ALIVE`, `PLAYER_UNGHOST`
- player-only `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT/START/STOP/INTERRUPTED/FAILED/FAILED_QUIET/SUCCEEDED/CHANNEL_START/CHANNEL_STOP` via `SafeRegisterUnitEvent`
- `PLAYER_TALENT_UPDATE` and `ACTIVE_PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED` (O10). `CHARACTER_POINTS_CHANGED` is gone.
- `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD` → `PS.Refresh()`, so a combat or death state that changed across a loading screen is re-read (INV §6.1 "RegenEnabled").

**Sends:** `PLAYER_STATE_CHANGED (key, value)`. It also caches the last `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED` spellID (plain only), which `NS.TriggerParams.CaptureLastCast()` reads.

**Calls:**
- On REGEN_ENABLED: `Equip.Batch(function() end)`. The empty bracket's close flushes the queued swaps.
- On cast or channel end: `Equip.DelayUntil(GetTime() + 0.5)`.
- On ALIVE: `Equip.Request()`.

### 5.3 `modules/ItemInfo.lua` (420): `NS.ItemInfo` (II)

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `II.ParseLink(link) → fields, name \| nil` | **The choke point** (INV §12). It calls `Secrets.PlainString(link)` first. The id comes from `NS.Item.ItemIDFromLink` and the bonus list from `NS.Item.BonusIDsFromLink`. The rest is a table-driven walk `FIELD_POS = { {"enchant",2},{"gem1",3},… }` with pattern `"(-?%d*)"`: signs are kept, an empty field reads 0 and a short link pads (B11). Fields 9 (LinkLevel) and 10 (SpecID) are not captured (Q9). `mods` is read from field 14 + n + 1 and the pairs after it (B12). |
| `II.FromLink(link)`, `II.FromBag(bag, slot)`, `II.FromSlot(slotName)` | Fill quality, level and minLevel from `Compat.ItemInfo`, invType from `Compat.ItemInfoInstant`, and `loc`, `bagType`, `azerite` (sorted). `CORRECTIONS` overrides the class. |
| `II.Bind(info) → "boe"\|"warbound"\|"bound"\|"none"` | Lazy. `Compat.IsBound(loc)`, `Compat.IsWarboundUntilEquip(loc)`, `bindType`. The fallback compares `Compat.TooltipLines(loc)` against `ITEM_BIND_ON_EQUIP`/`ITEM_ACCOUNTBOUND_UNTIL_EQUIP`. |
| `II.UniqueKeys(info) → { [key]=true } \| nil` | `Compat.ItemUniqueness(id)` (`"c<ID>"` keys). The fallback is a pattern built at call time from `ITEM_LIMIT_CATEGORY_MULTIPLE`. |
| `II.Usable(info) → bool` | `Compat.CanUseItem(id)`. The fallback is "any red tooltip line" (HSV `s>0.2, v>0.2, h<50 or h>310`) (B14). |
| `II.Exact(a, b)` | loops `EXACT_FIELDS = { "suffix","enchant","gems","uid","upType","diff","bonus","mods","azerite" }`. `diff` is included for **both** callers, which unifies `FindItem` and `ItemsAreSame`. |
| `II.SameIdentity(a, b)` | same id, or `ALIASES[a.id] == b.id`, and an equal suffix when the ids are equal |
| `II.Snapshot(info) → ItemRecord`, `II.EMPTY` | §3.3 |
| `II.UsesBothHands(item)` | `invType == "INVTYPE_2HWEAPON" and not PS.CanDualWield2H()` |
| `II.MetaSlot(item)` | Outfitter.lua:4026 |
| `II.LinkInfoLines(link) → { string }` | for `/of iteminfo`. Every field goes through `NS.SafeToString`. |

### 5.4 `modules/Inventory.lua` (460): `NS.Inventory` (Inv)

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `Inv.ScheduleSync()` | A 0.01 s debounce (cancel + reschedule). Hooked to `BAG_UPDATE`, `BAG_UPDATE_DELAYED`, `PLAYERBANKSLOTS_CHANGED` and player `UNIT_INVENTORY_CHANGED`. |
| `Inv.Sync() → any, equipped, bags` | `syncEquipped()` plus `syncBag(bag)` over `Inv.BagIDs()`. The plain link, or `""` for nil/secret (Q12), is compared with the snapshot. Changed bags are flushed. Sends `INVENTORY_CHANGED`. Perf bucket `bagSync`. |
| `Inv.Cache()` | A lazy rebuild over equipped items plus bag items whose `INVTYPE_TO_SLOT` exists and where `II.Bind ~= "boe"`. The min-level check stays disabled (Q3, Outfitter.lua:5851). |
| `Inv.Flush()` | On PEW and `GET_ITEM_INFO_RECEIVED`. There is **one** registration, on the module target (B5). |
| `Inv.BagIDs()` | Bank closed: `0 … NUM_TOTAL_EQUIPPED_BAG_SLOTS`. Bank open: plus `Compat.BankBagIDs()`. |
| `Inv.IsBankBag(bag)` | a set built at bank open, replacing the `i > NUM or i < 0` heuristic |
| `Inv.BankOpen()` | `BANKFRAME_OPENED/CLOSED` and `PLAYER_INTERACTION_MANAGER_FRAME_SHOW/HIDE` with `Compat.IsBankerInteraction` |
| `cache:BeginPass() → pass` | Claims live in `pass.claimed[info]`, never on the item (B9) |
| `cache:Find(item, pass, opts) → info\|nil, claimedDup` | `classify` → exact / partial / claimed. The first exact unclaimed match wins, else the **only** partial. |
| `cache:FindOrAlt`, `cache:FindAll`, `cache:SlotHas(slotName, item)`, `cache:Same(a, b)` | Inventory:965-1382 semantics |
| `Inv.Suspend()` / `Resume()` | unregister, cancel the debounce, flush / re-register, flush, `ScheduleSync()` |

Events are dropped while `NS.Equip.SuspendedForLoad()` is true (between `PLAYER_LEAVING_WORLD` and PEW).

### 5.5 `modules/Inventory_Lists.lua` (320): extends `NS.Inventory`

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `Inv.Missing(rec) → missing[], banked[]` | Native: `FindOrAlt` per slot, skipping phantoms and empty markers. EM: `EM.Missing`. |
| `Inv.Unused()` | Odds 'n Ends: claim every outfit's items in one pass, then take the unclaimed that are not `IGNORED_UNUSED` and not warbound |
| `Inv.BoE()`, `Inv.Warbound()` | a shared `walkBags(pred)` |
| `Inv.ItemSections() → { {key, labelKey, items} }` | Odds 'n Ends, Warbound, BoEs, each sorted by level desc, then name, code, location (for the window list, INV §7.2) |
| `Inv.OutfitsUsing(info) → ids[]` | paired slots via `cache:Same` (for the tooltips) |
| `Inv.NextCooldownItem(itemIDs, ignoreSwapCooldown) → info, seconds` | Plain cooldowns. `enabled` is normalized to bool (B19). No "Use:" counts as 30 s, and bag items have a 30 s floor unless `ignoreSwapCooldown` (Q10, INV §16 #16). A secret start is treated as 30 s. |
| `Inv.SlotItemActive(slotName) → bool` | the equipped item's use effect is running (TRINKET_QUEUE) |
| `Inv.HasUseEffect(link)` | `Compat.ItemSpell(link) ~= nil` |

### 5.6 `modules/Outfits.lua` (500): `NS.Outfits` (O)

O is the only writer of `char.outfits/order/bindings/nextID/seeded` outside the instance-row path (C12). It is the only sender of `OUTFIT_ADDED/DELETED/EDITED/RENAMED`.

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `O.Get(id)` | nil for non-`^o%d+$` ids (C28) |
| `O.Ids(category)`, `O.Each(category)`, `O.Categories()` → `{"Complete","Accessory"}` | |
| `O.FindByName(name)` | case-insensitive exact match |
| `O.Create(name, items, opts) → id` | `opts = { category?, icon?, trigger?, builtin? }`. Allocates `"o"..nextID`, categorizes, appends to the order, sends `OUTFIT_ADDED` |
| `O.CreateFromItem(link) → id` | bar drop: an outfit named after the item, holding that item |
| `O.Duplicate(id)`, `O.Reorder(category, ids)` | |
| `O.Delete(id)` | `Stack.Remove(id)` → `TriggerBinder.Unbind(id)` → unlink from the order and from `char.bindings` → `EM.DeleteSet` if `kind == "em"` → `OUTFIT_DELETED` |
| `O.DeleteAll()` | §3.6 |
| `O.Rename(id, name) → ok, errKey` | refuses a duplicate. EM goes through `EM.Rename`. Sends `OUTFIT_RENAMED`. No UNWEAR/WEAR dance (O16). |
| `O.Categorize(rec)` | the shared `IsComplete(slotHas, mainHandInvType)`: every slot but `UNEQUIP_EXEMPT`; an off-hand may be absent under a both-hands MH (B10) |
| `O.Changed(id, what)` | re-categorize, move between orders, `OUTFIT_EDITED (id, what)` |
| `O.Items(rec)` | `KIND[rec.kind].Items(rec)`: native → `rec.items`, em → `EM.Items(rec)` (replaces the two metatables) |
| `O.SetItem(id, slot, item)`, `O.ClearSlot(id, slot)` | kind dispatch. The EM variants unignore the slot and save. |
| `O.SetSlotEnabled(id, slot, on)` | enabling snapshots the equipped item. Then `Changed`. |
| `O.SetAllSlotsEnabled(id, on)` | footer All/None (Outfitter.lua:5058) |
| `O.SlotState(id, slot, cache) → "off"\|"on"\|"unknown"` | `unknown` = enabled and owned but not equipped (the checkbox tri-state) |
| `O.UnknownSlots(id, cache) → set` | the slots in state `unknown`, used by recording |
| `O.Status(id, cache) → "disabled"\|"missing"\|"banked"\|"normal"` | for the row, tooltip and bar; `"disabled"` = `rec.trigger and rec.trigger.disabled` (§3.3) |
| `O.SetToCurrent(id) → ok, msg` | snapshots the enabled slots, `Changed`, `Stack.Wear(id, {now=true})`, prints `L["Updated the outfit '%s' to currently equipped items"]`. A nil id returns `false, L["No Complete outfit is equipped."]` (INV §5 Fix). |
| `O.AddNewItems(id, items)` | Window-open recording. Skips `O.UnknownSlots(id)`. The model never reads frames. |
| `O.SetBinding(id, n\|nil)` | clears `n` from any other outfit (`char.bindings[n] = id`) |
| `O.BindingOf(id) → n\|nil`, `O.BoundTo(n) → id\|nil` | |
| `O.SetFlag(id, key, value)` | allow-list `unequipOthers, ignoreComparisons, titleID` → `Changed(id, key)`. `icon`, `bar.hide` and the trigger flags are instance rows (§3.5). |
| `O.UsingItem(link) → ids[]` | `Inv.OutfitsUsing` wrapper |
| `O.IsEM(id)` | |
| `O.LoadPass()` | §5.7 |

Trigger id, flags and params are written through the Schema instance rows (§3.5), and the announce routes into `O.Changed`.

### 5.7 `modules/Outfits_Seed.lua` (170): LoadPass

`O.LoadPass()` runs from a 1 s AceTimer after the **first** `PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`, and from `DeleteAll` (C32).

**Repair** (always):
- prune `recent` of missing ids and duplicates (Outfitter.lua:5334-5345)
- drop `stack` entries and `bindings` that point at missing ids
- re-assert `nextID`, re-categorize every record
- `NS.ItemData.ApplyFactionAliases(...)`

**Seed** (when `char.seeded ~= true`), in INV §3.1 order:
1. `EM.Sync()`.
2. **Normal** from the current gear (`builtin = "NORMAL"`), set as the stack `{ {id = normal} }`.
3. **Naked**: every slot `II.EMPTY` (`builtin = "NAKED"`).
4. The class outfits from `SEED_CLASS[class] = { { nameKey, triggerID } }`:
   - DRUID: Caster `DRUID_CASTER`, Bear `DRUID_BEAR`, Cat `DRUID_CAT`, Travel `DRUID_TRAVEL`, Moonkin `DRUID_MOONKIN`, Tree `DRUID_TREE`, Prowl `DRUID_PROWL`
   - ROGUE: Stealth `ROGUE_STEALTH`
   - SHAMAN: Ghost Wolf `SHAMAN_GHOST_WOLF`

   (Outfitter.lua:1058-1107)
5. One outfit per spec, named `"<Localized Class>: <Spec>"`, with trigger `SPEC_n` and the spec icon (Outfitter.lua:5776-5800).
6. `seeded = true`.

Profession smart outfits stay dead (INV §18).

Then `NS.Stack.Init()` and `NS.TriggerBinder.BindAll()` run. The **first** LoadPass of the session ends with `NS.OnLoadPassDone()` (`core/OutfitterReborn.lua`, §4.7).

The 1 s timer is `O.loadTimer`, owned by `NS.Outfits`: `O.Suspend()` cancels it and `O.Resume()` reschedules it if the first PEW has been seen and the first LoadPass has not run (§5.12).

### 5.8 `modules/OutfitStack.lua` (460): `NS.Stack` (St)

St owns `char.stack`, `char.recent`, the session `selectedId`, the expected outfit, and the runtime table `rt[id] = { didEquip, didUnequip }` (C31).

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `St.Wear(id, opts)` | `opts = { layer, byTrigger, now, hw, clearDisabled }`. Runs `Equip.Batch(wearBody, id, opts)` (or `BatchNow` when `now`). **wearBody**:<br>1. `clearDisabled` and `rec.trigger and rec.trigger.disabled` → `NS.SetByPath("outfit.trigger.disabled", false, id)`, which re-enables and (through `OUTFIT_EDITED`) rebinds the trigger<br>2. a Complete outfit clears the stack<br>3. `unequipOthers` clears the Accessory entries (Outfitter.lua:3424-3428)<br>4. `pushEntry(id, layer)`: an existing entry is removed first (`OUTFIT_REMOVED`), then `collapseTemps()`<br>5. a Complete outfit moves to the end of `recent`<br>6. window open: select if topmost, else clear (Outfitter.lua:3446-3456, comment kept)<br>7. `rt[id].didEquip = byTrigger`<br>8. `Equip.Request()`<br>9. `OUTFIT_WORN (id, layer, byTrigger)`<br>10. `if opts.hw then St.UpdateTitle(true) end` (INV §3.2, §6.4: the title follows every hardware-event wear) |
| `St.Remove(id, opts)` | `opts = { byTrigger, now, hw, clearDisabled }`. `clearDisabled` re-enables the trigger as wearBody step 1 does (a key press clears `Disabled` before it decides wear or remove, Outfitter.lua:3741-3743). Remove the entry, `collapseTemps()`, move a Complete outfit to the **front** of `recent`, clear the selection if it was this one, `rt[id].didUnequip = byTrigger`, `Equip.Request()`, `OUTFIT_REMOVED`, then `if opts.hw then St.UpdateTitle(true) end`. Removing a Complete outfit runs `wearFallbackComplete()`: walk `recent` from newest, **bounded** by `#recent`, pruning stale ids (Outfitter.lua:3519-3545); the fallback wear carries the remove's `hw`, so the title follows the outfit put back on (the legacy `HasHWEvent` spans the fallback). The HACK comment is kept verbatim (3469-3477). |
| `St.Toggle(id, opts) → worn` | `ToggleOutfit` (Outfitter.lua:3549-3567): a worn outfit is removed, a Complete one too (the fallback walk puts the previous one back, as `/of toggle` did); otherwise worn. The re-wear of a Complete outfit is the bar's and the key binding's own rule, at their call sites (OutfitterBar.lua:783-788, `St.WearBound`). |
| `St.WearBound(n) → worn, rec` | the anti-panic logic (§4.4); every wear or remove it makes passes `hw = true, clearDisabled = true` |
| `St.IsWorn(id)`, `St.Top()`, `St.CurrentComplete()` | |
| `St.CurrentInfo() → name, id\|nil` | `""` for empty, `L["Custom"]` for a temp top (Equipment:1301) |
| `St.CurrentLabel()` | `""` → `L["None"]` |
| `St.TagLayer(id, layer)` | Outfitter.lua:1277 |
| `insertIndexFor(layer)` (local) | the lowest entry tagged `layer`, else the top. Derived, never stored (B6). |
| `St.Compile(excludeId) → items` | the inventory base, then each entry's `O.Items`. Off-hand → `II.EMPTY` under a both-hands MH (`AdjustOffhandSlot`). |
| `St.Expected()`, `St.SetExpected(items)` | session |
| `St.Select(id\|nil)`, `St.Selected()` | sends `SELECTION_CHANGED`; `Selected()` answers nil once the record is gone. `O.Delete` clears the selection when it holds the deleted id (after `St.Remove`, whose fallback can re-select it; Outfitter.lua:5101-5105). |
| `St.UpdateTitle(hw)` | the highest stacked `titleID` → `Compat.SetCurrentTitle`, only when `hw` (INV §13). Callers: wearBody step 10, removeBody, `/of updatetitle`, the title menu's 0.5 s re-apply. Trigger wears carry no `hw`, so they never change the title (as today). |
| `St.DidEquip(id)`, `St.DidUnequip(id)` | smart-unequip reads |
| `St.Clear()`, `St.Init()` | `Init` restores: drop unknown ids, keep temps whole, then `SetExpected(Compile())`, then the worn gear the expected outfit does not hold becomes the temporary outfit and the expected outfit takes it (`UpdateTemporaryOutfit(GetNewItemsOutfit(ExpectedOutfit))`, OutfitterEquipment.lua:1010-1012) |
| `St.Suspend()` / `Resume()` | recompute expected. Resume does not force an equip. |

CCN: `wearBody` is about 10 and `removeBody` about 11.

### 5.9 `modules/OutfitStack_Watch.lua` (300)

Listens to `INVENTORY_CHANGED`. `W.OnInventoryChanged(equippedChanged)` runs inside `Equip.Batch` (Outfitter.lua:1938-1983):
1. `newItems = diffAgainst(previousCurrent)`, skipping the off-hand under a both-hands main hand.
2. `subtract(newItems, St.Expected())`.
3. If anything is left: with a selection and the window open (`NS.Dock.IsShown()`), call `O.AddNewItems(sel, newItems)` and print `L["Adding %s to %s outfit"]`. Otherwise call `updateTemp(newItems)`.
4. If `not Equip.Pending()` and `sameOutfit(current, St.Expected())`, send `SWAP_COMPLETE`.

`updateTemp` (Outfitter.lua:4481-4536) merges into the top temp entry or pushes a new one, then subtracts and prunes it.

`sameOutfit`/`subtract` work over `cache:Same` with `FULL_ALT_SLOT`.

`GetInventoryOutfit`'s 15-field compare becomes `II.Exact` plus an id check, which fixes the `UpgradeItemID` typo (INV §19).

### 5.10 `modules/Equip.lua` (440): `NS.Equip` (E)

**State** (session): `depth`, `needsUpdate`, `lastUpdate`, `delayUntil`, `timer`, `lastError = { tag, err, at }`, `sfxSaved`, `pendingNow`, `suspendedForLoad`.

**The one bracket.** There is no public Begin/End:

```lua
function E.Batch(fn, ...)
  depth = depth + 1
  -- Lua 5.1's xpcall forwards no extra arguments (`xpcall(f, h, 42)` calls f()), and the kit runs 5.1,
  -- so the arguments travel in a closure (the kit's own form, LK testkit/mock_ace.lua:579).
  local n, args = select("#", ...), { ... }
  local ok, err = xpcall(function() return fn(unpack(args, 1, n)) end, traceback)
  depth = depth - 1
  if depth < 0 then depth = 0 end                       -- unmatched-close guard kept (Equipment:825-836)
  if depth == 0 then
    local fok, ferr = xpcall(flush, traceback, pendingNow); pendingNow = false
    if not fok then surface("flush", ferr) end
  end
  if not ok then surface("batch", err) end
  return ok, err
end
-- E.BatchNow(fn, ...): same, and sets pendingNow = true (replaces EndEquipmentUpdate(nil, true)).
```

- `surface(tag, err)` records `lastError`, writes the trace to DebugLog, prints one tagged line per tag per session, and then calls `geterrorhandler()(err)` **after** the counter is restored.
- `flush(now)` runs `E.Update()` (now) or `scheduleUpdate()`, then sends `STACK_CHANGED`.
- Cross-slice users: the triggers, Bank and Watch call `NS.Equip.Batch(fn)`.

**Belt and braces, so the count can never stick:**
- `E.Suspend()` zeroes `depth`/`needsUpdate`, clears `suspendedForLoad` (a PEW missed while stood down could never clear it), cancels the timer and restores SFX (H26).
- `E.Resume()` zeroes `depth`.
- PEW (0.05 s debounce, INV §6.1) zeroes a stranded depth **before** opening its own Batch, and records `surface("pew", "stranded depth N")`. The Batch then clears the cast flags and runs `Inv.Flush()`, `Inv.ScheduleSync()` and `EM.Sync()` (INV §16 #4). `PS.Refresh()` and the States aura scan, form poll and zone schedule run from their own PEW handlers (§5.2, §6.10), which together cover INV §6.1's "RegenEnabled; UpdateAuraStates; ScheduleUpdateZone".
- `PLAYER_LEAVING_WORLD` sets `suspendedForLoad`.

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `E.Request()` | `needsUpdate = true`; at depth 0, `scheduleUpdate()` |
| `E.Pending()`, `E.DelayUntil(t)`, `E.SuspendedForLoad()` | |
| `E.SetUpdateDelay(start, delay)` | `E.DelayUntil(start + delay - 1.5)` (Outfitter.lua:7536-7542; C16) |
| `E.Update()` | Perf bucket `equipUpdate`. An ordered `GATES` list `{name, pred, action}`, where the first match decides:<br>`idle` (not needed) → return<br>`dead` → wait<br>`casting` (and not `PS.InterruptCasting()`) → wait<br>`delay` (`GetTime() < delayUntil`) → reschedule<br>`throttle` (< 1.5 s since last) → reschedule<br>`combat` (`PS.InCombat() or PS.MaybeInCombat() or Compat.InCombatLockdown()`) → keep needed, clear MaybeInCombat, reschedule (Equipment:897-907)<br>Then `compiled = St.Compile()`, `changes = Changes.Build(compiled, Inv.Cache(), ctx)`, `if #changes > 0 then E.Execute(changes, Moves.EmptyBagSlots()) end`, `St.SetExpected(compiled)`, `PS.SetInterruptCasting(false)`. |
| `scheduleUpdate()` | `max(0.05, 1.5 - elapsed)` (Equipment:967-985) |
| `E.Execute(changes, slots, fullErrKey)` | Perf bucket `equipExecute` (within `equipUpdate`). Only when `#changes > 0` (B3). If `NS.GetSetting("equip.muteSounds")` and the plain `Compat.GetCVar("Sound_EnableSFX")` is known, save it and set `"0"`. Then `pcall(runChanges…)`, and **always** `Compat.ClearCursor()` followed by the SFX restore (B8, INV §16 #13). An error is re-raised into `surface("execute")`. |
| `executeChange`, `needsEmptyThenEquip` | Equipment:477-528; specialty-bag bit-and via `Compat.BitAnd` |
| `E.ShowEquipError(item, claimedDup, slot)` | gated on `equip.showErrors`. Prints `L["Can't put %s in the %s slot because it's already being used in another slot"]` or `L["Can't find item %s for slot %s"]`. |
| `E.Diagnostic() → lines` | `depth`, `needsUpdate`, the time to the next update, `lastError` (tag and age), and the tripwire `L["Equipment updates are STUCK (count %d) -- reload to clear"]` when `depth > 0` outside a Batch |

### 5.11 `modules/Equip_Changes.lua` (480) and `modules/Equip_Moves.lua` (260)

**`NS.Changes.Build(compiled, cache, ctx) → changes`** is pure. `ctx = { slotLink, canDW2H, phantom, showError }`.
1. `pass = cache:BeginPass()`. Slots already holding their target are removed from a **working copy**, and their item is claimed (Equipment:28-48).
2. **TGFix:** when not `canDW2H` and the OH holds a 2H, queue `{ slot = OH, tgFix = true }` (Equipment:50-74).
3. `addSlot(slot)` in `SLOT_NAMES` order, keeping the durability comment (Equipment:76-80). Phantoms are skipped. An empty marker is queued only if the slot is occupied. Otherwise `FindOrAlt(…, { claim = true })` runs, `showError` fires on a miss, and `unique` deltas and `meta` are set.
4. `Unique.Order(changes)`.
5. `Opt.Apply(changes)`.

**Change shape:**

```lua
{ slot = "MainHandSlot", slotID = 16, itemName = "…", to = loc|nil, from = loc|nil,
  meta = "TwoHandSlot"|…, tgFix = bool, done = bool, unique = { [key] = delta }, rank = 1|2|3, rank2 = n }
```

**`Unique.Order`** (Q7, Equipment:165-250, 558-644):
- `rankOf(change, offsets)`: 1 = no net increase, 2 = mixed, 3 = increase only.
- At most 20 passes, sorted by `(rank, rank2)`, with an early stop when counts repeat.
- Split into `rankOf`, `onRank1/2/3` and a driver.
- Deltas: −1 per key of the outgoing item, +1 per key of the incoming item.

**`Opt.Apply`** is the intended optimize, now running.
- **B1:** `findSlot(changes, slot)` uses `c and c.slot == slot`.
- `classify(c)` → `done`, `tgfix`, `offhand`, `twohand`, `swap` or nil.
- `RULES[kind]`:
  - `tgfix`: move to index 1.
  - `offhand`: an equipping OH goes after the MH; an emptying OH goes before it (the Argent Lance fix).
  - `twohand`: drop the OH change and insert **one** empty-OH change, marked `done` (B2).
  - `swap`: `fixSwap` with `moveOneToTwo`, `emptyOneForTwo`, `moveTwoToOne`, `emptyTwoForOne`. One spelling, which fixes the `fixSlotSwapChange` case bug.
- The loop is bounded: `guard < 64`.
- No classes (B4).
- `NS.Changes.ForMoves(items, cache, filter)` builds the deposit/withdraw lists.

**`NS.Moves`:**

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `Moves.Pickup(loc)` | `Compat.PickupContainerItem` / `Compat.PickupInventoryItem` |
| `Moves.ToEmpty(loc, slots, errKey) → ok` | `pickTarget`: specialty bag first, then general. On failure prints `L[errKey]` with `NS.SafeToString(link)`: `"Can't remove %s because all bags are full"`, `cWithdrawBagsFullError`, or the bank-full key. |
| `Moves.EmptyBagSlots()`, `Moves.EmptyBankSlots()` | one bounded walk each (Outfitter.lua:4120-4195) |
| `Moves.UnequipByName(arg) → ok, msg` | Numeric → slot id. A name matches case-insensitively against the plain `II.FromSlot(slot).name`. **Returns** after `"Couldn't find an item named %s"` (INV §4, §19). |

### 5.12 Lifecycle and total disable (H26; `core/LifecycleSetup.lua`)

The latch is copied from BP §6.16:
- Two holds: `HOLD_DISABLED` (persisted via `enabled`) and `HOLD_PERF` (session).
- `NS.ApplyEnabled`, `NS.SyncEnabledHold`, `NS.IsStoodDown`, `NS.IsDisabled` and `NS.StoodDownRefusal` are as in BP. `StoodDownRefusal` is the one refusal helper (slash `feature()`, all 14 binding handlers, launcher toggles, Delete-all).

```lua
-- Stand-down runs top to bottom; stand-up runs bottom to top. Jobs that want to equip cancel before the engine resets;
-- States.Resume runs before TriggerBinder.BindAll on the way up.
local MODULES = { "Preview", "TriggerBinder", "States", "Optimize", "Companion", "EM", "Equip", "Stack",
                  "Outfits", "Inventory", "PlayerState", "Stats", "Tooltips", "Compare", "QuickSlots", "Bar",
                  "Dock", "SlotEnables", "Dialogs", "IconChooser" }
```

`standDown()`:
1. `addon:UnregisterAllEvents/Messages`, `CancelAllTimers`, `NS.BusStandDown()`.
2. `NS[m].Suspend()` for each module (a nil module is tolerated).
3. `NS.HideSkinPicker()`.

`standUp()`: `NS.BusStandUp()`, `NS.RegisterGameEvents()`, then each `Resume()` in reverse. It rebuilds from the current settings.

| Module | `Suspend` duty |
|---|---|
| Preview | ends silently |
| TriggerBinder | cancels the coalesced-rebind timer and clears `dirty`; `UnbindAll()`: per-target `UnregisterAllEvents/Messages`, `CancelAllTimers`, and each active descriptor's `onSuspend(ctx)` restores CVars it changed. Never equips. |
| States | unregister everything, cancel all timers |
| Optimize | cancels the job with `onDone(nil, "stood-down")` |
| Companion | cancels the ticker, `want = nil` |
| EM | drops `pendingSave` and its timer, `saving = 0` |
| Equip | `depth = 0`, `needsUpdate = false`, cancels the timer, **restores SFX** |
| Stack | recompute expected |
| Outfits | cancels the pending 1 s LoadPass timer (`O.loadTimer`); Resume reschedules it only if the first LoadPass has not run |
| Inventory | unregister, cancel the debounce, flush |
| PlayerState | unregister |
| Stats (`ItemStats_Sources`) | unregisters its `ADDON_LOADED` watch for Pawn/WeightsWatcher; Resume re-registers it and re-runs `Src.Install()` (guarded by `installed[name]`) |
| Tooltips, Compare, QuickSlots | hooks stay installed; bodies gate on `NS.IsStoodDown()` |
| Bar | hide |
| Dock | out of combat: disarm secure rows and hide. In combat: already hidden (§7.2); queue `"dock.hide"`. In both cases it hides `OutfitterRebornSheetButton` (insecure, so legal in combat). Resume re-shows the button; the `PaperDollFrame` OnShow hook body re-shows it only when not stood down. |
| SlotEnables | hide |
| Dialogs, IconChooser | `CloseAll("standdown")` / `Close("standdown")` |

**Hooks that cannot be unhooked, each gated in its body:**
- the `TooltipDataProcessor` post-call
- `GameTooltip_ShowCompareItem`
- `C_PetJournal.SummonPetByGUID`
- `PaperDollFrame` OnShow/OnHide (OnShow is also the sheet button's re-show path, latch-gated)
- the `Character<Slot>` Pre/PostClick
- the flyout `getItemsFunc`/`postGetItemsFunc`
- `GameTooltip` OnShow/OnHide (States)

**Survives a stand-down** (slash-commands-§7): both chat commands, the dispatcher, `NS.COMMANDS`, the settings categories, AceDB with callbacks, and the launcher. No SV writes happen while disabled except the Enabled row.

### 5.13 Compat accessors, EM mirror and Bank

**`core/Compat.lua` accessors** (added in P4.2; all plain-or-nil; each reaches `NS.Secrets` at call time):

| Accessor | Secret treatment | Consumer on nil |
|---|---|---|
| `ContainerItemLink(bag, slot)`, `InventoryItemLink(slotID)` | `PlainString` | empty slot (Q12) |
| `ItemInfo(link)` → name, quality, level, minLevel, bindType | each field `Plain*` | unknown; no filter |
| `ItemInfoInstant(id)` | plain by construction | not equippable |
| `DetailedItemLevel(link)`, `ItemStats(link)` | `PlainNumber` | 0 / absent |
| `ContainerItemCooldown`, `InventoryItemCooldown` | `PlainNumber`; enabled normalized | 30 s conservative |
| `UnitLevel("player")` | `PlainNumber` | don't filter |
| `TooltipLines(loc)` | `leftText` `PlainString`, colours `PlainNumber` | line skipped |
| `SummonedPetGUID()`, `PetGUIDByName(lower)`, `PetGUIDBySpecies(id)` | `PlainString`; compares run inside Compat | not found |
| `EMSetIDs/Info/Locations/IgnoredSlots`, `EMLocation(loc)` | per element via `SafeIterate` (≤ 512, no `#`) | the zero-sets path (preserve, don't delete) |
| `GetCVar("Sound_EnableSFX")` | `PlainString` | don't touch SFX |
| `KnownTitles() → { {id, name} }` | walks `GetNumTitles()` with `IsTitleKnown(i)`; each name through `PlainString`, a secret or nil name skipped; sorted by a plain `name:lower()` compare inside Compat (fixes INV §12 "`GetSortedTitles` compares") | empty list: the submenu shows None only |
| `TooltipInventoryUnit(tooltip) → unit\|"?"\|nil` | `tooltip:GetPrimaryTooltipInfo()`; when `getterName == "GetInventoryItem"`, the plain first getter arg; nil for any other getter or no info | `"?"` (not the player: no line) when the info, the getter name or the unit cannot be read; client check O41 |
| `ItemUniqueness`, `IsBound`, `IsWarboundUntilEquip`, `CanUseItem`, `ItemSpell`, `ItemFamily`, `AzeritePowers`, `BankBagIDs`, `IsBankerInteraction`, `PickupContainerItem`, `PickupInventoryItem`, `PickupItemLocation`, `UseContainerItem`, `EquipCursorItem`, `ClearCursor`, `ContainerNumFreeSlots`, `SetCurrentTitle`, `EMCreateSet/SaveSet/ModifySet/DeleteSet/SetID/NumSets/MaxSets/ClearIgnored/IgnoreSlot`, `HookPetSummon(fn)`, `HookCompare(fn)`, `MacroIcons(kind)`, `FlyoutLocationData(loc)`, `PawnVisibleScales()`, `PawnItemValue(link, scale)`, `CanUpgradeItem(loc)` | as applicable | |

Five client APIs need a client check before the ladders are final (O17): `C_Item.GetItemUniquenessByID`, `C_Item.IsBoundToAccountUntilEquip`, `C_PlayerInfo.CanUseItem`, the `C_Item.GetItemStats` keys, and `C_AzeriteEmpoweredItem` on 12.x. Each has its tooltip fallback.

**`modules/EquipmentManager.lua` (440): `NS.EM`.** State: `saving`, `ignoreUntil`, `pendingSave`.

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `EM.Sync()` | On `EQUIPMENT_SETS_CHANGED` and PEW. Returns if `saving > 0` or `GetTime() < ignoreUntil` (INV §16 #14). Then:<br>1. `markUnused()`<br>2. `ids = Compat.EMSetIDs()`. **Zero ids** → `preserveAll()` into `char.emPreserved`; nothing is deleted (Q8).<br>3. `adoptSet(setID)`: match by name case-insensitively, else restore from `emPreserved` (then `TriggerBinder.Bind`), else create a stub `{ kind="em", setID, name, items={} }`<br>4. `pruneUnused()` (only with ids > 0; `TriggerBinder.Unbind`, the set is not deleted)<br>5. `emPreserved = nil` when ids > 0<br>6. `EM_SYNCED` |
| `EM.Items(rec)` | `pendingSave` items, or locations + ignored slots → `resolveSlot`. A bag-located bank tab is reclassified via `Inv.IsBankBag`. Snapshots are written back via `II.Snapshot`. |
| `EM.Missing(rec)` | OutfitterOutfits.lua:1033-1069 |
| `EM.Save(rec, icon)` | `withSaving(fn)`: a pcall-balanced counter, then `ignoreUntil = now + 0.5` (O18) |
| `EM.SetSlotsFromItems(items)` | the enabled slots come from items, not checkboxes |
| `EM.StoreOnServer(id) → ok, popupKey` | Refuses at the max with `"OUTFITTERREBORN_EM_FULL"`. The name is cut to 31 bytes on a UTF-8 boundary (`truncateUTF8`). The icon is `rec.icon or 134400`. **B17:** it copies `trigger`, `triggerParams`, `triggerState`, `stat`, `unequipOthers`, `titleID`, `bar`, the binding (`O.SetBinding`) and `ignoreComparisons`. Then it adds, wears, selects, and deletes the native outfit. Reached from the row's EM checkbox (§7.3). |
| `EM.StoreLocally(id) → newId` | clones the set into a native outfit with the same copy list (INV §3.5), then deletes the EM mirror record (not the Blizzard set). Reached by unchecking the row's EM checkbox (§7.3). |
| `EM.Rename(rec, name)`, `EM.Icon/SetIcon` (134400 ↔ nil), `EM.DeleteSet(rec)` | |
| `EM.SetItems(rec, items)` | the rebuild path: `pendingSave` with a 10 s cancel, `St.Wear`, and a save on `SWAP_COMPLETE` with `L["Saved outfit to Equipment Manager"]` |

**`modules/Bank.lua` (260): `NS.Bank`.** Every operation refuses with `L["The bank must be open"]` unless `Inv.BankOpen()`, refuses in combat, runs inside `Equip.Batch`, and ends with `O.Changed(id, "bank")`.

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `Bank.IsOpen()` | `Inv.BankOpen()` |
| `Bank.Deposit(id, uniqueOnly)` | `St.Select(nil)` first (INV §3.7). Unique subtracts the items of other outfits with no banked items. `Changes.ForMoves`, then `Equip.Execute(changes, Moves.EmptyBankSlots(), "bankFull")`. |
| `Bank.DepositOthers(id)` | Outfitter.lua:6985-7030 |
| `Bank.Withdraw(id)` | Outfitter.lua:7032-7068 |
| `Bank.WithdrawOthers(id)` | **Implemented**, replacing the undefined `ExecuteEquipmentChangeList2` and `vExpectedInventoryCache` (INV §3.7 Fix) |
| `Bank.MissingReport() → lines` | |

The warband bank is not scanned. It is documented in `known-limitations.md` (INV §3.4 Decide default; O19).

### 5.14 Item stats and the optimizer (H15, H16)

**`modules/ItemStats.lua` (360): `NS.Stats`.**
- `Categories()` and `RegisterCategory(cat)` (sends `STATS_CHANGED`). `RegisterCategory` is private to the addon (the Pawn/WW sources use it). Third-party categories through `GetNumStats`/`GetIndexedStat` (INV §10.2) are **dropped** with the rest of the `Outfitter` global (H3), and the aliases are not kept (§5.16).
- `Get(statID)`, `ConfigName(cfg)`, `stat:Score(info, job)` (cached on `job.scores`, weak).
- `ItemStats(link)` maps `Compat.ItemStats` through `STAT_KEYS`:
  - STA `ITEM_MOD_STAMINA_SHORT`, STR `ITEM_MOD_STRENGTH_SHORT`, INT `ITEM_MOD_INTELLECT_SHORT`, AGI `ITEM_MOD_AGILITY_SHORT`, SPI `ITEM_MOD_SPIRIT_SHORT`
  - HAS `ITEM_MOD_HASTE_RATING_SHORT`, VER `ITEM_MOD_VERSATILITY`, MAS `ITEM_MOD_MASTERY_RATING_SHORT`, CRI `ITEM_MOD_CRIT_RATING_SHORT`
  - alias lists for the combined primaries (O20)
  - labels are `_G[key]` (B13)
- `ITEM_LEVEL` is `Compat.DetailedItemLevel`, read whether or not `GetItemStats` answers. `TOTAL_STATS` sums the nine. An item the client has no stats for yet scores 0 on them and stays a candidate (the legacy `GetUncachedItemScore(item) or 0`, `OutfitterItemStats.lua:148`); only an unreadable link or the level filter drops it. The category label is `_G.PLAYERSTAT_BASE_STATS` (the legacy `Name`), with `L["Base stats"]` as its fallback.
- Level filter: `minLevel > PlainNumber(Compat.UnitLevel("player"))` drops the item, and a nil level lets it through.
- `OutfitScore` and `MultiScore`.

**`modules/ItemStats_Sources.lua` (220).**
- `Src.Install()` runs at OnEnable and on `ADDON_LOADED` for `Pawn`/`WeightsWatcher` (`Compat.IsAddOnLoaded`), guarded by `installed[name]`.
- Pawn: one stat per visible scale; `Compat.PawnItemValue` is pcall-wrapped and plain, and nil scores 0.
- WeightsWatcher has the same shape.
- The built-in categories are never replaced.

**`modules/Optimize.lua` (420): `NS.Optimize` (Opt).**
- `Opt.Generate(name, cfg, opts, onDone)`. Results are delivered **exactly once** through `onDone(items|nil, reason)` (B7, the doubled Rebuild).
- An unknown stat returns `nil, L["Unknown stat ID: %s"]`.
- `complex = #cfg > 1 or cfg[1].min or cfg[1].max or stat.complex`.
- `simple(stat, job)`: a greedy per slot with alternate-slot bumping. `bestWeapons` enumerates `{2H}`, `{MH 1H, OH}` and `{MH, OH 1H}` when `PS.CanDualWield()` (B15). ITEM_LEVEL needs a non-empty MH.
- `Opt.Trial(cfg) → ok, errKey` for the new-outfit dialog's `cNoItemsWithStatError`.
- `Opt.Rebuild(id, cfg)`: for a Complete outfit, empty result slots keep their old items. Then `O`, `Changed` and `St.Wear`; EM outfits go through `EM.SetItems`.
- `Opt.Cancel(job)`, `Opt.Running()`, `Opt.Suspend()`.
- Sends `OPTIMIZE_STARTED/PROGRESS/DONE`.
- Summary lines: `L["Checked %s outfits in %s seconds (%s outfits per second)"]` and `L["Best outfit score: %s"]`.
- `cfg` shape: `{ { stat, min, max }, complex = bool }`, default `{ { stat = "STA" } }`.

**`modules/Optimize_Genetic.lua` (380).**
- `GA.Start(name, cfg, job, rng)`: a coroutine driven by an AceTimer ticker. The budget per resume is `job.highSpeed and 0.1 or 0.02`.
- A resume error → `surface("optimize")` and `onDone(nil, "error")`. The ticker is cancelled on every exit.
- Constants kept: population 300, parents 25, mutation 0.2, at most 1000 generations, stop after 100 unchanged.
- Split into `seedPopulation`, `breed` (`while p2 == p1` bounded to 8), `generation`, `converged`, `compareOne`/`compareCompound`.
- `scoreWithEmpty` makes ties favour empty slots (Q6).
- **`rng` is injected** (default `math.random`). Tests pass a seeded LCG.

### 5.15 `modules/Companion.lua` (240): `NS.Companion`

State: `want = nil | { act, guid, tries, deadline }`, `ticker`, `lastSummonCall`.

| Function | Notes |
|---|---|
| `SummonByName(name, delay)`, `SummonByGUID(guid, delay)`, `SummonBySpecies(speciesID, delay)` | The name or guid goes through `PlainString` first, so nil or secret returns false (INV §3.10 Fix) |
| `DismissByName(name)`, `DismissByGUID(guid, delay)` | Dismissing a pending summon of the same guid cancels it |
| `Summoned() → guid\|nil` | |
| `CanAct()` | `BLOCKERS`: flying, `PS.InCombat`, `PS.IsDead`, feign death, casting, channeling, `< 3 s` since a summon call |
| `reconcile()` | The auto-summon watch comment is kept verbatim (Outfitter.lua:8345-8353). Retries every 1 s are **capped**: a summon at `MAX_TRIES = 30` blocked passes; a dismiss at `MAX_DISMISS_TRIES = 600` (ten minutes of flight), with `deadline = now + 60` re-armed by each blocked pass, so the auto-summon watch runs 60 s from the moment the player can act (a dismiss asked in flight still catches the pet re-summoned on landing, Outfitter.lua:8337-8358). At the cap it gives up with a DebugLog line. *(Group D review: a blocked dismiss on the summon's 30-try cap was abandoned mid-flight.)* |
| Hook | `Compat.HookPetSummon`. The body is `if NS.IsStoodDown() then return end; lastSummonCall = GetTime()`. |
| `Suspend()` / `Resume()` | Suspend cancels the ticker and clears `want`. Resume installs the hook once and stamps `lastSummonCall = GetTime()`, so the 3 s window follows every stand-up as it followed the legacy initialization (Outfitter.lua:5285-5286). |

### 5.16 `modules/Api.lua` (180): `_G.OutfitterReborn.API.v1` (H3)

| Member | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `WearOutfit(name)` / `RemoveOutfit(name)` | `FindByName`, then `St.Wear/Remove(id, { hw = false })` |
| `FindOutfitByName(name)` | case-insensitive; returns a read-only copy `{ id, name, category }` |
| `GetCurrentOutfitInfo()` | `St.CurrentInfo()` → `""` / `"Custom"` / name |
| `GetOutfitsUsingItem(link) → { {id, name} }` | `O.UsingItem(link)` (secret link → empty); read-only copies. The one item query a bag addon needs (ArkInventory used `Outfitter_GetOutfitsUsingItem`). |
| `RegisterOutfitEvent(fn)` / `UnregisterOutfitEvent(fn)` | `fn(event, id)` for `OUTFIT_ADDED`, `OUTFIT_DELETED`, `OUTFIT_RENAMED`, `OUTFIT_WORN`, `OUTFIT_REMOVED` and `OUTFIT_EDITED` (INV §6.4's ADD/DELETE/RENAME/WEAR/UNWEAR set; `OUTFITTER_INIT` has no equivalent), each `xpcall`-isolated; a return value is never an error |

`Outfitter_GenerateSmartOutfit` and every `Outfitter_*` global are **not** shimmed.

**Third-party consumers that lose something** (INV §10.1; listed in the Version History and the FAQ, and in D1's Why):

| Consumer | Used | After 1.1.0 |
|---|---|---|
| OutfitterFu (a FuBar plugin; FuBar support is retired, INV §18) | the INV §10.1 list/wear/event globals (`Outfitter.lua:405-449`; P4.25 confirms which ones against the plugin's last release) | broken; the v1 equivalents are `FindOutfitByName`, `WearOutfit`, `RemoveOutfit`, `RegisterOutfitEvent`, `GetCurrentOutfitInfo` |
| FishingBuddy | `Outfitter_FindOutfitByStatID("FISHING")`, `Outfitter_WearOutfit` | broken; the Fishing stat outfit is retired (INV §18). It can find a FISHING-triggered outfit by name. |
| ArkInventory | `Outfitter_GetOutfitsUsingItem`, `OutfitterItemList_*` | `GetOutfitsUsingItem(link)` replaces the first; the item-list globals are gone |
| any stat-category plugin | `GetNumStats`/`GetIndexedStat`, `StatCategories` | dropped (§5.14) |
| macros | `/script Outfitter_WearOutfit(...)` | `/of wear <name>` (§4.3) |

### 5.17 CCN risk register

| Today (est. CCN) | Sanctioned shape | New max |
|---|---|---|
| `_EquipmentChanges:optimize` (~24) | `classify` + `RULES`; `fixSwap` in four cases | ~6 |

| `UpdateEquippedItems` (~14) | `GATES` list | ~5 |

Lizard (in the automated-tests battery) is the gate. No helper exists only to game the count.

---

## 6. Native triggers (H1, H9, H10, H13, H14)

### 6.1 Overview

Every preset becomes a **trigger descriptor**. A descriptor is plain data in `defaults/Triggers.lua`: id, category, class, localized name and description, events, typed params, icon, family and family args. It is paired with a **condition function**, which comes from a family factory (`Triggers_Families.lua`) or a bespoke entry in `NS.TriggerConditions[id]` (the three `Triggers_Cond*.lua` files).

**What an outfit stores.** An outfit stores only `trigger.id`, its flags and `triggerParams[id]`. Behaviour therefore always comes from the shipped descriptor, which keeps "preset scripts resolve live" by construction (INV §9.4).

**Binding.** Each bound outfit owns one AceEvent+AceTimer target (H14):
- Game events go through `NS.SafeRegisterEvent`.
- Pseudo-events (states, tooltip, outfit worn/removed) arrive as bus messages.
- The condition returns a verdict `(action, delay, layer, interrupt)`, which the binder applies inside `NS.Equip.Batch`. This reproduces `_ScriptContext:PostProcess` (`OutfitterScripting.lua:2125-2187`) without its bugs.

**H1 guarantees:** no `loadstring`, no user Lua, no Custom editor (anti-patterns #20).

The source files read for this section are `OutfitterScripting.lua:1-2187`, `Outfitter.lua:996-1307, 1590-1679, 1769-1930, 4631-5056, 7328, 7536`, LK `docs/api/Bus/version-2-docs.md`, `Schema/version-3-docs.md`, DR `core/CoreSetup.lua:55-76` and DR `modules/Watchers.lua:1-31`.

### 6.2 Files

| File | Budget | Contents |
|---|---|---|
| `defaults/Zones.lua` | ~160 | The single zone source (§6.5) |
| `defaults/TriggerData.lua` | ~170 | `AURA_SPELL_STATE`, `AURA_ICON_STATE`, `FORM_SPELL_STATE`, `ARGENT_MOUNTS[faction]`, `VIGILANCE_NPCS`, `LEVIATHAN_NPCS`, `DIFFICULTY_GROUPS`, `VASHJIR_MAP_IDS = {201, 204, 205}`, skillLines `{182, 186, 393, 185, 633}`, `FIND_FISH = { spell = 43308, texture = 133888 }`, cooking-pet speciesIDs (O21) |
| `defaults/Triggers.lua` | ~500 | `NS.TriggerCatalog = { CATEGORY_ORDER, rows }` |
| `modules/Triggers_Params.lua` | ~260 | `NS.TriggerParams` (§6.4) |
| `modules/Triggers_Families.lua` | ~320 | `NS.TriggerFamilies`, guards `NS.TriggerGuards`, `TooltipHasLine` (§6.6) |
| `modules/Triggers_CondGeneral.lua` | ~330 | IN_DUNGEON, INSTANCE_DIFFICULTY, HAS_BUFF, TARGET, TRINKET_QUEUE, FEET_QUEUE, IN_ZONES, TALENT_TREE, PVP_FLAGGED |
| `modules/Triggers_CondTrade.lua` | ~330 | LOCKPICKING, RIDING, FISHING, FALLING, RESTING, SPELLCAST, COOKING |
| `modules/Triggers_CondQuest.lua` | ~300 | QUEST_MULTIPHASE_SURVEY, QUEST_TURNIN, PET_BATTLE, DANCE, COMPANION |
| `modules/Triggers_Effects.lua` | ~170 | `NS.TriggerEffects`: AutoLoot, FishTracking, ClickToMove, ActionBarPage, SavePet/RestorePet; each records into `ctx.persist` and restores |
| `modules/Triggers.lua` | ~320 | `NS.Triggers` registry (§6.7) |
| `modules/Triggers_Binder.lua` | ~400 | `NS.TriggerBinder` (§6.9) |
| `modules/States.lua` | ~420 | `NS.States` producers (§6.10) |
| `modules/States_Zone.lua` | ~190 | zone producer, derived tables, `ZoneInfo()` (§6.5) |
| `tools/gen-zones.lua` | ~120 | Map.db2 diff, never loaded |

### 6.3 Descriptor

```lua
local addonName, NS = ...
-- defaults/Triggers.lua — one data row per non-zone trigger, in picker order (§6.3)
local L = NS.L
NS.TriggerCatalog = NS.TriggerCatalog or {}          -- architecture-§3 idempotent
local C = NS.TriggerCatalog
C.CATEGORY_ORDER = { "GENERAL", "TRADE", "PVP", "QUEST", "ENTERTAIN", "CLASS" }   -- OutfitterScripting.lua:1761-1769
C.rows = {
  { id = "SWIMMING", category = "TRADE", icon = 132112,
    name = L["Swimming"], desc = L["Worn while you are swimming"],
    events = { "STATE:SWIMMING" },
    family = "smartUnequip", familyArgs = { state = "SWIMMING", unequipDelay = 2.5, guards = "skipAll" },
    params = {
      { key = "disableInstance", type = "bool", label = L["Don't equip in dungeons"],       default = false },
      { key = "disableBG",       type = "bool", label = L["Don't equip in Battlegrounds"],  default = false },
      { key = "disablePVP",      type = "bool", label = L["Don't equip while PvP flagged"], default = false },
      { key = "disableVashjir",  type = "bool", label = L["Don't equip in Vashj'ir"],       default = true  },
    } },
  -- …
}
```

| Field | Type | Meaning / source |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | SCREAMING_SNAKE | stable; stored in `outfit.trigger.id` (§6.8 maps the old ids) |
| `category` | `GENERAL\|TRADE\|PVP\|QUEST\|ENTERTAIN\|CLASS` | picker order (`SortScripts`, 1805-1828) |
| `class` | classFile or nil | listed only for that class (a token, so localization-§4 holds) |
| `name`, `desc` | `L[...]` | the old `$SETTING` English literals become `L` keys |
| `icon` | fileID | the bar default icon (ported from `OutfitterBar.lua:9-53`, C30) |
| `events` | array | UPPER game names plus pseudo keys `STATE:<ID>`, `TIMER`, `TOOLTIP_SHOW`, `TOOLTIP_HIDE`, `OUTFIT_WORN`, `OUTFIT_REMOVED`, `AURAS`, `BIND`. The catalog writes `BIND` here (as §6.8 does); `Finalize` removes it from the list and sets `initial = true`, so it is never registered. |
| `unit` | `"player"` or nil | first-arg filter, replacing `select(1, ...) ~= "player"` |
| `initial` | bool | **set by `Finalize` only**, from `BIND` in `events`: one evaluation at bind with event `"BIND"`. A catalog row never writes it (`test_triggers` pins that). |
| `ticker` | seconds or nil | an always-on AceTimer while bound |
| `params` | `{ key, type, label, default, min, max, step, suffix, hidden }` | typed (§6.4). `hidden(ctx)` replaces "a nil label skips the field" (1905). |
| `family`, `familyArgs` | | resolved by `Finalize` |
| `condition` | `fn(ctx, event, ...) → action, delay, layer, interrupt` | set by `Finalize` |
| `onWorn`, `onRemoved`, `onSuspend` | `fn(ctx)` | this outfit's own edges, and the stand-down restore |
| `layer` | string or nil | default layer on EQUIP (`"shapeshift"` for druid forms) |
| `refuseEM` | bool | TRINKET_QUEUE (590-594) |
| `persist` | bool | gives `ctx.persist = outfit.triggerState[id]` |

**Verdict** (multiple returns, so nothing is allocated per event; performance-§2):
- `action` is `NS.TRIG.EQUIP = "equip"`, `UNEQUIP = "unequip"`, `TAG = "tag"`, or nil.
- `delay` is applied only if the outfit changed (2170-2174).
- `layer` is the layer.
- `interrupt` calls `PS.SetInterruptCasting(true)`.

**Context** (`ctx`, one per bound outfit):

| Member | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `ctx.outfitId`, `ctx.outfit` | the id and the live record |
| `ctx.desc` | the descriptor |
| `ctx.params` | the stored value, falling back to the **descriptor** default; rebuilt at bind (fixes INV §9.8) |
| `ctx.state` | session scratch, wiped on rebind (replaces `self.MountIDs`, `unequipTime`, `previousSpecialization`, `UnequipWhenNotFalling`, `DismountTime`, `savedCompanionID`, `summonedPet`) |
| `ctx.persist` | `outfit.triggerState[id]` (named non-setting state) |
| `ctx:IsWorn()`, `ctx:DidEquip()`, `ctx:DidUnequip()`, `ctx:IsComplete()` | Stack reads |
| `ctx:StartTicker(sec)` / `ctx:StopTicker()` | idempotent (fixes the TIMER double-register) |
| `ctx:Disable(reasonKey)` | `NS.SetByPath("outfit.trigger.disabled", true, ctx.outfitId)`, prints once, unbinds |
| `ctx.now` | `GetTime()` at dispatch |

### 6.4 Typed parameters (H13; `NS.TriggerParams.TYPES[type] = { default, validate, normalize, display, capture }`)

| Type | Stored | Validate / normalize | Capture helper (UI buttons) | Replaces |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bool` | boolean | `type == "boolean"` | — | `boolean` |
| `number` | number | within `min..max`, rounded to `step` | — | `number` |
| `string` | string ≤ 64 | trimmed | — | `string` |
| `spelllist` | spellIDs ≤ 32 | positive integers, deduped, order kept | `CaptureLastCast()` (PS's last plain `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SUCCEEDED`), `CapturePlayerAura(i)` | HAS_BUFF `buffName`, SPELLCAST `spell` |
| `itemlist` | itemIDs ≤ 32 | the same; order = priority | `CaptureCursorItem()`, `CaptureSlotItem(slot)` | `stringtable` item names (TRINKET_QUEUE, FEET_QUEUE) |
| `maplist` | uiMapIDs | positive integers | `CaptureCurrentMap()` (`C_Map.GetBestMapForUnit("player")`) | `zonelist`/`GetZoneText` |
| `subzonelist` | strings | non-empty, deduped | `CaptureCurrentSubzone()` (`GetMinimapZoneText()`) | `zonelist` + MinimapZone |
| `unitlist` | `{npcID=n}` or `{player="Name-Realm"}` | exactly one key | `CaptureTarget()` | EQUIP_ON_TARGET `targetName` |
| `pet` | petGUID or `""` | `""` or `^BattlePet%-` | owned-pet dropdown, `CaptureSummonedPet()` | COMPANION pet name |

Display always goes from ID to name: `C_Map.GetMapInfo(id).name`, `C_Spell.GetSpellName(id)`, `C_Item.GetItemNameByID(id)` (async; the ID shows until it resolves) and `C_PetJournal.GetPetInfoByPetID`. Names never flow back into logic (localization-§4).

Schema instance rows are covered in §3.5.

### 6.5 Zones: five tables collapsed into one

```lua
NS.Zones = NS.Zones or {}
local Z = NS.Zones
-- ORDER IS WEAR ORDER: later rows are worn over earlier ones (cZoneSpecialIDs note, Outfitter.lua:1128-1137).
-- mapIDs are Map.db2 row IDs (8th return of GetInstanceInfo), InstanceType 3 (bg) / 4 (arena).
-- Regenerate with tools/gen-zones.lua against a wago.tools export; NEVER from memory.
Z.rows = {
  { id = "PVP_BATTLEGROUND", kind = "generic", instanceTypes = { pvp = true, arena = true }, name = L["Battleground"] },
  { id = "PVP_ARENA",        kind = "generic", instanceTypes = { arena = true },            name = L["Arena"] },
  { id = "BG_AV",  kind = "bg",    mapIDs = { 30, 2197 } },          -- name = GetRealZoneText(mapIDs[1]) at Finalize
  -- … (§6.8.2)
  { id = "ARENA_CAGEOFCARNAGE", kind = "arena", mapIDs = { 2759 } },
}
Z.BRAWL_MAP_IDS = { 1170, 1280, 1691, 2799 }   -- generic Battleground only
```

Per-map names come from `GetRealZoneText(mapID)`, so 62 locale keys disappear (§4.6).

**Derived at load in `States_Zone.lua`, never kept by hand:**

| Old table | Derived as |
|---|---|
| `cInstanceMapIDZoneIDs` | `BY_MAP_ID[mapID]` = the row id, plus `PVP_BATTLEGROUND` for kind bg/arena, plus `PVP_ARENA` for kind arena. Brawl maps give the generic battleground only. |
| `cZoneSpecialIDs` | `ORDER`, the array of row ids |
| `cSpecialIDEvents`, `BuiltinEvents` | the pseudo key `"STATE:" .. id`; no table at all |
| `PresetScripts` (zone rows) | `Triggers.Finalize` emits one trigger per row (family `zone`) |

**Producer:**
- Events: `ZONE_CHANGED_NEW_AREA`, `ZONE_CHANGED`, `ZONE_CHANGED_INDOORS`, PEW, each with a 0.01 s cancel+reschedule debounce. Perf bucket `zoneUpdate`.
- The three-field early-out is kept: instance type, instance mapID, and `GetZoneText()` as a change detector only (the Ashran/Wintergrasp comment, 4929-4938).
- `CurrentIds()`: the instance type gives the generics (so a brand-new map still works), and `BY_MAP_ID` gives the specifics. **It never matches a name.**
- It emits inside one `NS.Equip.Batch`, running `States.Set(id, active)` in `ORDER`, so a specific outfit beats the generic one.
- First sighting seeds from `Triggers.ForState(id)` against the stack (`WearingOutfitWithScriptID`, 4881-4885).
- Public reads: `NS.States.InZone(id)`, `NS.States.InPvPInstance()`, and `NS.States.ZoneInfo()` for `/of zone` (5017-5049: map id, active ids, "no entry", plus `Equip.Diagnostic()`).

**IN_ZONES.** It matches the best map or any ancestor (`parentMapID`, bounded at 8) on uiMapID. Subzones match `GetMinimapZoneText()` strings captured on the player's own client, under deviation **D3**.

### 6.6 Families and guards (`modules/Triggers_Families.lua`)

**Guards** (`NS.TriggerGuards`, G):

| Guard | Blocks when |
|---|---|
| `G.instance(ctx)` | `params.disableInstance` and `IsInInstance()` is party or raid |
| `G.bg(ctx)` | `params.disableBG` and `States.InPvPInstance()` |
| `G.pvp(ctx)` | `params.disablePVP` and `UnitIsPVP("player")` |
| `G.vashjir(ctx)` | `params.disableVashjir` and the best map is in `VASHJIR_MAP_IDS` |
| `G.specOff(ctx)` | `params["spec"..PS.Spec()] == false` |
| `G.noControl()` | not dead and `not HasFullControl()` |

`SPEC_PARAMS(default)` builds `spec1..spec4`. The label is `select(2, GetSpecializationInfo(i))`, for display only. `spec4` is hidden when there are fewer than 4 specs.

**Families** (each reproduces a generator at `OutfitterScripting.lua:217-492`):

| Family | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `simple{state}` | on → EQUIP, off → UNEQUIP (`GenerateSimpleScript`) |
| `smartUnequip{state, unequipDelay, spec, guards}` | `spec` and `G.specOff` → UNEQUIP. on → EQUIP; off and `DidEquip` → UNEQUIP with `unequipDelay`. `guards = "skipAll"` → nil if any guard blocks. |
| `zone{state}` | `smartUnequip{state, spec = true}`; events `STATE:<id>` + `ACTIVE_PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED` (O10) |
| `druidForm{state}` | nil if bg/pvp, `specOff` or `noControl`. `OUTFIT_WORN` → TAG `"shapeshift"`. on and not worn → EQUIP `"shapeshift"`. off and (`unequipComplete` or not Complete) → UNEQUIP, with delay 2 in combat (393-405). |
| `druidCaster` | nil if instance/bg/pvp, `specOff` or `noControl`. `OUTFIT_WORN` → TAG. on → EQUIP `"shapeshift"` with **delay 0.1** (cancels the transient caster form, 1145-1160). off and not Complete → UNEQUIP. |
| `gathering{skillLine, text}` | `TOOLTIP_SHOW`: the line is found, exact, at line ≥ 2, and difficult (or `ignoreDifficulty`) → EQUIP. `TOOLTIP_HIDE` and `DidEquip` → UNEQUIP with delay 1 (433-490). |
| `vehicleMount{unitType, npcs, regenUnequip}` | the shared body of the three vehicle quests (1376-1567; §6.8 rows 24-26) |
| `spec{index}` | acts only when `PS.Spec()` differs from `ctx.state.prevSpec`. EQUIP if it equals `index`, else UNEQUIP; delay 0.5 "so artifacts equip properly" (1786-1801). |

`TooltipHasLine(text) → found, difficult, exact, line` reproduces `TooltipContainsLine` (7328-7351): HSV `s>0.2, v>0.2, h<50 or h>150`, at most 30 lines. It uses a **plain** `find(s, text, 1, true)` (the old pattern find broke on `-` and `(`), and secret `GetText()` counts as no match.

### 6.7 Registry (`modules/Triggers.lua`)

```lua
NS.Triggers = NS.Triggers or {}
local T = NS.Triggers
T.PSEUDO = {                                       -- H14: the ONE translation table, Catalog-backed
  STATE          = "STATE_CHANGED",                -- NS.MSG key; payload (stateId, isActive)
  AURAS          = "AURAS_SCANNED",
  TOOLTIP_SHOW   = "TOOLTIP_SHOWN",
  TOOLTIP_HIDE   = "TOOLTIP_HIDDEN",
  OUTFIT_WORN    = "OUTFIT_WORN",                  -- payload (id, layer, byTrigger)
  OUTFIT_REMOVED = "OUTFIT_REMOVED",               -- payload (id, byTrigger)
  TIMER = false, BIND = false,                     -- binder-internal (AceTimer / bind-time), never a message
}
```

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `T.Finalize()` | Runs in `OnInitialize`. Walks the catalog rows and the zone rows, and resolves `condition` from `NS.TriggerFamilies[family](familyArgs)` or `NS.TriggerConditions[id]`. Fills the param defaults. Indexes `byId`, `byState`, `sorted`. In debug it asserts that every event is a pseudo key or UPPER_SNAKE. It appends `SPEC_1..N` named `"<UnitClass>: <spec>"` (`InstallTalentTreeScripts`), re-run at `PLAYER_LOGIN` if spec data is not ready. |
| `T.Get(id)` | the descriptor or nil |
| `T.List(category, classFile)`, `T.Categories(classFile)` | Sorted by `CATEGORY_ORDER`, then the lower-cased name. Class rows appear only for `classFile`. Spec rows are under category `SPEC`. |
| `T.ForState(stateId)` | ids subscribing to `STATE:<id>` |
| `T.SchemaRows()` | §3.5 |
| `T.ValidateParam(desc, key, value) → ok, err` | via `TYPES` |
| `T.IsPseudo(name)` | `name:find("^STATE:") or T.PSEUDO[name] ~= nil`. Pseudo keys never reach `RegisterEvent` (quirk 8). |

### 6.8 Every existing preset mapped to its new trigger

These shared mechanics hold for every row and are not repeated:
- "smart" means unequip only when `DidEquip`.
- A Complete outfit is always re-worn on EQUIP, so the stack clears.
- The delay is applied only when the outfit changed.
- `combatDisabled` and `trigger.disabled` gate every row.

Status values: **Port**, **Port+fix** (with the INV §19 fix named), or **Dropped** (with the reason).

#### 6.8.1 Activities, general and quest

| # | Old ID | New id | Cat / class | Events | Params (default) | Condition | Status |

| 2 | MINING | GATHER_MINING | TRADE | same | ignoreDifficulty (false) | `gathering{skillLine=186}` | Port+fix |

### 6.9 Binder (`modules/Triggers_Binder.lua`): `NS.TriggerBinder`

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `BindAll()` | Returns if `NS.IsStoodDown()` or `not NS.GetSetting("auto.enabled")` (a loaded legacy addon does not hold binding, O2). Otherwise binds every outfit with a `trigger.id`, then sends `TRIGGERS_REBOUND`. Called at the end of LoadPass, from Resume, and through `SetActive(true)`. |
| `UnbindAll()` | Stand-down, `auto.enabled` off, profile callback re-apply, DeleteAll |
| `SetActive(on)` | `BindAll`/`UnbindAll`. Its callers: the binder's own `SETTINGS_CHANGED` handler for `auto.enabled` (the one route for every writer: panel, `/of auto`, the AutoOn/AutoOff bindings) and `NS.ApplyProfile()` on a profile change (which writes no row, §3.1). |
| `Bind(id)` | Fails quietly on an unknown id (prints "Trigger %s is not available" once), `trigger.disabled`, or `refuseEM` with kind em. Builds `ctx`, `t = NS.NewBusTarget()`, then `LibStub("AceTimer-3.0"):Embed(t)`. Registers the events (below), calls `States.Demand(key, +1)`, starts `desc.ticker`, and runs the 0 s `BIND` evaluation if `desc.initial`. |
| `Unbind(id)` | `t:UnregisterAllEvents()`, `UnregisterAllMessages()`, `CancelAllTimers()`, `States.Demand(key, -1)`, `desc.onSuspend` if worn, then drop `ctx` |
| `Bind(id)` on `OUTFIT_ADDED` | an outfit created with a trigger (`O.Create` `opts.trigger`, `EM.StoreOnServer`, `EM.StoreLocally`, `EM.Sync` restoring from `emPreserved`) is bound at once, not at the next `BindAll` |
| `Rebind(id)` | on `OUTFIT_EDITED (id, "trigger")` only, the one per-outfit message (§3.5: the outfit-schema announce sends no `SETTINGS_CHANGED`). **Coalesced**: it marks `dirty[id]` and one 0 s AceTimer rebinds every dirty id, so a dialog `SetMany` of N entries costs one unbind/bind and one `BIND` evaluation. `OUTFIT_DELETED` unbinds at once. |
| `rejected`, `faulted` | listed in `/of diagnostics` |

**Routing one event name:**
- `STATE:<ID>`: one `t:RegisterMessage(NS.MSG.STATE_CHANGED, h)` per target. `h` drops states not in `ctx.states`, then calls `Dispatch(ctx, "STATE", stateId, active)`.
- `AURAS`, `TOOLTIP_SHOW`, `TOOLTIP_HIDE`: `RegisterMessage` for the mapped key.
- `OUTFIT_WORN`/`OUTFIT_REMOVED`: filtered to `ctx.outfitId`. Runs `desc.onWorn/onRemoved` (isolated), then `Dispatch`, so TAG still works. Replaces `OutfitScriptEvents` (2087-2100).
- `TIMER`: the ticker; nothing is registered.
- Anything else: `NS.SafeRegisterEvent(t, name, h, Binder.rejected)`; `h` applies `desc.unit`, then dispatches.

**Dispatch** (gate → call → apply, each CCN ≤ 6):

```lua
local function Gate(ctx)                       -- old cScriptPrefix 1836-1840
  local tr = ctx.outfit.trigger
  if ctx.faulted or tr.disabled then return false end
  if tr.combatDisabled and (NS.PlayerState.InCombat() or NS.PlayerState.MaybeInCombat()) then return false end
  return true
end
function Binder.Dispatch(ctx, event, ...)
  if not Gate(ctx) then return end
  local t0 = Perf.on and debugprofilestop()
  ctx.now = GetTime()
  local n, args = select("#", ...), { ... }            -- 5.1 xpcall forwards no arguments (§5.10)
  local ok, action, delay, layer, interrupt = xpcall(function()
    return ctx.desc.condition(ctx, event, unpack(args, 1, n)) end, errorHandler)
  if t0 then Perf.Note("triggerDispatch", debugprofilestop() - t0) end   -- one static bucket (§2.1)
  if not ok then return Fault(ctx, action) end
  if action then Apply(ctx, action, delay, layer, interrupt) end
end
```

- The condition runs **outside** any bracket, so a raising condition cannot strand the counter (fixes "TIMER errors strand the count").
- `Fault` prints once: "Trigger %s for outfit %s failed and was switched off for this session: %s". It sets `ctx.faulted` and unbinds. The CPU-lockup guard (2133-2153) is retired, because no code is user-written.

**Apply** (`PostProcess`, 2155-2186):

```lua
NS.Equip.Batch(function()
  if action == "equip" then
    local worn = NS.Stack.IsWorn(id)
    if not worn or ctx:IsComplete() then NS.Stack.Wear(id, { layer = layer or desc.layer, byTrigger = true }) end
    changed = not worn
  elseif action == "unequip" then
    if NS.Stack.IsWorn(id) then NS.Stack.Remove(id, { byTrigger = true }); changed = true end
  elseif action == "tag" then NS.Stack.TagLayer(id, layer) end
  if interrupt then NS.PlayerState.SetInterruptCasting(true) end
  if changed and delay then NS.Equip.SetUpdateDelay(ctx.now, delay) end
end)
```

**Initial evaluation.** For every descriptor with `initial` (that is, `BIND` in its catalog `events`, §6.3), `Bind` runs the `BIND` evaluation 0 s later. This is a **deliberate fix**: today `ActivateAllScripts` runs 1 s after the first PEW (5213), so RESTING and IN_DUNGEON miss the login.

### 6.10 State producers (`modules/States.lua`): `NS.States`

**States:**
- `MOUNTED`, `SWIMMING`, `DINING`, `SPIRIT_REGEN`, `STEALTH`, `GHOST_WOLF`, `FEIGN_DEATH`
- `FORM_CASTER`, `FORM_BEAR`, `FORM_CAT`, `FORM_TRAVEL`, `FORM_MOONKIN`, `FORM_TREE`
- the 33 zone ids

**`States.Set(id, active)`** (`SetSpecialOutfitEnabled` 4876-4915):
- coerces the value to boolean; unchanged is a no-op
- seeds a nil state from the worn outfits
- publishes `STATE_CHANGED`
- an unknown id is a debug line, not a raise

**Auras** (INV §8.1, all kept):
- **Trigger:** player `UNIT_AURA`. In combat it runs as a unique 2 s AceTimer, otherwise immediately. It also runs on `PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED`, `PLAYER_ALIVE/UNGHOST` and **`PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD`** (INV §6.1 "UpdateAuraStates" after every loading screen; the form poll follows the scan as usual, and the zone producer has its own PEW debounce, §6.5). Between `PLAYER_LEAVING_WORLD` and PEW, `UNIT_AURA` is dropped (`Equip.SuspendedForLoad()`), matching the old unregister (INV §6.1).
- Perf bucket `auraScan`.
- `AuraRestricted()` resolves `Enum.AddOnRestrictionType` **by name** (Combat, Encounter, ChallengeMode, PvPMatch). While restricted, every aura source reads false (O28).
- **Scan:** bounded at 100, with `NS.IsSecret(aura)` checked **before** the nil test. Icon first (`AURA_ICON_STATE`: 134062, 132805 → DINING), then spell (`AURA_SPELL_STATE`: 2645 → GHOST_WOLF, 5384 → FEIGN_DEATH, 58984 → STEALTH, 5215 → STEALTH (new)).
- **After the scan:** fills `auraSpells` (plain spellIDs, read by `PlayerHasSpell`), publishes `AURAS_SCANNED`, then schedules the form poll 0.01 s later.
- `IsFeigning()` is exposed for Companion.

**Forms** (`GetShapeshiftFormInfo` 4th return → `FORM_SPELL_STATE`):
- 5487 → FORM_BEAR (sets `PS.SetMaybeInCombat(true)`), 768 → FORM_CAT, 783 → FORM_TRAVEL, 24858 → FORM_MOONKIN, 33891 → FORM_TREE (new), 1784 → STEALTH
- a druid with no active form → FORM_CASTER
- the index cache is runtime
- while suspended for a load (`Equip.SuspendedForLoad()`), it re-polls every 0.1 s (4787-4796)

**Merged:** `STEALTH = auraWant.STEALTH or formWant.STEALTH` (fixes the independent NOT_STEALTH edge). The first scan after a Resume reads the forms itself before the merge, so a rogue standing in Stealth (a form with no aura) gets no off/on edge in the 0.01 s before the first form poll.

**Poll:** a 0.5 s repeating AceTimer sets `MOUNTED = IsMounted() and not UnitOnTaxi("player")` and `SWIMMING = IsSwimming()` inside one Batch. It no longer broadcasts TIMER. The aura scan sets `MOUNTED` too, inside its own Batch, since mounting fires `UNIT_AURA` (the legacy `UpdateAuraStates` ran `UpdateMountedState`, Outfitter.lua:4768).

**Dining:**
- `UNIT_HEALTH`/`UNIT_POWER_UPDATE` are registered only while DINING is on and unregistered only when it goes off (fixes 4895-4899).
- On either event while DINING is on: `if PlayerIsFull() then States.Set("DINING", false) end`. That edge drives the smart unequip of the DINING outfit (INV §8.4 "while dining, `PlayerIsFull()` auto-removes the outfit").
- In the aura scan: `DINING = auraWant.DINING and not PlayerIsFull()` (INV §8.4 "UpdateAuraStates also suppresses Dining when the player is full").
- `PlayerIsFull` is ported in logic (1887-1930): every unreadable or secret value means not full.

**Spirit:**
- Demand-driven. `UNIT_SPELLCAST_SENT` and `UNIT_POWER_UPDATE` (player, MANA) are registered only while some binding demands `STATE:SPIRIT_REGEN`. This replaces the non-existent `UNIT_MANA`.
- A mana drop within 10 s of a cast turns it off and (re)schedules a 5 s re-enable.
- `prevMana` survives secret windows.

**Tooltip:**
- `GameTooltip:HookScript("OnShow"/"OnHide")`, installed once.
- The bodies return when stood down or when `Demand("TOOLTIP") == 0`.
- The MobInfo2 hook is dropped (INV §17).

**Suspend/Resume:** unregister everything and cancel timers / clear the state table, then re-run the zone, aura and form checks.

### 6.11 Timers owned by triggers

| Delay | Owner | Kind |
|---|---|---|
| 0.01 s | zone update | cancel + reschedule |
| 0.5 s | mounted/swimming poll | repeating, States target |
| 0.5 s | per-binding ticker (TRINKET_QUEUE, FEET_QUEUE, FALLING; dynamic for LOCKPICKING, RIDING) | repeating, idempotent |
| 2.0 s | in-combat aura re-scan | unique |
| 0.01 / 0.1 s | form poll after a scan / while suspended | unique |
| 5.0 s | Spirit re-enable | cancel + reschedule |
| 0 s | `BIND` evaluation | one-shot |
| 0 s | coalesced `Rebind` of the dirty ids (§6.9) | one-shot, binder module target |

All are AceTimer on the owning target, cancelled by Suspend/Unbind. `SchedulerLib` is gone.

### 6.12 Secret values and IDs

**Read through `NS.Secrets`:**
- aura `icon`/`spellId`
- `UnitPower`/`UnitPowerMax`/`UnitHealth`/`UnitHealthMax`/`UnitPowerType`
- `UnitGUID("mouseover"|"target")`, `UnitName("target")`
- the `UNIT_SPELLCAST_START` spellID
- tooltip `GetText()`

A secret value never matches and never counts as full.

**IDs matched:**

| Domain | Match key |
|---|---|
| Spells/auras | spellID |
| Items | itemID |
| Maps | uiMapID / instance mapID |
| NPCs | creatureID |
| Professions | skillLine (182, 186, 393, 185; 633 under O22) |
| Difficulty | difficultyID |
| Class | classFile |
| Pets | petGUID or speciesID |
| Faction | the first return of `UnitFactionGroup` (H10) |

The faction comment reads: `-- 1st return is the English token ("Alliance"/"Horde"); the 2nd is localized (H10)`.

**Names used in logic, and why each is allowed:**
- Player names (TARGET) are not localized.
- Subzone strings are under D3.
- Profession display names come from `GetTradeSkillDisplayName(id)`: the client's own localized name for an ID, never a hardcoded or `L` string. localization-§4's MUST names a GlobalString constant, which this is not, so it is recorded as row **D10** rather than argued as equivalent.

---

## 7. UI surfaces

Every LibKa0s call below was checked against v1.85.0:
- Skin `lib.VOCAB`
- `Widgets.lua:746` Dropdown, `:757` ContextMenu, `:788` CloseMenu, `:836` SearchBox, `:1003` TestStrip
- `WidgetsDragHandle.lua:979` DragHandle
- `Core.lua:221` ApplySkin, `:272` MakeCloseButton, `:324` RaiseStaticPopups
- `Pool.lua` New, Acquire, ReleaseAll, NewKeyed, AcquireKeyed

### 7.0 Ground rules

1. **Prefixed global names.** Frames are `OutfitterReborn*` and popups `OUTFITTERREBORN_*`, so a legacy install's `OutfitterFrame`, `OutfitterItem0..13`, `OutfitterEnable<Slot>` and `OUTFITTER_*` never collide.
2. **The window is never shown during combat lockdown (C40).**
   - The list rows own secure buttons (`Outfitter.lua:8448-8494`). A protected child restricts its parent and its anchors (comment at `Outfitter.lua:1326-1331`).
   - The window therefore hides inside `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`, while protected calls are still allowed, and comes back at `PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED` if the sheet is open.
   - This replaces today's in-combat `Hide()` from the PaperDollFrame OnHide hook (`:1338-1342`) and its silently dropped strata write (`:1427`, events-frames-taint-§2).
   - It is what lets the window go into `UISpecialFrames` (C13).
   - The slot checkboxes hold no secure frames and may restack in combat.
3. **Nothing is written into a Blizzard frame or table** (skinning-§7). Three writes are dropped as a result:
   - the `PaperDollSidebarTabs` nudge (`Outfitter.lua:5348`, INV §17)
   - `CharacterLevelText:Hide()` (INV §7.3)
   - `StackSplitFrame:Hide()` (`:8703, 8712`)

   The flyout function replacement becomes `hooksecurefunc` (§7.9).
4. **Layer values come only from `NS.Constants.LAYERS`** (frame-layering-§5). There is no `SetFrameStrata("…")` literal anywhere. The recursive `Outfitter.SetFrameLevel/SetFrameStrata` helpers (`OutfitterUITools.lua:554-588`) are deleted.
5. **Every hook body checks the latch first**: `if NS.IsStoodDown() then return end` (H26). Window follow runs on an owned driver frame.

### 7.1 Frames, tokens and layers

`S` is `NS.Skin`, `L` is `C.LAYERS`, and "Host" is `OutfitterRebornSheetHolder`.

| Frame | Type / size | Parent | Strata / level | Built with | Replaces |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OutfitterRebornSheetHolder` | Frame, `SetAllPoints(PaperDollFrame)`, draws nothing, no mouse | `PaperDollFrame` | inherited | — | `OutfitterButtonFrame` (Outfitter.xml:578) |
| `OutfitterRebornSheetButton` | Button 22×22, `btn.icon = C.LOGO_128`, right of `CharacterFrameCloseButton` (−2, 0) | Host | inherited | `S.Button` + `S.SquareIcon(btn,{crop=true})`; tooltip `L["Toggle the Outfitter window"]` | `OutfitterButton` robe art (O29) |
