**Owner tokens (`NS.SKIN_TOKENS`, skinning-§1/§9).** The defaults are today's values (INV §14, §7.2):

| Token | Default | Follows |
|---|---|---|
| `of.outfit.disabled` | `0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1` | `text.disabled` |
| `of.outfit.missing` | `1, 0.1, 0.1, 1` | `sem.bad` |
| `of.outfit.banked` | `0.25, 0.2, 1.0, 1` | `sem.info` |
| `of.row.trigger` | `1, 1, 1, 1` | `icon.tint` |
| `of.slot.unknown` | `1, 0.82, 0, 1` | `sem.statusWarn` |
| `of.bar.worn` | `1, 0.82, 0, 1` | `icon.hot` |
| `of.dialog.error` | `1, 0.33, 0.33, 1` | `sem.bad` |

**`C.LAYERS`** (`core/Constants.lua`, frame-layering-§5):
- `STRATA = { HUD = "MEDIUM", MODAL = "DIALOG", POPUP = "FULLSCREEN_DIALOG", CURSOR = "TOOLTIP" }`. The keys are frame-layering-§1's (T9's key is `CURSOR`).
- `LEVEL = { DOCK_OVER_HOST = 1, SLOT_ENABLES_OVER_HOST = 1, SLOT_CHECKBOX = 1, CHROME = 1, CONTENT = 2, ROW_CONTROLS = 1, ROW_SECURE = 2, SCROLL = 3, BAR_ROW = 1, BAR_BUTTON = 2, COVER = 30, MARKER = 40, HANDLE = 50 }`. `BAR_ROW` and `BAR_BUTTON` sit in the HUD content band `L+1..L+19` (§3 :75); `SLOT_CHECKBOX` is the content level inside the slot-checkbox container.
- **Every** `SetFrameLevel` argument names one of these members (frame-layering-§5); there is no literal `+ 1` anywhere.

The header comment states the whole stack once (frame-layering-§5 :101):
- **Host-matched:** `CharacterFrame` is the host of the two docked surfaces, the window (host level + `DOCK_OVER_HOST`) and the slot checkboxes (highest descendant + `SLOT_ENABLES_OVER_HOST`) (BP §6.3, frame-layering-§3 :75).
- **Own tiers:** bar `HUD`, dialogs and progress `MODAL`, icon chooser `POPUP`, compare tooltips `CURSOR`.
- **Library tiers it relies on:** the DebugLog console at `DIALOG`, which the icon chooser and a dialog can cover; the copy window at `FULLSCREEN`; Widgets menus and dropdown pullouts at `FULLSCREEN_DIALOG`; the drag-handle grip at +10.
- **Blizzard tiers:** StaticPopups in `DIALOG` (raised by `NS.RaiseStaticPopups`); `GameTooltip`/`ShoppingTooltip1/2` at `TOOLTIP`.

### 7.2 Dock (`modules/Dock.lua`): `NS.Dock`

```lua
local Dock = { wanted = false, suspended = false, followDisabled = false, built = false }
NS.Dock = Dock
function Dock.ShouldShow()
  return Dock.wanted and not Dock.suspended and not NS.IsStoodDown()
     and PaperDollFrame:IsVisible() and not InCombatLockdown()
end
function Dock.Refresh()            -- the ONLY place OutfitterRebornWindow:Show/Hide is called
  if InCombatLockdown() then return end          -- rule 7.0.2
  if Dock.ShouldShow() then Dock.build(); Dock.Attach(); Dock.ApplyLayers(); win:Show(); NS.Window.MarkDirty(true)
  elseif win and win:IsShown() then win:Hide() end
end
```

**Entry points.** All of them use `Dock.Open/Close/Toggle`, and `Dock.IsShown()` reports the state:
- the sheet button
- the `OutfitterRebornToggleWindow` binding
- `/of show`
- the launcher's "Show window"

`Dock.Open()`:
- If the sheet is hidden, it calls `ToggleCharacter("PaperDollFrame")` first (INV §7.1).
- In lockdown it prints `L["The Outfitter window opens when combat ends."]` once and keeps `wanted = true`.

| Hook / event | Handler |
|---|---|
| `PaperDollFrame:HookScript("OnShow")` | `Dock.Refresh()` (latch-gated) |
| `PaperDollFrame:HookScript("OnHide")` | `wanted = false; Refresh(); NS.CloseMenu(); NS.Dialogs.CloseAll("host")` ("doesn't reappear next open", Outfitter.lua:1500-1504) |
| `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED` | if shown: `Rows.DisarmSecure()`, `win:Hide()`, `suspended = true`. Dialogs stay open (O30). |
| `PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED` | `suspended = false; NS.SecureQueue.Flush(); Refresh()` |
| Lifecycle | `Dock.Suspend()` / `Dock.Resume()` (§5.12) |

`win:OnHide` runs `St.Select(nil)` (which hides the slot checkboxes via `SELECTION_CHANGED`), `NS.CloseMenu()` and `NS.IconChooser.Close("opener")`.

**Attach and follow** (keeps `Outfitter.lua:1355-1488`):
- `PositionWindow()` is a single `SetPoint("TOPLEFT", UIParent, "BOTTOMLEFT", right*hostScale/scale, top*hostScale/scale + Dock.Y_OFFSET)` with `Y_OFFSET = -30`, and no ClearAllPoints.
  - If `GetRight()` is nil, it parks at the UIParent TOPLEFT.
  - Corner reads go through `NS.SafeNumber`.
- `Attach()` runs `pcall(PositionWindow)`. On failure it sets `followDisabled = true`, calls `win:SetParent(OutfitterRebornSheetHolder)` (the "usable but pins the sheet" fallback, :1477-1488), then `Window.Relevel()`, and writes one `NS.Debug("Dock", …)` line.
- The driver's OnUpdate is set in `win:OnShow` and cleared in `OnHide`, throttled to `FOLLOW_INTERVAL = 0.05`. Each tick, in Perf bucket `dockFollow`:
  1. `ApplyLayers()` (cached compare)
  2. `Attach()` if the corner moved
  3. `Window.Render()` if dirty

**Layer sync** (H4, frame-layering-§1 :42, §4 :90):

```lua
function Dock.ApplyLayers()
  NS.SlotEnables.MaybeRestack()                      -- allowed in combat
  local strata, level = host:GetFrameStrata(), NS.SafeNumber(host:GetFrameLevel(), 0) + L.LEVEL.DOCK_OVER_HOST
  if strata == Dock.lastStrata and level == Dock.lastLevel then return end
  NS.SecureQueue.Run("dock.layers", function()       -- protected writes: window has secure children
    win:SetFrameStrata(strata); win:SetFrameLevel(level); NS.Window.Relevel(level)
    Dock.lastStrata, Dock.lastLevel = strata, level
  end)
end
```

The strata always comes from the host; the comment at `Outfitter.lua:1412-1418` is kept.

`Window.Relevel(base)` re-asserts the whole stack (chrome +CHROME, body and rows +CONTENT, row controls row+ROW_CONTROLS, secure row+ROW_SECURE, scroll +SCROLL). It runs after every strata or level change and after any SetParent (frame-layering-§3 :77-80). Because CharacterFrame changes level when raised, the follow tick re-syncs on every raise.

### 7.3 Window and list

**Chrome (`modules/Window.lua`, `NS.Window`):**
- **Title** `L["Outfitter Reborn"]` (O31). A title-strip with settings, then close (standalone :40).
- **Footer:** New outfit on the left. On the right, `Slots: [All] [None]` → `NS.Outfits.SetAllSlotsEnabled(sel, true|false)`.
- **No tabs.** Options become the settings pages and About becomes the landing page, which removes the crossed tab-id fix.
- No test marker (H17). Scale is inherited from UIParent, with a comment.
- `Window.MarkDirty(wipeCache)` replaces `Update(true)` (Outfitter.lua:3153). It is triggered by `OUTFIT_ADDED/DELETED/EDITED/RENAMED/WORN/REMOVED`, `STACK_CHANGED`, `INVENTORY_CHANGED`, `SELECTION_CHANGED`, `EM_SYNCED`, `TRIGGERS_REBOUND` and `SETTINGS_CHANGED` for `auto.enabled`. It renders only while shown.

**Rows (`modules/Window_Rows.lua`, `NS.WindowRows`).** These carry the secure-button constraint (INV §16.3):
- **Fixed and virtualized.** All 14 rows are built once in `Rows.Build()`, which runs only from `Dock.Refresh` and so only out of combat. Scrolling re-binds data by offset (`ROW_H = 18`). Rows are **never** re-anchored, re-parented, shown or hidden after build. A blank row has `row.entry == nil`.
- **The secure child:**
  - `CreateFrame("Button", name.."Secure", row, "SecureActionButtonTemplate")`, `SetAllPoints(row)` once, `useOnKeyDown = false`.
  - `HookScript` wires PreClick, PostClick, OnEnter and OnLeave to the row handlers (`:8423-8444`).
  - The "never SetPoint/SetParent it" comment is kept verbatim (`:8452-8462`).
- **`Rows.ArmSecure(row)` / `Rows.DisarmSecure()`** are no-ops in lockdown. Item rows set `type = nil`, `target-bag` and `target-slot`, and Show; other kinds Hide the secure child (`:8467-8494`). Disarm runs before a row goes blank, on render, and at `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`.

**Render:** `RENDER[entry.kind](row, entry)`, with `S.ListRow(row, { stripe = odd, selected = entry.id == St.Selected() })`.

| Kind | Regions |
|---|---|
| `category` | chevron (`chevron-right`/`chevron-down`), name `S.Text(fs,"heading")`; hover shows the category description |
| `outfit` | worn checkbox `row.worn`; EM checkbox `row.em` (`L["Stored in the Equipment Manager"]`); name coloured by `STATUS_TOKEN[O.Status(id)]` (`cDisabledOutfitName` format for disabled); trigger mark `row.trig` = `NS.Icon("ph:lightning")` with `of.row.trigger`, alpha 0.4 when `auto.enabled` is off or `trigger.disabled` is set (the same flag the name colour reads through `O.Status`); menu button `row.menu` = `NS.Icon("ellipses")` |
| `item` | 16 px icon + name in `NS.QualityColor` |

**Clicks** (`Outfitter.lua:8688-8734`):
- PreClick records `SpellCanTargetItem() or SpellCanTargetItemID()`. While a spell is targeting, the secure button handles the click.
- Category: toggle `Window.collapsed[id]` (session only).
- Item left: `Compat.PickupItemLocation(loc)`.
- Item right: `Compat.UseContainerItem`, unless the merchant buyback tab is open (`MerchantFrame.selectedTab == 2`, read only).
- Outfit left: `NS.Stack.Wear(id, { hw = true, clearDisabled = true })`.
- Outfit right or the menu button: `NS.OutfitMenu.Open(row, id)`.
- Outfit body: `St.Select(id)`.
- Worn checkbox: check → `St.Wear(id, { hw = true, clearDisabled = true })`, uncheck → `St.Remove(id, { hw = true })`.
- **EM checkbox `row.em`** (INV §3.5 `OutfitServerButton`; `Outfitter.lua` row handler):
  - checked on a native outfit that is **not selected** → `St.Wear(id, { hw = true, clearDisabled = true })` and `St.Select(id)`, and the box is put back unchecked (the first check only selects)
  - checked on the **selected** native outfit → `ok, popupKey = NS.EM.StoreOnServer(id)`; on refusal `StaticPopup_Show(popupKey)` (`OUTFITTERREBORN_EM_FULL`) and the box is put back unchecked
  - unchecked on an `em` outfit → `NS.EM.StoreLocally(id)`
  - in combat it is refused with `L["Outfits can't be changed in combat."]` (EM saves are protected)

**Hover:**
- Item: `GameTooltip:SetBagItem/SetInventoryItem`, with the repair or sell cursor through `NS.Compat`.
- Outfit: `NS.OutfitTooltip.Show(id, row, "ANCHOR_RIGHT")`.

**List model (`modules/Window_ListModel.lua`, pure).** `ListModel.Build(collapsed, cache)` returns `{kind, id, item?, depth}` entries from:
- `O.Categories()`
- `O.Ids(cat)`, sorted by name with nil names last and an EM tiebreak
- `Inv.ItemSections()`

`ListModel.Status(id, cache)` caches `O.Status`, and the cache is wiped by `MarkDirty(true)`.

### 7.4 Slot checkboxes (`modules/SlotEnables.lua`): `NS.SlotEnables`

- **View only.** The data layer reads `outfit.items`, never the frames, which retires the readers at `OutfitterOutfits.lua:50,538,558`.
- **State** is `O.SlotState(sel, slot, cache)`:

  | State | Display |
  |---|---|
  | `off` | `SetChecked(false)`, unknown overlay hidden |
  | `on` | `SetChecked(true)` |
  | `unknown` | `SetChecked(false)` with the `cb.unknown` mark |

- **Click** (`Outfitter.lua:3333-3356`): with an item on the cursor (`CursorHasItem()`), drop it into the slot (`Compat.PickupInventoryItem(slotID)`); `OnReceiveDrag` does the same. Otherwise call `O.SetSlotEnabled(sel, slot, on)`, which leads to `OUTFIT_EDITED`.
- **`SlotEnables.UnknownSlots()`** returns `O.UnknownSlots(St.Selected())`, used by recording.
- **Stacking** (keeps `Outfitter.lua:1451-1456` and the CLAUDE.md gotcha):

  ```lua
  function SlotEnables.Restack()
    hostLevel = NS.SafeNumber(CharacterFrame:GetFrameLevel(), 0)
    frame:SetFrameStrata(CharacterFrame:GetFrameStrata())
    frame:SetFrameLevel(SlotEnables.HighestLevel(CharacterFrame, OutfitterRebornSheetHolder) + L.LEVEL.SLOT_ENABLES_OVER_HOST)
    SlotEnables.Relevel()               -- checkboxes container+1 (frame-layering-§3 :77)
  end
  function SlotEnables.MaybeRestack() if frame and frame:IsShown() and NS.SafeNumber(CharacterFrame:GetFrameLevel(), 0) ~= hostLevel then SlotEnables.Restack() end end
  ```

  - `HighestLevel(root, skip)` is iterative with a depth cap of 12. It skips the `skip` subtree and `IsForbidden()` frames, and reads through pcall + `SafeNumber`.
  - It runs only when CharacterFrame's level changes, so there are no steady-state allocations.
  - **The first Restack happens in `SlotEnables.Show(id)`** on `SELECTION_CHANGED`, not at build. The comment at `Outfitter.lua:3323-3325` is kept, re-worded: the sheet's level is only meaningful once the sheet is shown.
  - Restacking is allowed in combat (no secure frames).
  - Never use a fixed strata.
- Shown only while an outfit is selected. `CharacterLevelText` is left alone.

### 7.5 Outfit context menu (`modules/OutfitMenu.lua`): `NS.OutfitMenu`

`NS.OutfitMenu.Open(anchor, id)` calls `NS.OpenMenu(anchor, fill)`, the DR recorder (`DpsRace/core/WidgetsSetup.lua:36-104`). It uses `W.ContextMenu(owner, rows, {owner = addonName, cursor = true})` one level at a time, with `MenuUtil` as the fallback. The library hides the menu before a callback runs, which replaces the 0.1/0.2 s auto-hide. The stale-pool comment (OutfitterBar.lua:792-835) is retired.

| Section fn | Rows |
|---|---|
| `fillGeneral` | Title (the name); Rename → `NS.OutfitDialog.OpenRename(id)`. **Key binding** (submenu) None / Outfit 1–10 with the current holder ("Outfit 3 (Tank)") → `O.SetBinding`. **Title** (submenu) None + `NS.Compat.KnownTitles()` (plain names, sorted in Compat, §5.13), 30 per level with a "More" submenu → `O.SetFlag(id, "titleID", v)`, then `St.UpdateTitle(true)` 0.5 s later (the menu click is the hardware event). **Bank** (submenu) Deposit / Deposit unique / Withdraw / divider / Deposit others / Withdraw others, each `disabled = function() return not NS.Bank.IsOpen() end` with a reason tooltip. Unequip others (non-Complete only, `keepOpen`). Ignore comparisons (`keepOpen`). Delete → `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_DELETE`. |
| `fillRebuild` | Set to current items → `…_CONFIRM_SET_CURRENT`. "Rebuild for %s" (only with `stat`) → `…_CONFIRM_REBUILD`. Rebuild for… → RebuildDialog. |
| `fillAutomation` | **Automation: <trigger or None>** (submenu): No trigger, then one level per category. Picking writes `NS.SetByPath("outfit.trigger.id", tid, id)` and opens `NS.TriggerDialog.Open(id)` if the trigger has params. Trigger settings… → `NS.TriggerDialog.Open(id)`. "Turn trigger off" / "Disable outfit in combat" (`keepOpen`; disabled without a trigger) → `outfit.trigger.disabled` / `.combatDisabled` through `NS.SetByPath(…, id)`. Turning the trigger off also hides the outfit from the bar and dims its row (§3.3). |
| `fillBar` | Show in outfit bar (`keepOpen`) → `NS.SetByPath("outfit.bar.hide", not v, id)`. Change icon… → `IconChooser.Open(id, anchor)` (fixes the doubled "…"). |

Dropped: `EDIT_SCRIPT` (H1) and the unreachable title sentinel.

**No glyphs in labels.** A submenu row's arrow is drawn by `W.ContextMenu` (the `chevron-right` mark), so no label carries `▸` or any other character outside localization-§7's safe set. `tests/test_glyphs.lua` guards every display string (§9.3).

### 7.6 Dialogs and popups

**Shared chrome (`modules/UIDialogs.lua`).** `NS.Dialogs.New{ name, key, width, height, title }` builds a frame with:
- strata `L.STRATA.MODAL`, toplevel, clamped, movable by the title band
- `NS.ApplySkin` and `NS.MakeCloseButton(f, cancel)`
- an entry in `UISpecialFrames`
- `OnShow` → `NS.RaiseStaticPopups()`; `OnHide` → `NS.CloseMenu()`
- position saved on drag stop through `NS.Dialogs.SavePosition(key, …)` into `profile.windows.dialogs[key]`. The first open centres over the window, or over UIParent.

Controls: OK `S.Button(b,{primary=true})`, Cancel `S.Button`, inputs `S.EditBox(eb,{padInput=true})`, checks `S.Checkbox`, dropdowns `NS.MakeDropdown`.

`NS.Dialogs.CloseAll(reason)` runs on host hide, stand-down and reset-all. It does **not** run on the combat suspend (O30).

**`OutfitterRebornOutfitDialog`** (`Dialog_Outfit.lua`, `NS.OutfitDialog`, INV §7.9):
- `NS.OutfitDialog.OpenNew()` and `NS.OutfitDialog.OpenRename(id)`.
- **Name EditBox.** Enter means Done, and Done is disabled while the name is empty.
- **Category** dropdown: General, Activities, PvP, Questing, Entertainment, the player's class (female form when `UnitSex == 3`), Specialization.
- **Trigger** dropdown: `NS.Triggers.List(cat, classFile)`. A "…" suffix marks a trigger with params, and the tooltip is the description. Two menus are used because one flat menu of about 70 triggers does not fit on screen. Picking fills the name when it is empty or holds the previous auto-fill.
- **Build** radios: Empty / Current items (default) / Best for `<stat>`, the last with a stat dropdown.
- **Errors:** `L["That name is already used"]`, and `cNoItemsWithStatError` via `NS.Optimize.Trial`. The error line uses `of.dialog.error`.
- **Done:**
  1. `O.Create(name, items, { category })` (the build source picks the items or the optimizer)
  2. `St.Wear(id, { hw = true })`
  3. `NS.SetByPath("outfit.trigger.id", tid, id)`
  4. if the trigger has params, `NS.TriggerDialog.Open(id)` (INV §7.7)
- **Rename mode** shows the name field only, and is 140 px shorter.

**`OutfitterRebornTriggerDialog`** (`Dialog_Trigger.lua`, `NS.TriggerDialog`: `Open(id)`, `Close(reason)`; H1, H12, H13):
- **Title** `L["Automation for %s"]`. Category and Trigger dropdowns, plus "No trigger", and the description in muted text.
- **Body:** fields from `NS.Triggers.Get(tid).params`, or `L["There are no settings for this trigger."]`.
- **Below:** "Disable outfit in combat" and "Turn this trigger off".
- **Staged editing:**
  1. `Open(id)` builds a `draft`, filling a missing value from `param.default` (never the type default; fixes INV §9.8).
  2. Done runs `NS.Triggers.ValidateParam` per changed key. A failure shows in that field's error line, and nothing is written.
  3. On pass, it calls `NS.Schema.SetMany(entries, { instanceId = id })`, where each entry is `{ path = "trigger.<TID>.<key>" | "outfit.trigger.<flag>" | "outfit.trigger.id", value }`. This is all-or-nothing, and the announce routes to `O.Changed(id, "trigger")`, which leads to `Binder.Rebind`.
  4. Runtime state is in `triggerState`, so Done cannot wipe the Fishing restore.
- Cancel and the close button both discard the draft. Today the X saves; that is now fixed to match Escape.

**Field types** (`Dialog_TriggerFields.lua`). `FIELD_TYPES[type] = { height, build(parent), set(field, v), get(field) }`. Editors come from `NS.Pool.NewKeyed()`/`AcquireKeyed(pool, type, factory)` and are released on close (INV §9.8 "pooled per type"). The list covers all nine C20 types:

| Type | Editor |
|---|---|
| `bool` | `S.Checkbox` + label |
| `number` | numeric `S.EditBox` + suffix (min/max/step from the descriptor; one `suffix` field, so the case bug is gone) |
| `string` | `S.EditBox` 300×20 |
| `spelllist` | list editor (ScrollFrame + `S.ScrollBar`, 300×80; one entry per line, shown as names) + "Add last spell cast" (`CaptureLastCast`), "Add a buff…" (`CapturePlayerAura`) |
| `itemlist` | list editor + "Add item on cursor" (`CaptureCursorItem`), "Add equipped item…" (`CaptureSlotItem`) |
| `maplist` | list editor + "Add current zone" (`CaptureCurrentMap`) |
| `subzonelist` | list editor + "Add current subzone" (`CaptureCurrentSubzone`, under D3) |
| `unitlist` | list editor + "Add current target" (`CaptureTarget`) |
| `pet` | `NS.MakeDropdown` of owned pets + "Use summoned pet" (`CaptureSummonedPet`) |

The capture buttons are words, not arrows: `←` is outside localization-§7's safe set.

**Rebuild and progress** (Optimize survives, H15):
- `OutfitterRebornRebuildDialog` (`NS.RebuildDialog`): rows of stat dropdown + operator ("Highest" / "At most" / "At least"; `≤`/`≥` are outside the safe set) + value EditBox, add/remove buttons (rows from `NS.Pool`), and a "Complex" checkbox. `GetConfig()` returns the §5.14 `cfg`. `Update` is written fresh.
- `OutfitterRebornOptimizeProgress`: `L["Optimizing %s for %s"]`, a StatusBar, "High speed" (`job.highSpeed`), "Best score: %s", and Cancel → `NS.Optimize.Cancel(job)`. It is driven by `OPTIMIZE_*` messages.

**Popups** (`modules/Popups.lua`, `NS.Popups`: `ShowDelete(id)`, `ShowRebuild(id, cfg)`, `ShowSetCurrent(id)`, `ShowDeleteAll()` (refuses in combat and through `NS.StoodDownRefusal()`, §3.6), `ShowEMFull()`). Every popup has `timeout = 0, whileDead = true, hideOnEscape = true`, and the id travels in `data`:

| Key | Text | Accept |
|---|---|---|
| `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_DELETE` | `L["Delete the outfit \"%s\"?"]` | `O.Delete(data)` |
| `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_REBUILD` | `cConfirmRebuildMsg` | `NS.Optimize.Rebuild(data.id, data.cfg)` |
| `OUTFITTERREBORN_CONFIRM_SET_CURRENT` | `cConfirmSetCurrentMsg` | `O.SetToCurrent(data)` |
| `OUTFITTERREBORN_DELETE_ALL` | §3.6 | `O.DeleteAll()` |
| `OUTFITTERREBORN_EM_FULL` | `cServerFull` | — (raised by `EM.StoreOnServer`) |

`OUTFITTERREBORN_RESET_ALL` lives in `settings/OptionsSetup.lua`.

Dropped: `OUTFITTER_CANT_RELOADUI` and `OUTFITTER_CANT_SET_ICON`. The MC2 `StaticPopup_EscapePressed` taint leaves with the library.

### 7.7 Outfit bar (`modules/Bar.lua`, `modules/Bar_Buttons.lua`): `NS.Bar`

The bar is a HUD (skinning-§6) and a free-floating display: persisted position, Lock and Reset (BP §11). It stores no colours, so it owes no `FromSkin` rows; ARCHITECTURE records the HUD classification. The rows are those of §4.2: `bar.shown`, `bar.vertical`, `bar.showBackground`, `bar.combatAlpha`, `bar.position`, plus Master `scale`/`alpha`/`locked`/`visibility`.

**`Bar.Render(source)`** (Perf bucket `barRender`):
1. `source` is `Bar.LiveSource` or `NS.Preview.Source`. Both answer `Rows() → { {categoryID, outfits = {…}} }`. The live source skips outfits with `trigger.disabled` (§3.3) and `bar.hide`.
2. `ReleaseAll` per row, then exactly `#outfits` acquires (fixes the extra button).
3. Rows lay out 1×N, or N×1 when vertical. The pitch is 37 px with 32 px buttons.
4. Growth direction comes from `QUADRANT[savedPoint]`.
5. `bar:SetScale(scale)` and `bg:SetShown(bar.showBackground)`.

Rebuilds are coalesced with one 0 s AceTimer flag. They are triggered by `OUTFIT_WORN/REMOVED/ADDED/DELETED/EDITED/RENAMED`, `SETTINGS_CHANGED` for `bar.*`/Master rows, `PREVIEW_CHANGED` and `PROFILE_CHANGED`.

**Visibility and alpha:**
- shown = `(bar.shown or Preview.IsActive()) and visibility rule and not inPetBattle and not stood down`
- `PET_BATTLE_OPENING_START`/`PET_BATTLE_OVER` toggle `inPetBattle`
- in combat (`PLAYER_STATE_CHANGED "combat"`) alpha is `alpha × combatAlpha`, else `alpha`, with the 0.3 s / 0.5 s fades kept
- effective alpha 0 → `EnableMouse(false)` on the buttons (frame-layering-§4 :89; insecure, so legal in combat)

**Drag handle.** `W.DragHandle(bar, spec)` with:
- `label = L["Outfit bar"]`, `moveFrame = bar`, `owner = addonName`, `helpIcon = NS.Icon("help")`
- `canDrag = function() return not NS.GetSetting("locked") end`
- `onDragStop = NS.Bar.SavePosition` (the nearest corner `{point, x, y}` divided by the effective scale)
- `onRightClick = function() NS.OpenOptionsPanel("bar") end`
- `tooltip = { L["Drag to move. Right-click for bar settings."] }`
- `ignoreParentAlpha = true`, `level = L.LEVEL.HANDLE`

It is shown only while unlocked. The settings popup is replaced by the page (O32), and the undefined `FrameStopDragging` disappears.

`NS.Bar` API: `Refresh`, `ApplyScale`, `ApplyAlpha`, `SetLocked`, `SavePosition`, `ResetPosition`, `OutfitIcon(rec)`, `Suspend`, `Resume`.

**Buttons (`Bar_Buttons.lua`):**
- **`OutfitIcon(rec)`** follows the ladder: `rec.icon` → `NS.Triggers.Get(rec.trigger.id).icon` (or the stat icon) → a single item's icon → 132662.
- **Worn:** the `btn.worn` mark is shown.
- **Left:** wear, or remove for a worn non-Complete outfit, with `{ hw = true }`.
- **Right:** `NS.OutfitMenu.Open`.
- **Hover:** `NS.OutfitTooltip.Show(id, btn, anchor, true)` (`always`: the bar draws the tooltip for every outfit).
- **Drop** (`OnReceiveDrag`, or `OnClick` with a cursor; `GetCursorInfo()`):
  - With Alt: `item` → `C_Item.GetItemIconByID`, `spell` → `C_Spell.GetSpellTexture(spellID)` (fixes the removed `GetSpellTexture`), `macro` → `GetMacroInfo` icon. Each is written via `NS.SetByPath("outfit.icon", v, id)`.
  - Without Alt, an `item` creates `O.CreateFromItem(link)`, then wears it.
- **Placeholder** (`btn.outfit.placeholder`): every click and drop returns at once, and the tooltip is `L["Sample outfit (test mode). Nothing is equipped."]` (H17).
- No Masque (INV §7.6 Decide).

### 7.8 Icon chooser (`modules/IconChooser.lua`)

- `IconChooser.Open(id, opener)` / `Close(reason)`.
- Popup tier (FULLSCREEN_DIALOG, clamped). It closes on:
  - an outside `GLOBAL_MOUSE_DOWN`, not counting its own dropdown and search box
  - window or sheet close
  - Escape
  - stand-down
  - the opener hiding
- **Set dropdown:**
  - Recommended (wildcard, the outfit's item icons, the trigger icons)
  - Equipped and bags
  - Item icons, Ability icons (both via `Compat.MacroIcons(kind)`)
  - All

  The Spellbook set is retired.
- **Search:** `W.SearchBox(f, 160, { owner = addonName, hint = L["Filter by item name"] })`. It filters the named sets only, and is disabled with a tooltip for the fileID-only sets (O33).
- **Grid:** 6×5 buttons of 36 px from `NS.Pool`, `S.SquareIcon`, the current pick drawn as `S.ListRow(b,"selected")`, and an `S.ScrollBar` slider.
- The wildcard 134400 means "Outfitter chooses" and saves nil.
- OK/Enter → `NS.SetByPath("outfit.icon", v, id)`. Cancel discards.

### 7.9 Flyout quick slots (`modules/QuickSlots.lua`, `QuickSlots_Sort.lua`)

- Built in OnEnable. The bodies gate on `quickSlots.enabled` and the latch.
- `HookScript("PreClick"/"PostClick")` on each `Character<Slot>` button. PostClick (`OutfitterQuickSlots.lua:318-333`):
  - flyout open on this button → `EquipmentFlyoutPopoutButton_OnClick(popout)` when the popout holds `flyoutLocked` (Blizzard clears the lock and the arrow), else `EquipmentFlyout_Hide()`. A bare hide of a locked flyout would leave the lock set, and the next click on the slot would only unlock it.
  - else an item on the cursor or an empty slot → `EquipmentFlyoutPopoutButton_OnClick(button.popoutButton)`, so Blizzard sets its own `flyoutLocked`; a stale lock (a flyout closed some other way) is cleared through one more call first, so the open always opens, as the legacy forced open did
- **Filter:** `hooksecurefunc(PaperDollItemsFrame.flyoutSettings, "getItemsFunc", fn)` removes on-player locations in place.
- **Sort:** `hooksecurefunc(PaperDollItemsFrame.flyoutSettings, "postGetItemsFunc", function(btn, displayTable) local n = #displayTable; Sort.Items(displayTable, n); Sort.PlaceInBagsLast(displayTable, n) end)`. A post-hook receives the caller's `numItems`, **not** the original's return, and the original inserts `EQUIPMENTFLYOUT_PLACEINBAGS_LOCATION` at index 1 and returns `numItems + 1` (`OutfitterQuickSlots.lua:24-33`). The count is therefore read from the dense display table after the original has run. `test_quickslots` pins a fixture where the original inserts the place-in-bags entry.
- **`QuickSlots_Sort.lua`** is a verbatim port with every comment (`OutfitterQuickSlots.lua:85-314`):
  - track ceiling → TrackStringID → item level → name → index
  - untracked items banded into a track
  - a pcall'd `Compat.CanUpgradeItem` with the headroom fallback
  - links via `NS.Unsecret`; a secret or void location → nil
  - `Compat.FlyoutLocationData`, with the legacy unpack retired
- QuickSlots owns no frames. Conditional row D11 applies if an audit reads the hooks as writing into Blizzard tables.

### 7.10 Tooltips

**"Used by outfits" (`modules/Tooltips.lua`, INV §3.8).** One `TooltipDataProcessor.AddTooltipPostCall(Enum.TooltipDataType.Item, Tooltips.OnItem)`, registered once in OnEnable (H15).

`OnItem` returns early when any of these hold:
- stood down
- `tooltip.usedBy` off
- `tooltip` is not GameTooltip or a stock shopping tooltip (`ShoppingTooltip1/2`, the worn items beside a compare: the legacy compare path decorated them, Outfitter.lua:7885-7893; our own compare tooltips decorate themselves)
- the tooltip shows another unit's inventory item: `NS.Compat.TooltipInventoryUnit(tooltip)` is non-nil and not `"player"` (INV §3.8 "SetInventoryItem (player only)"; inspect and target gear never get the line, and an inventory tooltip whose unit cannot be read answers `"?"`, O41)
- the link from `TooltipUtil.GetDisplayedItem` is nil or secret

Otherwise:
- `Tooltips.LineFor(link)` is cached per link and wiped on `OUTFIT_*` and `INVENTORY_CHANGED`. It is built from `O.UsingItem(link)`, with names coloured by `O.Status` via `NS.SkinEscape`; banked is blue `of.outfit.banked` (fixes the red line).
- **Replacing the Equipment Sets line:**
  1. Build a pattern once from `ConvertFormatStringToSearchPattern(EQUIPMENT_SETS)`.
  2. Scan the left fontstrings with a pcall'd `find`, skipping secret text.
  3. On a match, `fs:SetText(line)`; otherwise `AddLine` (O34).
- Perf bucket `tooltipUsedBy`.
- `NS.Tooltips.Refresh()` wipes the line cache; the `tooltip.usedBy` row calls it (§4.2).

**Compare (`modules/Tooltips_Compare.lua`, `NS.Compare`):**
- `NS.Compare.Refresh()` hides all five frames; the `tooltip.compare` row calls it (§4.2).
- `Compat.HookCompare(Compare.OnShowCompare)`, a `hooksecurefunc` on `GameTooltip_ShowCompareItem`.
- **Gates:** the latch, `tooltip.compare`, `TooltipUtil.ShouldDoItemComparison()` with the COMPAREITEMS modifier, a plain link, armour only.
- **Shows:** up to `MAX = 5` lazy `OutfitterRebornCompareTooltip<N>`. Items come from outfits without `ignoreComparisons`, quality ≥ 2, sorted by level, with duplicates skipped. Line 1 is `"<outfit> | Outfitter"` in `text.muted`.
- **Placement** is beside `ShoppingTooltip2`, falling back left-to-right on secret geometry.
- The `LibTipHooker` re-dispatch is not needed (H15).
- All five hide via a `HookScript` on GameTooltip OnHide that touches only our frames, and on a released modifier via an OnUpdate `HookScript` on the first one (`Compare.OnTick`; the legacy per-tooltip OnUpdate, Outfitter.lua:8057-8064): the client does not call `GameTooltip_ShowCompareItem` when the modifier goes up.

**Outfit tooltip (`modules/OutfitTooltip.lua`).** `Show(id, owner, anchor, always)` draws the name, the trigger description, `L["Missing:"]` + links in `of.outfit.missing`, and `L["Banked:"]` in `of.outfit.banked`. The newbie-tip fallback follows the UberTooltips CVar. With `always` (the bar's buttons, the legacy `pShowEmptyTooltips`, OutfitterBar.lua:844) the full tooltip draws even with nothing missing or banked.

## 8. Test mode (H17; `modules/Preview.lua`, `defaults/PreviewOutfits.lua`)

```lua
Preview.Start(opts)
  -- 1 stood down -> silent return
  -- 2 InCombatLockdown() -> NS.Print(L["Test mode is not available in combat."]); return
  -- 3 Preview.active = true; NS.State.testMode = true; Preview.openedBar = not NS.GetSetting("bar.shown")
  -- 4 Preview.Source = { Rows = function() return NS.PreviewOutfits.Rows() end }
  -- 5 NS.SendBus(NS.MSG.PREVIEW_CHANGED, true) -> Bar.Render(Preview.Source); strip:Show(); bar body pushed by NS.TEST_STRIP_HEIGHT + 2
Preview.Stop(reason)   -- "switch" | "combat" | "reset" | "standdown" (silent, via Suspend)
  -- combat -> NS.SayTestModeEnded() (core/OutfitterReborn.lua, as DR core/DpsRace.lua:198); clear state; PREVIEW_CHANGED false -> Bar.Render(Bar.LiveSource); strip:Hide(); hide bar iff openedBar
Preview.Toggle(), Preview.IsActive(), Preview.Suspend(), Preview.Diagnose()
```

**Placeholders** (`defaults/PreviewOutfits.lua`). The file publishes `NS.PreviewOutfits = { rows = <the literal below>, Rows = function() … end }`; `Rows()` returns `rows` with `placeholder = true` and `id = "preview:<n>"` stamped on each record once:

```lua
{ { categoryID = "Complete",  outfits = { { name = L["Test Outfit 1"], icon = 132276 }, { name = L["Test Outfit 2"], icon = 1376041 },
                                          { name = L["Test Outfit 3"], icon = 136245 } } },
  { categoryID = "Accessory", outfits = { { name = L["Test Outfit 4"], icon = 132261 }, { name = L["Test Outfit 5"], icon = 134062 } } } }
```

- Each record carries `placeholder = true` and `id = "preview:<n>"`.
- The icons are the shipped default trigger icons, so the release Currency check covers them (preview-mode :64-77). The 1.1.0 ANALYSIS.md records them as "Refreshed".
- Five real-icon buttons satisfy preview-mode :5.
- The names follow H17 ("Test Outfit N"), replacing the UI slice's "Tank/PvP/…" names.

**Marker:**
- The bar's `W.TestStrip`, amber, with text `L["Test mode: sample outfits. Nothing is equipped or saved"]`.
- The strip's right-click menu ends the mode (`onEnd = Preview.Stop`).
- It sits above the top row in band MARKER and moves with the bar (preview-mode :11-16).
- One marker only (:18). The window keeps real data and has no marker.

**What ends it:** the General "Test mode" row (session, default false), the launcher's entry (greyed while disabled, :27), `/of test`, `PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED`, Reset all, and stand-down. Re-locking the bar does **not** end it (:29).

**No SV writes.** Placeholders never reach `NS.Outfits` or `NS.Stack`, and `O.Get("preview:n")` is nil (C28). Every write path in `Bar_Buttons` checks `placeholder` first. Dragging the real bar during test mode still saves `bar.position`.

**Simulator declined** (H18, Q4, Q5). It is recorded in ARCHITECTURE and scope.md. There is no launcher sim row.

## 9. Tests

### 9.1 Principles

1. **The kit replaces the harness completely** (testing-§1). `tests/_kit/` is a byte-identical copy of the LibKa0s v1.85.0 testkit (rev 50) and is never edited (BP §15.2). Nothing under `tests/` re-implements the registry, the assertions, the loader or the base mock.
2. **Characterize the old code first, then rewrite** (testing-§13, C24). The old addon cannot load under the kit loader, so its behaviour is **captured into data fixtures**:
   - `tools/legacy_capture.lua` runs the old harness against a read-only worktree of `7ea3dc3` and writes `tests/fixtures/legacy/*.lua`.
   - The new suites assert against those fixtures.
3. **Keep pins assert the legacy value; Fix pins assert the corrected value.** Each Fix case carries `-- legacy: <value> (INV §19 <id>); fixed:` and a `-- red under:` mutation note (testing-§12).
4. **Pure logic first, headless.** The planners (`NS.Changes.Build`, `NS.ItemInfo.ParseLink`, `St.Compile`, `ListModel.Build`, `NS.Optimize` with injected `rng`, `Triggers.Finalize`, `States_Zone.CurrentIds`) take plain tables and return plain tables.
5. **Spine files are orchestrator-owned** (§12): the TOC, `tests/run.lua` SUITES, `.luacheckrc`, `core/Bus.lua`, `core/LifecycleSetup.lua` and the three `locales/enUS*.lua`. P4.0 pre-declares them. A task edits only its own pre-declared line in `SUITES` and its own marker block in each enUS file (§12).
6. **Gates enumerate from the tree, never from a hand list:**
   - zone lockstep derived from `NS.Zones.rows`
   - the `loadstring`/removed-API scans walk `git ls-files`
   - the budget walks every authored `.lua`

### 9.2 Layout

```
tests/
  _kit/                    LibKa0s v1.85.0 testkit, rev 50; never edited
  run.lua            ~270  DR shape (BP §15.3)
  wow_mock.lua       ~350  thin extender over _kit/mock_base + mock_bindings + mock_layers (+ mock_paint)
  mock_gear.lua      ~700  bags/bank/equipped/items/equip recorder/C_EquipmentSet
  mock_world.lua     ~600  instance+map, auras (+restriction), forms, mount/swim/fall, units, pets, quests, CVars, frames
  fixtures/
    items.lua        ~500  hand-authored item DB (links, bonus ids, unique-equip, 2H/1H, BoE, warbound, upgrade tracks)
    inventories.lua  ~400  named scenarios ("full_bags", "tg_warrior", "unique_rings", "bank_open", …)
    legacy/                GENERATED by tools/legacy_capture.lua from 7ea3dc3; never hand-edited; each file < 1000 lines
      README.md            commit, generator command, regeneration rule
      zones.lua presets.lua states.lua slots.lua stack.lua matching.lua plans.lua quickslots.lua
      commands.lua bindings.lua locale_keys.lua credits.lua
  skin_surfaces.lua  ~220  Kit.skinSwitch surfaces (§9.5)
  prose_waivers.lua   ~40
  perf.lua           ~300  offline perf scenarios, outside the gate (testing-§7)
  test_*.lua               ≤ 700 each (§9.3)
```

**`tests/run.lua` specifics:**
- `Loader.addonName = "OutfitterReborn"` and `ADDON_FILES = Loader.tocFiles(root .. "/OutfitterReborn.toc")`.
- SV reset: `_G.OutfitterRebornDB, _G.OutfitterRebornPerfDB = nil, nil`.
- The kit floor asserts the 16 leased majors.
- `Kit.diagnostics.brand = "Ka0s Outfitter Reborn"`.
- `_G.OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST = Kit.expose{ …, gear = MockGear, world = MockWorld, fixtures = Fixtures, legacy = Legacy }`. `Legacy` lazy-`dofile`s `fixtures/legacy/<name>.lua`.
- `Kit.layoutCap = { hub = "docs/ARCHITECTURE.md", exempt = {} }`.
- Kit-dir suites are declared as `{ name = …, dir = "tests/_kit/" }` and are **always live**: the kit raises a hard error on a `pending` entry whose file exists (LK `testkit/framework.lua:384-388`), and every kit suite's file exists from C4. The kit suites that cannot pass yet are handled in §11 (self-skip or a temporary decline row).
- `pending = "<task>"` is only for **local** suites whose `tests/test_*.lua` file does not exist yet (LK `testkit/inventory.lua:331-335`, "declared-and-deliberately-absent"). The task that writes the file flips its own line in the same commit (§12).
- The helper files (`mock_*`, `fixtures/`, `skin_surfaces`, `prose_waivers`, `perf.lua`) are listed in `docs/module-map.md` `## Not shipped`. The kit inventory scans only the `test_*.lua` basenames (`_kit/inventory.lua:305`).

**Mock fidelity** (testing-§1 rules):

`mock_gear`:
- The link and item-info calls answer from `fixtures/items.lua`. A `secret = true` entry is wrapped in `Kit.secret`.
- The pickup/equip calls move items for real and record into `M.__equipCalls`.
- Bag-full and specialty bags are modelled.
- `C_EquipmentSet` keeps real set tables, and `M.__emFlake = true` answers 0 sets (Q8).

`mock_world`:
- `GetInstanceInfo` gives all 9 fields.
- `C_RestrictedActions.IsAddOnRestrictionActive` reads `M.__restricted`.
- `Enum.AddOnRestrictionType` has Combat = 0 (reproduces the nil hole, INV §8.1).
- Aura getters raise while restricted.
- Unit reads are real or `Kit.secret(n)`.
- `C_PetJournal.SummonPetByGUID` is hookable.
- `SecureCmdOptionParse` evaluates `[combat]`, `[mod:shift]` and `;`, and returns nil when nothing matches.
- Frames: `PaperDollFrame`/`CharacterFrame` with `GetChildren`, levels and strata; `Character<Slot>` buttons; `PaperDollItemsFrame.flyoutSettings`; `TooltipDataProcessor`; `UISpecialFrames`; a toggleable `InCombatLockdown`; `GetCursorInfo`; `StaticPopup_Show`; GameTooltip `TextLeftN` with `GetTextColor`.
- APIs: `C_Map`, `C_UnitAuras.GetAuraDataByIndex`, `GetShapeshiftFormInfo`, `C_TradeSkillUI.GetTradeSkillDisplayName/GetBaseProfessionInfo` and `C_QuestLog.IsOnQuest/IsComplete`.

### 9.3 Suite catalogue

Every local suite opens with `local T = _G.OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST`.

**Standard-required and structural:**

| Suite | Pins | Green at | ≈Lines |
|---|---|---|---|
| `test_skeleton` | `GetAddon("OutfitterReborn") == NS`; `NS.Print == NS.Util.print` with `[OR]`; both slash aliases reach `OnSlash`; DB defaults equal `NS.defaults`; wire regex `^Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_%u%a+$` over `NS.MSG`; icons resolve; every authored file opens with `local addonName, NS = ...` and a `-- path — purpose` line 2 (the §6.3 sample shows it); Lua 5.1 only | C5, C7, final P4.27 | 300 |
| `test_toc` | header order; Title; `## Version: 1.1.0` = `NS.FALLBACK_VERSION`; IconTexture path exists; SV names; `X-Curse-Project-ID: 1698220`; no `X-ReloadTag`/`AddonCompartmentFunc`/`RequiredDeps`/`Dependencies: LibKa0s` | C5 | 150 |
| `test_loadorder` | runner list = TOC list; every path exists; no `libs/` in the addon list; folders libs → locales → core → defaults → modules → settings; `# LOAD-BEARING` lines in place; `Bindings.xml` not in the TOC; no `Deprecated.lua`/`Libraries\`/root `Compat.lua`; domain order ItemData → Zones → TriggerData → Triggers and Triggers_Params → Families → Cond* → Triggers → Binder; `core/Compat.lua` holds no file-scope `NS.Secrets` capture (it loads before `core/Secrets.lua`), with a mutation self-test; the compat family is exactly `core/Compat.lua` (compat.md :7) | C5, extended P4.0 | 250 |

Outfitter cases: an outfit with an active `IN_DUNGEON` trigger seeded before stand-down, after which `__registrations()` holds none of its events and no AceTimer is live; Equip `depth == 0`, `needsUpdate == false` and SFX restored after stand-down and resume (with an open Batch and a pending update seeded); `PLAYER_EQUIPMENT_CHANGED` while down leaves `db.char` unchanged; the tooltip/compare/pet/quick-slot/PaperDoll hook bodies no-op; the driver frame has no OnUpdate; bar, window, checkboxes and `OutfitterRebornSheetButton` hidden, and a `PaperDollFrame` OnShow while down leaves the button hidden; an AutoOn/AutoOff press while down leaves `auto.enabled` unchanged (step 8); the LoadPass timer and the Pawn/WW `ADDON_LOADED` watch are cancelled; resume rebuilds (an outfit added while down binds on resume) | C14, full P4.27 | 600 |

### 9.4 Legacy capture and retiring the old harness

**Capture (P3 C2).**
1. `git worktree add ../Outfitter-legacy 7ea3dc3`.
2. `lua tools/legacy_capture.lua ../Outfitter-legacy tests/fixtures/legacy`, run twice; the diff between the two runs must be empty.
3. Any pin the old headless mock cannot reach (unique ranks, quick-slot sort, simple optimizer picks) is hand-authored with `source = "hand: <file:line>"`. The C2 commit message lists them (O36).
4. The worktree is removed at P14.

**Retirement (P3 C5).** These are deleted:
- `tests/framework.lua`, `loader.lua`, `run-all.sh`, `wow_mock.lua` (replaced) and `client_events.lua` (re-homed into `mock_world`/`mock_gear`)
- the 14 old suites: harness, syntax, eol, toc, xml, load, compat, locale, deprecated, zones, scripts, secrets, outfits, commands

The mapping of the 144 old cases is reproduced in `docs/testing.md` `## Retired harness` (testing-§13):

| Old suite (cases) | New home | Dropped, and why |
|---|---|---|
| harness (8) | kit `assertSuiteInventory`; `test_packaging`; `test_toc` | none |
| syntax (3) | kit loader; luacheck `std = lua51`; `test_skeleton` 5.2 scan | the BOM list (MC2DebugLib deleted, H15) |
| eol (3) | kit `test_eol` (CRLF) | per-file LF/CRLF pins (H27 policy change) |
| toc (6) | `test_loadorder`, `test_toc` | "Deprecated loads after Outfitter" → "no Deprecated.lua" |
| xml (6) | `test_keybinds`, `test_slot_enables` (2) | XML handlers and templates: no XML ships but Bindings.xml |
| load (18) | `test_skeleton`, `test_api` (inverted leak check), `test_toc`, `test_launcher`, `test_locale`, `test_credits` | "broker identity is still Outfitter" (superseded by §1.6 #6) and "display name is Outfitter Reborn" (Title now "Ka0s Outfitter Reborn") |
| compat (5) | `test_secrets` | none |
| zones (16) | `test_zones`, all kept | none |

### 9.5 Skin-switch surfaces (`tests/skin_surfaces.lua`)

Each surface has a builder:
- sheet button
- window
- a row of each of the three kinds
- a slot checkbox in each of the three states
- the footer buttons
- outfit, trigger, rebuild and progress dialogs
- a trigger field of each of the nine types
- the icon chooser
- the bar and a bar button
- the drag handle
- the test strip
- a compare tooltip (`S.Tooltip`, §7.1)

`docs/smoke-tests.md` lists the same surfaces, plus the in-client checks: Escape ordering, combat open/close, the sheet stays movable by other addons, the flyout, and the tooltip replacement.

---

## 10. Documented deviations register

ARCHITECTURE `## Documented deviations` uses the standard's **exact** row shape (documentation-§3 :191-195): `| Rule | What differs | Why | Decided | Re-check trigger |`, with **Rule** a `filename-§N` reference. The table below adds one leading **`id` column for this design only** (D1…D11 are cross-referenced throughout). C16 writes the five standard columns and drops it; where an id helps a reader, it goes at the start of the Why cell ("D2. Owner ruling R5 …").

- **Written in C16, unconditionally:** D1, D2, D3, D4, D6, D8, D10. The code under D4, D8 and D10 ships from P4 on, so the rows exist from the start (documentation-§3: "a deviation not in the register is not ratified").
- **Conditional:** D7 and D11. They are written only if the client check (D7) or the audit reading (D11) goes the way the row describes. No code that differs from the standard as this design reads it ships under either, so nothing is unratified in the meantime. They are tracked as open issues (O-items in §14), **not** parked in `known-limitations.md`.
- **Dropped:** D5 and D9. Both recorded compliance, which the register must not hold (documentation-§3 :166, :337). D5's content (the persisted stack and recents) is named non-setting state in ARCHITECTURE `## Settings Schema` (§3.4). D9's is the H21 code comment in `settings/OptionsSetup.lua` plus one line of ARCHITECTURE prose. The ids are not reused.
- **Temporary kit declines** (§11): rows for `test_prose` and `test_diagnostics_contract`, written in C5 and removed by the commit that lands each seam. They are listed in §11, not here.

The cap census reads "Nothing is over the cap today."

## 11. P3 scaffold commit sequence

**Gates:**
- Before C5, the gate is the legacy `./tests/run-all.sh`. It only stays meaningful because **C2 amends the legacy `Ctx.luaFiles`** (`tests/run.lua:39-52`) to skip `^libs/` and `^tools/` as well as `^tests/` and `^Libraries/`, and says so in its message. Without that, `test_toc`'s orphan check (`tests/test_toc.lua:27-36`), `test_secrets`' seam scan (`tests/test_secrets.lua:105-140`, which matches `libs/LibKa0s/Perf.lua`), `test_syntax` and `test_deprecated` all walk the new `tools/` and `libs/` files and fail C2–C4.
- From C5 on, it is `bash tests/_kit/run-automated-tests.sh --suite lint --suite tests --no-bundle`: luacheck 0/0 and `lua tests/run.lua` with 0 failures.
- **Every kit suite is live in `SUITES` from C5** (`{ name, dir = "tests/_kit/" }`, never `pending`: the kit errors on a `pending` entry whose file exists, LK `testkit/framework.lua:384-388`, and leaving one out is a kit hole):
  - `test_skin_values`/`test_skin_switch` self-skip while `Kit.skinValues`/`Kit.skinSwitch` are unset (LK `testkit/test_skin_switch.lua:23-42`); C8 sets them.
  - `test_diagnostics_contract` self-skips while `Kit.diagnostics` is unset (LK `testkit/test_diagnostics_contract.lua:42`); `run.lua` sets `Kit.diagnostics` in C12, not C5.
  - `test_prose` cannot pass until C16 rewrites the docs. C5's minimal ARCHITECTURE carries a temporary `## Documented deviations` row declining it (`| localization-§5 | tests/_kit/test_prose declined until the doc set is rewritten in US English | … | 2026-11-03 | C16 lands |`). The kit honours it as a declared skip only because the Rule cell names the gate's rule and the row names the kit's own path (`testkit/inventory.lua:218-226` `KIT_GATE_RULE`, `:282-300` `declineFor`). **C16 deletes the row** in the commit that makes the suite pass.
  - If any other kit suite cannot pass at the commit that makes it live, it gets the same kind of temporary row, removed by its landing commit.

**Pre-condition:** a clean tree. The pending plan edit and this design doc are committed first as `P2: Design for the Ka0s conversion`.

## 12. P4 sub-ledger

**Conventions for every task:**
- **Commit gate:** `bash tests/_kit/run-automated-tests.sh --suite lint --suite tests --no-bundle` is green.
- **Test first:** each task writes its suites red, then turns them green. Negative assertions are mutation-checked and carry `-- red under:`.
- **Complexity:** `--suite complexity --no-bundle` is advisory. Any CCN > 15 a task introduces is fixed in that task.
- **Commit subject** starts with the task id.
- **Files:** every file < 1000 lines (`test_file_budget`).
  - `core/Compat.lua` stood at 954 lines after P4.2, and at 987 after the group C merge. A task whose new accessor would take the family past 1000 first moves one cohesive topic into a `core/Compat<Topic>.lua` (compat.md: PascalCase, no underscore, named in the root's header, listed right after `core/Compat.lua` in the TOC) in its own commit, and hands back the TOC line and the `.luacheckrc` stanza.
- **Spine files** are owned by the orchestrator: `OutfitterReborn.toc`, `tests/run.lua`, `.luacheckrc`, `core/Bus.lua`, `core/LifecycleSetup.lua`, `locales/enUS*.lua`.
  - P4.0 pre-declares everything in them: every TOC line, **one `SUITES` line per suite** (`{ name = "test_foo", pending = "P4.x" }`), every `.luacheckrc` per-file stanza of §1.5, the `MODULES` list, and **one `-- P4.x keys` marker block per task** in each enUS file, each block separated from its neighbours by an unchanged line.
  - A task may edit, in a spine file, **only its own lines**: it flips its own `SUITES` line(s) (drops `pending`) in the same commit that creates the suite file, and appends its enUS keys inside its own marker block. That is what keeps each task's own commit green: the kit errors on a `pending` entry whose file exists (LK `testkit/framework.lua:384-388`), and `test_locale` fails on a key read but not defined.
  - Any other spine edit (a lint global P4.0 missed, a TOC note) is handed back, and the orchestrator applies it in the wave's merge commit.
  - At the wave merge, the one-line and marker-block edits from different tasks touch disjoint lines and merge cleanly. A key that two tasks both added fails `test_locale`'s cross-file duplicate gate (§4.6), and the orchestrator keeps one copy in the merge commit.
- **Isolation:** parallel tasks run in separate worktrees. No two tasks in one parallel group share an owned file; the spine-line rule above is the one sanctioned overlap.
- **Calls into a module that has not landed (the fake rule).** P4.0's stubs publish no NS table, and production code calls other modules at call time with **no** nil guards, because by P4.27 every module exists and `test_surface_parity`/`test_disabled` exercise the whole graph. A suite whose module calls a module owned by a later group **or by a parallel task in the same group** installs a recorded fake for it for the length of the case (`T.fake(NS, "Stack", { Remove = rec("Remove") })`, restored after; a 20-line helper in `tests/wow_mock.lua`, P4.1) and asserts the calls it expects. The owning task's suite pins the real behaviour. This covers, among others: `O.Delete`/`LoadPass` → Stack, TriggerBinder, EM (P4.6); `E.Update` → Stack, EM (P4.8); the zone producer → Equip, Triggers, Stack (P4.14); OutfitTooltip → Triggers (P4.17); `EM.Sync` → TriggerBinder (P4.10 ∥ P4.15); `Opt.Rebuild` → EM (P4.12 ∥ P4.10); Dock/Window → Dialogs, IconChooser, OutfitMenu (P4.19); Bar → Preview, OutfitMenu (P4.20); `OutfitStack_Watch` → Dock (P4.9); the `deleteall` verb → `NS.Popups` (P4.24 ∥ P4.21). P4.27 adds no fakes: its suites run on the real modules.

**Size:** 37 tasks. A planned ceiling of about 24 000–26 700 addon Lua lines (§1.1) and about 15 000 test lines across about 60 suites plus the mocks and fixtures.

---

## 13. P5 gate

1. **No pending suites.** `grep -n 'pending *=' tests/run.lua` shows only `test_refactor_pins`, or nothing after step 4.
2. **Full battery with a bundle.** `bash tests/_kit/run-automated-tests.sh` writes `docs/automated-tests/<YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS>/` and requires:
   - lint 0/0
   - tests 0 failures
   - perf recorded (amber allowed; never a skip, because `tests/perf.lua` exists)
   - complexity `warnings == 0`, `blindFiles == 0`, `crashedFiles == 0` (automated-tests-§3)
3. **Size.** `test_layout_cap` and `test_file_budget` are green. The census reads "Nothing is over the cap today", and the 1000–1500 band is empty (H19).
4. **CCN breaches.** For each function above 15:
   - write green-before pins in `test_refactor_pins.lua`
   - decompose it with a sanctioned shape
   - re-run from step 2

   Never split for length alone, and never re-tune `-L`.
5. **Parallel parity.** `lua tests/run.lua -j auto` agrees with the serial run case for case (testing-§14). Record the wall and CPU times.
6. **Vendor sync.** `test_vendor_sync` is green with `../LibKa0s` present (not the skip arm), and `git ls-files -s tests/_kit/run-automated-tests.sh` shows `100755`.
7. **Line endings.** Kit `test_eol` is green, and `git add --renormalize . && git status --porcelain` stages nothing.
8. **Tree greps** are empty outside `libs/`, `tests/_kit/`, `tools/`, `tests/fixtures/legacy/` and `docs/`:
   - `loadstring|setfenv|RunScript`
   - `gOutfitter_`
   - `Libraries[/\\]`
   - `OutfitterAPI`
   - `Interface\\AddOns\\Outfitter\\`
   - `SetFrameStrata%("`
9. **Inventory.** `docs/test-cases.md` and the README badge are regenerated, and the count equals the `## Totals` Total.
10. **ANALYSIS.md** is written for the bundle, with `## Preview and simulation data`: test mode only, placeholder icons "Refreshed", sim declined (H18).
11. **Unverified list.** `docs/smoke-tests.md` lists everything headless can't reach:
    - real equips and the unique-equip error path
    - docked layout, anchoring and strata against real CharacterFrame children
    - the combat hide/restore of the window, and refusals of secure rows and bindings
    - Escape ordering
    - CJK and Cyrillic in skin faces
    - Pawn/WW live
    - secret-value windows in an encounter
    - every O-item marked "client check" in §14
12. **Commit** `P5: Green gate — …`, and set the plan ledger row P5 to `done` with the bundle path.

### Contradictions resolved (summary)

See §0.3 for the full table (C1–C40). The principal ones:
- **Names:** SV `OutfitterRebornDB`; wire prefix `Ka0s_OutfitterReborn_`; test global `OUTFITTERREBORN_TEST`; `OutfitterReborn*` binding, frame and popup names; `outfitterreborn.logo.*`.
- **Settings and schema:** 3 settings pages, not 5; the data-slice schema paths everywhere (`auto.enabled`, `equip.muteSounds`, `bar.shown`, Master scale/alpha/locked).
- **Messages and store:** per-kind outfit messages instead of `OUTFITS_CHANGED`, and `OUTFIT_REMOVED` instead of `UNWORN`; the store shape (`char.bindings` map, a stack array with layer tags, runtime `didEquip`).
- **Triggers:** the trigger record with `triggerParams`/`triggerState` and instance paths `outfit.*`/`trigger.<TID>.*` through Schema v3 `instanceId`; new SCREAMING_SNAKE trigger ids; nine param types; zones as one table.
- **Engine and UI:** the engine module names (`NS.Stack`, `NS.Equip.Batch`, `NS.PlayerState`) adopted by triggers and ui; `UISpecialFrames` over the Q2 default.
- **Tests:** legacy behaviour captured into fixtures rather than pinned in the old harness.
- **Media and locales:** both kept textures dropped in favour of the logo and the catalog `help` icon; three enUS topic files with orchestrator-merged keys.
